# Acceptance Criteria (Given / When / Then)

This document describes the application's behavior as **acceptance criteria** in a concise Given/When/Then form. It is the single source of truth for "how the app should behave" and should be updated whenever features or behavior change.

Criteria are grouped by **feature** (e.g. AC 1.10 Bookmarking collections). Each feature is split into **API** (HTTP, status codes, payloads, authorization) and **UI** (labels, screens, user-visible behavior) so backend and frontend concerns stay separate while related behavior stays together. Sub-items are numbered **AC section.subsection.id** and always state the **Actor** (see below).

## Common API conventions

Use these global contracts unless a specific AC explicitly defines a different behavior.

- **Auth and status codes**
  - **401 Unauthorized**: Request requires authentication and the caller is anonymous or token is missing/invalid (e.g. owner-only or user-specific endpoints).
  - **403 Forbidden**: Caller is authenticated but does not have permission to perform the operation on a resource whose existence is not hidden (e.g. admin-only maintenance endpoints).
  - **404 Not Found**: Resource does not exist **or** its existence must not be revealed to this caller (e.g. private collections/items for non-owners).
  - **400 Bad Request**: Validation or contract errors (missing/invalid parameters, malformed body, business-rule violations that are not authorization).
- **Pagination shape**
  - Paged list endpoints return an object with at least: `items` (array), `totalCount` (number), and page metadata. The exact page metadata fields are endpoint-specific today (for example `page` or `pageNumber`, plus `pageSize` and sometimes `totalPages` / `hasNext` / `hasPrevious`).
- **Date/time format**
  - All timestamps in APIs are ISO 8601 UTC strings (e.g. `2026-03-15T14:30:00Z`). Clients SHOULD treat them as UTC and convert to local time zones for display.
- **ProblemDetails / error shape**
  - Most domain and authorization errors use RFC 7807-style **ProblemDetails** with fields such as `type`, `title`, `status`, and `detail`.
  - Unexpected handler failures return the operation's fixed generic `detail` with the unchanged error code and status. `detail` never contains exception text (including AddItemsBulk per-item `ItemErrors` in the 200 body); exceptions are logged server-side (#281).
  - Some validation failures currently return framework-native validation payloads (for example FluentValidation error arrays) instead of a uniform `ProblemDetails.errors` shape. Feature-specific ACs should call out stricter requirements where they matter.
- **Naming conventions**
  - JSON fields use **camelCase** (e.g. `isPublic`, `createdBy`, `pageSize`). Database columns and C# properties may use **PascalCase**, but the wire format follows the JSON naming.
- **Idempotent behavior**
  - **GET**, **HEAD**, and **OPTIONS** are side-effect free.
  - Some **DELETE** endpoints are idempotent, but this is not yet a universal contract. Unless a feature-specific AC says otherwise, repeated delete of a missing resource may return `404 Not Found`.
  - **POST/PUT/PATCH** for "toggle" or "upsert" operations (e.g. bookmarking, ratings, user settings) are idempotent per `(user, resource)` pair: repeating the same request leaves state unchanged and returns success.
- **Null vs empty collections**
  - Collection-valued fields and list endpoints return **empty arrays** (`[]`) when there are no items, not `null`. Scalars may be `null` when meaningfully "missing" (e.g. optional description).

---

## Global invariants

These invariants apply everywhere unless explicitly overridden by a feature-specific AC.

### Collections

- **Invariant C1 – Active collection uniqueness**
  - An authenticated user who has at least one collection always has **exactly one active collection** at any time. APIs and UI that depend on an active collection must not allow a state with multiple active collections. (See AC 1.2.1–1.2.9.)
- **Invariant C2 – Collection ownership cardinality**
  - A user may own zero or more collections. The system must support the state where a user has no collections yet (e.g. just signed up) as well as multiple collections. (See AC 1.1 and AC 1.2.2.)
- **Invariant C3 – Immutable collection ownership**
  - Collection ownership (`CreatedBy`) is immutable after creation. No API or UI changes the owner of an existing collection; transfer of ownership is not supported.
- **Invariant C4 – Collection deletion cleanup**
  - Deleting a collection removes that collection and all relations that reference it (collection-items, bookmarks, shares and every user's test-date plan for it, AC 3.23.4). No dangling collection-item, bookmark or test-date plan records remain that reference a deleted collection. (See AC 1.8.1.)
- **Invariant C5 – Collection write access (#289)**
  - Collection write endpoints are owner-only, and admins are treated as non-owners (no admin exception): `PUT` and `DELETE /collections/{id}`, `POST /collections/{id}/items`, `POST /collections/{id}/items/bulk`, `DELETE /collections/{id}/items/{itemId}`, plus the owner-only read `GET /collections/{id}/bookmarks`. Order of checks: 401 (no valid token, before the body is bound), then request-body validation (400), then the collection is loaded (invalid id format on `PUT`/`DELETE /collections/{id}` is 400; unknown id is 404), then ownership, and only after that any item lookup, membership check, cascade delete or field change. A non-owner who cannot see the collection (private) gets the same empty-body 404 as a missing id; a non-owner who can see it (public) gets 403 ProblemDetails (`title` "Forbidden", `detail` stating the rule). A non-owner call never changes any data.
- **Invariant V1 – Private collection access**
  - A collection with `IsPublic = false` is accessible **only to its owner**; non-owners (authenticated, admin or anonymous) receive 404 for direct access by ID and do not see it in Discover. (See AC 1.4.2–1.4.3.) This also holds for the write and owner-only endpoints listed in Invariant C5 and for `POST /collections/{id}/bookmark`: a non-owner, including an admin, gets the same 404 as a missing id before any side effect (#289). The same applies to `GET /collections/{id}/rating` (#298) and to `GET /collections/bookmarks`, which omits bookmarked collections the caller can no longer access (#298).
- **Invariant V2 – Public collection access by ID**
  - A collection with `IsPublic = true` ("anyone with the link") is accessible to **anyone** (authenticated or anonymous) via its ID, subject to standard error handling when the collection does not exist. Direct access needs only `IsPublic`; `IsDiscoverable` does not matter. (See AC 1.4.1 and AC 1.9.6.)
- **Invariant V3 – Discover visibility for collections**
  - Only collections with `IsPublic = true` **and** `IsDiscoverable = true` appear in Discover or any other search/browse endpoint. Link-only collections (`IsPublic = true`, `IsDiscoverable = false`) and private collections never appear there. `IsDiscoverable` is always false while `IsPublic` is false (setting `isPublic = false` turns it off). Collections created before #201 kept their listing: the migration set `IsDiscoverable = IsPublic`; repo-managed public collections are always discoverable. (See AC 1.7.1 and AC 1.9.1–1.9.3.)
- **Invariant V4 – Collection-scoped sharing boundary**
  - Making a collection public (`IsPublic = true`) does **not** change the `IsPrivate` flag of any items in that collection. Collections are an explicit sharing boundary: private items may be visible within a shared collection context but remain private and non-discoverable outside that collection.

### Items

- **Invariant I1 – Single item owner**
  - Every item has exactly one owner (`CreatedBy`); ownership of an item cannot be shared or transferred after creation.
- **Invariant I2 – Private items and discovery**
  - Private items (`IsPrivate = true`) are never discoverable through category browsing or search for non-owners. Only the owner can see their own private items in category/keyword scopes; anonymous and authenticated non-owners never see other users’ private items. (See Item visibility in Terms / dictionary and AC 3.3.2, AC 3.5.1.)
- **Invariant I3 – Collection-scoped visibility for items**
  - Items included in a collection that a user can access may be visible within that **collection context** even if they are private, according to collection-scoped visibility rules. This does not make those items globally public or discoverable outside that collection. (See AC 1.5.1 and **Collection-scoped visibility** in Terms / dictionary.) Collection-scoped visibility never overrides picture review: an item whose uploaded pictures are not Approved is shown in a collection only to its creator (Invariant I7).
- **Invariant I4 – Removing items from collections**
  - Removing an item from a collection immediately revokes collection-scoped access to that item for users who only had access via that collection. Subsequent calls to `GET /items?collectionId={id}` must not include the removed item.
- **Invariant I5 – Direct item access**
  - Direct item APIs (e.g. `GET /items/{id}`) follow a strict rule: the owner can always access their own item by ID; an admin may read any item by ID (AC 2.3.6; admin moderation and edit flows rely on this, and the exception does not extend to lists or discovery); anyone else (authenticated or anonymous) can access an item by ID only if the item is public (`IsPrivate = false`). Private items that are only visible via a collection remain inaccessible by direct item ID for non-owners; they receive exactly the same 404 as a non-existent id. (See AC 2.3.1–2.3.6.) A public item whose uploaded pictures are not Approved (Invariant I7) follows the same rule as a private one: only the owner and admins can read it by ID, and everyone else receives the same 404 as a missing id.
- **Invariant I6 – Item write access (#286)**
  - `PUT /items/{id}`, `DELETE /items/{id}` and `PUT /items/{id}/category` are owner-or-admin only. The owner is the authenticated user whose resolved id equals the item's `CreatedBy`; an unresolved user id is never an owner. Anonymous callers get 401. Any other authenticated caller gets 404 (identical to a missing id) if the item is private, or 403 if it is public. The check runs after the item is loaded and before any category, keyword or other write. (See AC 2.1.10–2.1.14.) A non-owner, non-admin caller on a public item whose uploaded pictures are not Approved gets 404 instead of 403, because the item is not visible to them (Invariant I7, AC 3.25.7).
- **Invariant I7 – Uploaded item pictures are gated by review (#400)**
  - An item has a picture review state exactly when one of its picture slots holds an upload (`QuestionImageAssetId` or `ExplanationImageAssetId`). Its text and pictures are shown to anyone other than the creator only while that state is absent or `Approved`; admins can read it by ID and in admin reports. Every read path, count, random pick and search applies this gate to the item before sampling, ranking or counting (AC 3.25.7); the only way to read an uploaded picture's bytes as a non-owner is an item-bound delivery route that checks the item and the review state (AC 3.25.5). The publish guards (AC 3.25.6) are admin workflow policy; this gate is the security boundary. (See AC 3.25.)

### Keywords and categories

- **Invariant K1 – Public categories**
  - Categories are always public; there are no private categories. Category lists and navigation are visible to all users; item-level visibility rules still apply within categories. (See AC 3.1.1–3.1.2.)
- **Taxonomy item-tag slugs**
  - Any keyword name listed in a category’s taxonomy definition (`docs/quizymode_taxonomy.yaml`, including every L1 and L2 slug in that category) is treated as a **public** tag when attached to items: the API resolves or creates a non-private keyword (seeded by the taxonomy SQL as repo-managed public content) instead of a user-private, review-pending keyword.
- **Invariant K2 – Private navigation keywords**
  - Private navigation or item-level keywords (`IsPrivate = true`, `CreatedBy = user`) are visible only to their creator when authenticated. Anonymous users and other authenticated users never see another user’s private keywords in browsing or search. Keywords created while editing an item are owned by the item's creator, even when an admin performs the edit (AC 2.1.6). (See AC 3.3.1 and Terms / dictionary.)
- **Invariant K3 – Keyword-based navigation stability**
  - Keyword slugs and category slugs used in URLs resolve deterministically to a single effective category or keyword in a given scope (public wins over private on name collisions), ensuring that navigation paths consistently represent the same logical scope. (See AC 3.2 and AC 3.3.)

### Access and ownership

- **Invariant A1 – Visibility for anonymous users**
  - Anonymous users never see private keywords, and never see private items except when those items are included in a collection they can access under the collection-scoped visibility rules. All APIs and queries must enforce this rule. Anonymous users also never see an item whose uploaded pictures are not Approved, or those pictures (Invariant I7).
- **Invariant A2 – Owner access**
  - The owner of a resource always has access to that resource (subject to technical deletion), regardless of `IsPublic` or other sharing flags, unless a specific AC calls out an exception.

---

## Actors

Acceptance criteria are written per **Actor**. Use these labels consistently:

| Actor | Description |
|-------|-------------|
| **Owner** | The user who created the collection (or resource). |
| **Authenticated (non-owner)** | Signed-in user who is not the owner (and not admin, unless specified). |
| **Admin (non-owner)** | User with admin role who is not the owner. Unless stated otherwise, behavior is the same as **Authenticated (non-owner)**. |
| **Anonymous** | Not signed in. |

Where behavior is the same for "anyone with access" (e.g. public collection), the actor may be stated as **Anyone** (authenticated or anonymous).

---

## Terms / dictionary

Terms used in this document with a specific meaning:

| Term | Definition |
|------|-------------|
| **Active collection** | The single collection the authenticated user has selected for quick "add to collection" / "remove from collection" on item views. Exactly one at a time; the UI uses it for the +/- controls. |
| **Bookmark** (collection) | Saving a collection to "my bookmarks" for quick access. Distinct from ownership; anyone with access to a public collection can bookmark it. |
| **Breadcrumb** | A navigation trail showing the current path (e.g. **Categories** → category name → keyword(s)). Each segment is clickable and takes the user back to that level. On category pages, keyword segments may show descriptions as tooltips. Used to orient the user and allow quick navigation up the hierarchy. |
| **Bucket** | In the Sets view (Categories), a clickable card/cell representing a keyword or category, showing name, item count, and optional description. Clicking navigates deeper or opens the item list. |
| **Default collection** | The collection created automatically for a user when they have none; when the user's display name is available it uses the first 3 characters for the name (for example `Abc's Collection` for `Abcdefgh`) and `<display name> default collection` as the description; otherwise it falls back to "Default Collection". Used as the initial active collection. |
| **Discover** | The feature where anyone can search and browse collections shared by others that are both public and listed (IsPublic = true and IsDiscoverable = true). Results can be narrowed by **text** (`q`) on collection name and description, and by **item taxonomy**: optional category, up to two **navigation** keyword names (L1/L2, matching item `NavigationKeywordId1` / `NavigationKeywordId2`), and optional **item tag** names (AND on `ItemKeywords`). |
| **Item visibility** | Rules for which items a user sees outside a specific collection context: **Anonymous** — only non-private items; **Authenticated** — non-private items plus their own private items. Used when listing items by category/keywords or in APIs that filter by visibility (e.g. `GET /items` by category); collection-scoped visibility is defined separately. |
| **Item-level keyword** | A keyword that is a tag on items but is **not** used as rank-1 or rank-2 in the **KeywordRelation** tree for that category. Can be used as a filter (e.g. in query params) to narrow the item list; does not drive the Sets hierarchy. |
| **Metadata** (e.g. collection) | Summary data for a resource (name, description, owner, item count, IsPublic, etc.) **without** the full list of items. Items are loaded separately (e.g. `GET /items?collectionId=...`). |
| **Navigation keywords** | Keywords linked in the **KeywordRelation** table per category: **ParentKeywordId** null = root (rank-1, e.g. "aws"); otherwise the keyword is a child of that parent (rank-2, e.g. "Solutions Architect Associate" under "aws"). The same keyword can be a child of multiple parents in the same category. They drive the Sets view hierarchy and can appear in the URL path. Each item has required **NavigationKeywordId1** (rank-1) and **NavigationKeywordId2** (rank-2). |
| **Primary topic (rank 1)** | In import and create flows, the **first** navigation keyword under a category — e.g. the main subject or exam name (e.g. "Spanish", "AWS"). Shown in the UI as "Primary topic" for non-technical users. |
| **Subtopic (rank 2)** | In import and create flows, the **second** navigation keyword under a primary topic — e.g. a specific unit or section (e.g. "Greetings", "Solutions Architect Associate"). Shown in the UI as "Subtopic" for non-technical users. |
| **Topic group (topic picker)** | The shared **Topic** * control on every create or import form (AC 12.9): suggested topic chips (from the typed text, no LLM), a searchable category > L1 > L2 picker, and **Browse all topics** with the cascading selects; a chosen path shows as one removable breadcrumb pill. Never chooses a value for the user. |
| **Topic chip** | One suggested full category > L1 > L2 path from `POST /taxonomy/suggest`, labelled **Yours** or **Popular** when it comes from the user's own or the most-used topics rather than from the text (AC 12.10). |
| **Bulk Create (retired)** | The former AI-assisted flow on `/items/bulk-create`, removed in 3.100.0 (#296). The URL now redirects to `/create/import` (AC 2.2.5); admins import in bulk from `/admin/import` (AC 5.13) and everyone can use Paste text on `/create/import` (AC 2.6). |
| **Path keywords** | Keywords that appear in the URL **path** (e.g. `/categories/certs/aws`). They define the **navigation scope**; the same names are used in API parameters (e.g. `selectedKeywords`). |
| **Private item** | An item marked private (`IsPrivate = true`) that is hidden from general discovery flows, including category browsing, search, and direct access by users who do not otherwise have permission. If a private item is included in a collection that a user can access, that user may view and use the item **within the context of that collection only** (collection-scoped visibility); the item remains non-discoverable outside that collection. |
| **Private keyword** | A keyword with `IsPrivate = true`, `CreatedBy = user`. Visible only to that user when authenticated; anonymous users do not see it. |
| **Query keywords** | Keywords that appear in the URL **query** (e.g. `?keywords=s3,ec2`). They add an extra filter (AND) to the scope; items must have these keywords in addition to the path/keyword scope. |
| **Public keyword / item** | A keyword or item with `IsPrivate = false` (or equivalent). Visible to everyone in general discovery flows (category browsing, search) and via direct item access where allowed by API rules. |
| **Scope** | The **list of items** returned in a given context. Scope is determined by: (1) **Category/keywords** — navigation (path) and optional filters (query, scope filters) on the Categories page; or (2) **Collection** — items in a specific collection (collection-scoped visibility). The same scope can be viewed in **List**, **Flashcards**, or **Quiz** (and in Categories, also **Sets**). |
| **Scope filter** | On the Categories page, filters applied to the current scope to narrow the list (e.g. item type, search text, rating) without changing the category/keyword path. Applied in addition to path and query keywords. |
| **Seed set** | A logical label for a repo-managed item scope, typically derived from the GitHub-loaded seed-source path (for example `data/seed-source/items/sports`). It is returned in preview/apply responses for context, but per-item identity is the permanent `itemId`. |
| **Repo-managed item** | A public item whose content is controlled by source control and the admin seed-sync process, identified by a permanent repo-managed `itemId` that matches `Items.Id`. Repo-managed rows are marked with `IsRepoManaged = true`; the item row itself does not store extra seed metadata columns. |
| **Anyone with the link can view** / **List in Discover** | UI labels for the two collection sharing switches: **IsPublic** = true lets anyone with the link view and quiz; **IsDiscoverable** = true (only possible while IsPublic) also lists the collection in Discover. Older docs call link access "Shared with others". |
| **Slug** | A URL-friendly segment for a **category or navigation keyword** name: lowercase, spaces to dashes, special characters removed (e.g. "ACT Math" → `act-math`, "World Records" → `world-records`). Used in the path (e.g. `/categories/act-math/world-records`). The frontend resolves the category slug back to the canonical category name for API calls; keyword segments are treated as keyword **slugs** and are resolved on the backend. |
| **Sets view** | On the Categories page, the view that shows a grid of **buckets** (keywords or categories). Clicking a bucket either navigates deeper (adds a keyword to the path) or, at the leaf, opens the List view for that scope. Collections do not have a Sets view. |
| **User settings** | Per-user key-value preferences persisted in the database (e.g. **PageSize** for default pagination). Only the authenticated user can read or update their own settings. See AC 4.7. |
| **Collection-scoped visibility** | Visibility rules when viewing items through a specific collection. When a user can access a collection, the collection items API returns all items in that collection that are visible through the collection access rules, including private items shared through that collection. Those private items remain non-discoverable outside that collection context and are not treated as globally public or searchable items. |

---

## 1. Collections

### AC 1.1 Creating a collection

**API**

- **AC 1.1.1** [Owner] **Given** I am authenticated, **when** I call `POST /collections` with name (and optionally **description**, `isPublic`, `isDiscoverable`), **then** the API creates a collection with me as `CreatedBy` and returns 201; optional description is stored when provided; `isPublic` and `isDiscoverable` default to false if omitted, and `isDiscoverable` is stored as false unless `isPublic` is true. The response includes `isPublic` and `isDiscoverable`.
- **AC 1.1.2** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not authenticated, **when** I call `POST /collections`, **then** the API returns 401.

**UI**

- **AC 1.1.3** [Owner] **Given** I am on an item view (List Items, Flashcards, or Quiz) and open the create-collection dialog, **when** I submit with a name (name only; no description or "Shared with others" in this dialog), **then** a new collection is created per API and I can use it (e.g. set as active or add the item); validation reflects API rules (e.g. name required).
- **AC 1.1.4** [Owner] **Given** I am on the Collections page and create a new collection, **when** I submit the form with name (and optionally description, **Anyone with the link can view**, and **List in Discover** — the latter disabled until link access is checked), **then** the collection is created per API and I am taken to the new collection; validation reflects API rules (e.g. name required, description max length).

---

### AC 1.2 Active collection and default collection

**API**

- **AC 1.2.1** [Owner] **Given** I am authenticated, **when** my user record is created (e.g. first request after signup), **then** the backend may create a default collection using the user's display name: name = first 3 characters + `'s Collection` (for example `Abc's Collection` for `Abcdefgh`) and description = `<display name> default collection`; if no display name is available it falls back to "Default Collection". The backend sets `ActiveCollectionId` in my user settings.
- **AC 1.2.2** [Owner] **Given** I am authenticated and have no collections, **when** I call `GET /collections`, **then** the API creates and returns that same default collection shape (so I always have at least one).
- **AC 1.2.3** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not authenticated, **when** I call `GET /collections`, **then** the API returns 401.

**UI**

- **AC 1.2.4** [Owner] **Given** I am authenticated, **when** I use the app, **then** I have exactly one active collection at any time; the UI uses it for "add to collection" / "remove from collection" and for quick switching; if none is set, the frontend can set the first (or default) collection as active.
- **AC 1.2.5** [Owner] **Given** I am **authenticated** and on any item view (list, explore, quiz, or study), **when** the page loads, **then** I see controls to **add** the item to my active collection (+), **remove** the item from my active collection (-), and **select or change** my active collection.
- **AC 1.2.6** [Owner] **Given** I am authenticated and use the "select active collection" control (e.g. folder/manage-collections), **when** the list of collections is shown, **then** I see **only collections I own** (same set as `GET /collections`), and selecting one sets it as my active collection.
- **AC 1.2.7** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not authenticated, **when** I am on any item view, **then** I do **not** see the add-to-collection (+), remove-from-collection (-), or select-active-collection controls.
- **AC 1.2.8** [Owner] **Given** I am authenticated and have an active collection, **when** the item is **already in** my active collection, **then** the minus (-) icon is enabled and the plus (+) icon is disabled. **When** the item is **not** in my active collection, **then** the plus (+) icon is enabled and the minus (-) icon is disabled.
- **AC 1.2.9** [Owner] **Given** I am on the Collections page (**Mine** tab), **when** I use the "Set active" control on a collection card, **then** that collection becomes my active collection (used for add/remove on items); the current active collection is indicated on the card (e.g. checkmark or filled icon).
- **AC 1.2.10** [Owner] **Given** I am authenticated and have an active collection, **when** I visit Create Item (`/create/question`) page, **then** I see an informational banner identifying my active collection by name. The banner warns that newly created items will be added to that collection, and if the collection is public (or made public in the future), those items will be accessible via its shareable link. If the collection is currently public the banner highlights this prominently.
- **AC 1.2.11** [Owner] **Given** I successfully create one or more items (single-item create), **when** the creation succeeds, **then** the new item(s) are automatically added to my active collection (fire-and-forget; failure does not block or surface an error to the user).

---

### AC 1.3 Listing my collections

**API**

- **AC 1.3.1** [Owner] **Given** I am authenticated, **when** I call `GET /collections`, **then** the API returns only collections where I am the owner (`CreatedBy` = current user), ordered by creation date (newest first).
- **AC 1.3.2** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not authenticated, **when** I call `GET /collections`, **then** the API returns 401.

**UI**

- **AC 1.3.3** [Owner] **Given** I am on the **Mine** tab, **when** the list loads, **then** I see only my collections per API; ordering and empty state follow API behavior.
- **AC 1.3.4** [Owner] **Given** I am on the Collections page (**Mine** tab), **when** the list loads, **then** each of my collection cards shows: (1) an **Edit** control (e.g. pencil icon) that opens a modal to edit name, description, **Anyone with the link can view**, and **List in Discover**; (2) a **Set active** control that sets this collection as my active collection (current active is indicated); (3) a **Copy link** control that copies the collection URL (e.g. `{origin}/collections/{id}`) to the clipboard.
- **AC 1.3.5** [Owner] **Given** I use the Copy link control on a collection that is **not** public, **when** the link is copied, **then** the UI shows a warning that others cannot open the link until I turn on **Anyone with the link can view** (Edit); I can dismiss the warning.
- **AC 1.3.6** [Owner / Authenticated] **Given** I am on the Collections page (any tab), **when** I select a collection (e.g. by clicking its name on a card), **then** the UI shows a panel with the collection **name**, **description** (if any), and a **View details** control that opens the collection detail page (created by, date, items).
- **AC 1.3.7** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not signed in, **when** I open `/collections`, **then** I can use **Discover**; **Mine** and **Bookmarked** are disabled until I sign in; I do not see create-collection or other owner-only actions on this page; Discover supports category, L1/L2, item tags, text search, and open-by-ID; collection ratings on cards are **read-only** and bookmark controls are not offered until I sign in.

---

### AC 1.4 Viewing a collection by ID (shareable link when public)

**API**

- **AC 1.4.1** [Anyone] **Given** the collection has `IsPublic = true`, **when** anyone (authenticated or anonymous) calls `GET /collections/{id}`, **then** the API returns the collection (name, description, owner, item count, `isPublic`, `isDiscoverable`) and no login is required, whether or not the collection is listed in Discover. `GET /collections` (AC 1.3.1) returns `isPublic` and `isDiscoverable` per collection too.
- **AC 1.4.2** [Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** the collection has `IsPublic = false` and I am not the owner, **when** I call `GET /collections/{id}`, **then** the API returns 404 (existence not revealed).
- **AC 1.4.3** [Anonymous] **Given** the collection has `IsPublic = false`, **when** I call `GET /collections/{id}` (anonymous), **then** the API returns 404.
- **AC 1.4.4** [Owner] **Given** I am the owner, **when** I call `GET /collections/{id}`, **then** the API returns the collection regardless of IsPublic.
- **AC 1.4.5** [Anyone] **Given** the collection ID does not exist, **when** I call `GET /collections/{id}`, **then** the API returns 404.

**UI**

- **AC 1.4.6** [Anyone] **Given** I have access to the collection (per API rules), **when** I open `/collections/{id}`, **then** I see the collection name, description, item count, and (for owner) edit/delete controls. "Owner" is decided by comparing the collection's `createdBy` with my internal user id from `GET /users/me`, never with the sign-in token's `sub` (they differ); the same rule applies to owner-only controls on items, comments, and the My Collections list (#207); `GET /collections/{id}` returns **collection metadata only**. The list of items in the collection is loaded via a separate items API (see AC 1.5). When access is denied per API, I see an appropriate error (e.g. not found).

---

### AC 1.5 Items in a collection (List Items / Flashcards / Quiz via shareable link when public)

**Design:** The list of items in a collection is returned by **`GET /items?collectionId={id}`** (not `GET /collections/{id}/items`). One items API with a collection filter keeps the contract simple and matches "items by category"; the same item DTO and pagination apply. Use `GET /collections/{id}` for collection metadata only.

**API**

- **AC 1.5.1** [Anyone] **Given** the collection has `IsPublic = true`, **when** anyone calls `GET /items?collectionId={id}`, **then** the API returns all items in that collection according to **collection-scoped visibility**, including private items that the owner has placed in that collection; those private items remain non-discoverable outside that collection and are not treated as globally public items.
- **AC 1.5.2** [Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** the collection has `IsPublic = false` and I am not the owner, **when** I call `GET /items?collectionId={id}`, **then** the API returns 404.
- **AC 1.5.3** [Owner] **Given** I am the owner, **when** I call `GET /items?collectionId={id}`, **then** the API returns all items in that collection.
- **AC 1.5.4** [Anyone] **Given** I request items without `collectionId` (e.g. by category), **when** I call `GET /items`, **then** item visibility applies (anonymous: only non-private; authenticated: own private + non-private).

**UI**

- **AC 1.5.5** [Anyone] **Given** I have access to the collection (per API), **when** I open the collection in the UI and use List Items, Flashcards, or Quiz for that collection, **then** I see the collection **metadata** (collection name, description, owner name, date created) and the **list of items** returned by the API; when access is denied, I see an appropriate error. **URL examples:** List Items mode: **`/collections/{id}`**; Flashcards mode: **`/explore/collections/{id}`**; Quiz mode: **`/quiz/collections/{id}`**.
- **AC 1.5.6** [Anyone] **Given** I am reviewing or using items within a collection in List Items, Flashcards, or Quiz modes, **when** the UI loads items for that collection, **then** it calls `GET /items?collectionId={id}` using the collection ID from the URL (not direct per-item lookups) so that private items and their private keywords included in that collection are visible per collection-scoped visibility rules.
- **AC 1.5.7** [Anyone] **Given** I open an item's details screen from a collection List Items, Flashcards, or Quiz view, **when** I use the back control on the item-details screen, **then** I return to the same collection URL I came from (including the current item, current view, and query-string scope) so my place in the collection flow is preserved; for Quiz this includes restoring the current item's revealed / answered state when that session state is still available.
- **AC 1.5.8** [Anyone] **Given** I am in **Flashcards** mode for a **collection** scope, **when** I study an item, **then** the Flashcards interaction matches **AC 3.6.2a** (question face-up; click to flip to answer and explanation; click again to flip back; reset to question face-up on previous/next item).
- **AC 1.5.9** [Anyone] **Given** I open a collection in **Quiz** mode, **when** its items are loading, unavailable, or empty, **then** the page waits for the full playable collection roster before starting, never treats a temporary single-item fetch as the complete quiz, and shows an appropriate loading, retry, or empty state with a public route back to browsing. Collection quiz scoring, completion, review, restoration, and replay otherwise follow **AC 3.6.7–3.6.12**.
- **AC 1.5.10** [Anyone] **Given** I call `GET /items` with `isRandom=true`, **then** the server picks up to `pageSize` distinct random items of the visible scope in the database and loads keywords and category only for those items, so an unscoped random request no longer loads every visible item into memory; `totalCount` is still the size of the whole scope (3.102.1, games hub load time).
- **AC 1.5.11** [Anyone] **Given** I call `GET /items` (list, random, `collectionId`), the export, or any other item list, **then** an item whose uploaded pictures are not `Approved` (state `PendingReview`, `Rejected` or `Withdrawn`) is returned only to its creator; to everyone else it is absent, also from counts, even when it is public or sits in a collection they can open. Items with no uploaded picture, and items with curated pictures, are unaffected. (See Invariant I7, AC 3.25.7.)

---

### AC 1.6 Adding and removing items (owner only)

**API**

- **AC 1.6.1** [Owner] **Given** I am the owner, **when** I call POST add/bulk or DELETE to add/remove items in that collection, **then** the API succeeds (e.g. 200/204).
- **AC 1.6.2** [Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** I am authenticated but not the owner, **when** I call add/remove for that collection, **then** the API returns 404 (identical to a missing collection id) when the collection is private, or 403 ProblemDetails (`title` "Forbidden", `detail` "Only the collection owner can add or remove items.") when it is public. Request-body validation (400) runs first; the ownership check runs before any item lookup, so a public non-owner gets 403 even for unknown item ids. Behavior change (#289): this previously returned 400 and revealed that a private collection exists.
- **AC 1.6.3** [Admin (non-owner)] Same as Authenticated (non-owner) (AC 1.6.2): an admin is explicitly treated as a non-owner (404 on a private collection, 403 on a public one).
- **AC 1.6.4** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not authenticated, **when** I call add/remove, **then** the API returns 401.
- **AC 1.6.7** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /collections/{collectionId}/items/bulk`, **when** `itemIds` is omitted or null, is an empty array, or contains the empty GUID, **then** the API returns 400 with `ItemIds is required.` / `At least one ItemId is required.` / `All ItemIds must be valid GUIDs.`, adds nothing, and never exposes an internal exception message; body validation runs before the collection lookup and ownership check. Behavior change: an empty array and an array containing the empty GUID previously returned 200 (0 added / skipped) and now return 400 (#273).
- **AC 1.6.8** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /collections/{collectionId}/items`, **when** `itemId` is omitted or the empty GUID, **then** the API returns 400 `ItemId is required.` and adds nothing; validation runs before the lookup and ownership checks. Behavior change: this previously returned 404 (#281).
- **AC 1.6.9** [Owner] **Given** I call `POST /collections/{collectionId}/items` or `/items/bulk` for my collection, **then** an item can be added only if it is public, created by me, or published by its creator through a public collection owned by that creator. Otherwise the single add returns the same 404 `Item.NotFound` as a missing item (checked before the idempotent already-in-collection result), and bulk add skips the item (counted in `skippedCount`, absent from `addedItemIds`). Collections owned by me or by a third party never make another user's private item addable, and admins get no exception (AC 1.6.3). Copies made earlier survive later revocation of the item's visibility (#285). An item whose uploaded pictures are not `Approved` can be added only by its creator (AC 3.25.7), even when it is public or published through a public collection; for anyone else the single add returns the same 404 and bulk add skips it.

**UI**

- **AC 1.6.5** [Owner] **Given** I am on a collection I own, **when** I use "add to collection" / "remove from collection", **then** the UI calls the API per above and reflects success or error.
- **AC 1.6.6** [Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** I am viewing a collection I do not own, **when** the page loads, **then** add/remove controls for that collection are not offered (or are disabled) per API rules.

---

### AC 1.7 Updating a collection (name, description, Shared with others)

**API**

- **AC 1.7.1** [Owner] **Given** I am the owner, **when** I call `PUT /collections/{id}` with name and/or description and/or `isPublic` and/or `isDiscoverable` (omitted fields are unchanged), **then** the API updates the collection and returns success with `isPublic` and `isDiscoverable`. Setting `isPublic = false` also sets `isDiscoverable = false`; `isDiscoverable = true` on a collection that is not public is ignored (stays false).
- **AC 1.7.2** [Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** I am not the owner, **when** I call `PUT /collections/{id}`, **then** the API returns 404 (identical to a missing id) when the collection is private, or 403 ProblemDetails (`title` "Forbidden", `detail` "You can only update your own collections.") when it is public, and nothing is changed. Request-body validation (400) runs first. Admins are non-owners (no exception). Behavior change (#289): this previously returned 400.

**UI**

- **AC 1.7.3** [Owner] **Given** I am on the collection **detail** page as owner, **when** I edit the description or the sharing switches, **then** I see two labeled switches (keyboard-operable, `role="switch"` with `aria-checked`): **Anyone with the link can view** (`isPublic`) and **List in Discover** (`isDiscoverable`), each with a one-line explanation; **List in Discover** is disabled while link access is off, and turning link access off also turns Discover off (the UI sends `isPublic: false, isDiscoverable: false`); changes are persisted per API. The **Details** dialog shows visibility as "Private", "Anyone with the link", or "Anyone with the link · listed in Discover".
- **AC 1.7.4** [Owner] **Given** I am on the Collections **list** page (**Mine** tab), **when** I use the Edit control on a collection card, **then** a modal opens where I can change name, description, **Anyone with the link can view**, and **List in Discover** (same rules as AC 1.7.3: Discover unchecks and disables when link access is unchecked); saving persists changes per API and closes the modal.
- **AC 1.7.5** [Owner, or Anyone viewing a public collection] **Given** I am on a collection detail page that I own or that has `IsPublic = true`, **when** I click **Share**, **then** a dialog shows the collection link in a read-only field with **Copy link** (clipboard write; shows "Link copied." or a copy-failed hint), **Share via…** only when the browser supports `navigator.share` (cancelling the share sheet does nothing; any other share failure falls back to copying the link), and **Show QR code**, which lazy-loads the `qrcode` library and renders a QR image of the link (alt text names the collection). For a private collection the dialog warns that only the owner can open the link until **Anyone with the link can view** is on. Escape or the close button dismisses the dialog.

---

### AC 1.8 Deleting a collection

**API**

- **AC 1.8.1** [Owner] **Given** I am the owner, **when** I call `DELETE /collections/{id}`, **then** the API removes the collection and its collection-items, bookmarks and every user's test-date plan for it (AC 3.23.4).
- **AC 1.8.2** [Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** I am not the owner, **when** I call `DELETE /collections/{id}`, **then** the API returns 404 (identical to a missing id) when the collection is private, or 403 ProblemDetails (`title` "Forbidden", `detail` "You can only delete your own collections.") when it is public. The collection and its items, bookmarks and shares survive. Admins are non-owners (no exception). Behavior change (#289): this previously returned 400.

**UI**

- **AC 1.8.3** [Owner] **Given** I am on the collection page as owner, **when** I delete the collection (with confirmation), **then** the UI calls the API and redirects or updates the list on success; errors are shown per API response.

---

### AC 1.9 Discover (collections shared with others)

**API**

- **AC 1.9.1** [Anyone] **Given** collections exist with `IsPublic = true` and `IsDiscoverable = true`, **when** anyone calls `GET /collections/discover` with optional `q`, `page`, `pageSize`, **then** the API returns matching public, discoverable collections paginated; if `q` is set, the collection's **name** or **description** must match (case-insensitive contains); if authenticated, bookmark state can be returned per collection.
- **AC 1.9.1a** [Anyone] **Given** I call `GET /collections/discover` with optional `category` (category name), optional `keywords` (comma-separated, 0–2 **navigation** keyword names, requires `category`), and optional `tags` (comma-separated item tag names, AND semantics on item keywords), **then** only public collections that contain **at least one item** satisfying **all** supplied item filters are included; when `q` is also set, the collection must match **both** the text filter and the item filters. Unknown category (for filters) yields no matches; unknown tag name yields no matches; `keywords` without `category` returns **400**.
- **AC 1.9.1b** [Anyone] **Given** I pass `category` and one or two `keywords`, **when** the path is not valid for that category (same rules as item navigation), **then** the API returns **400** with a validation-style error.
- **AC 1.9.2** [Anyone] **Given** a collection has `IsPublic = false`, or `IsPublic = true` with `IsDiscoverable = false` (link only), **when** discover is queried (with or without text or item filters), **then** that collection does not appear in the response; it is still reachable by ID per AC 1.4.1.
- **AC 1.9.3** [Anyone] **Given** I call discover or view discover results, **when** the API returns public collections from other users, **then** the response **must not include** other users' **email addresses** (e.g. only owner id and/or display name); this prevents email leaking to spammers.

**UI**

- **AC 1.9.4** [Anyone] **Given** I am on Discover, **when** I use text search and optional filters (category, L1/L2, item tags), **then** results match the API; I see only public collections; results are paginated; when signed in I see my bookmark state and can bookmark or unbookmark.
- **AC 1.9.5** [Anyone] **Given** I am viewing Discover results, **when** I see other users' collections, **then** I do **not** see other users' email addresses (only owner display name or identifier as provided by the API); the UI must not display or leak emails.
- **AC 1.9.6** [Anyone] **Given** I have or know a collection ID, **when** I search or enter the collection ID in the UI (e.g. on Discover or an "Open collection by ID" control), **then** I can open that collection at `/collections/{id}`; if it is public or I am the owner, I see metadata and items; otherwise I see an appropriate error. This allows finding a collection by ID without browsing discover.
- **AC 1.9.7** [Anyone] **Given** I am not signed in, **when** I use the main site navigation, **then** I still see **Collections** and can open the Collections page to use Discover (see AC 1.3.7).

---

### AC 1.10 Bookmarking collections

**API**

- **AC 1.10.1** [Owner] **Given** I am the owner and can access the collection, **when** I call `POST /collections/{id}/bookmark` or `DELETE /collections/{id}/bookmark`, **then** the API records or removes my bookmark and returns success.
- **AC 1.10.2** [Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** I am authenticated and can access the collection (e.g. via discover or by opening `/collections/{id}` when public) and I am not the owner, **when** I call bookmark/unbookmark, **then** the API records or removes my bookmark and returns success. **Given** the collection is private and I am not the owner (including an admin), **when** I call `POST /collections/{id}/bookmark`, **then** the API returns the same 404 as a missing id and writes no bookmark row; removing a bookmark I already hold on such a collection (`DELETE /collections/{id}/bookmark`) still returns 204 and deletes my row. Behavior change (#289): bookmarking a private collection previously returned 204 and wrote a row.
- **AC 1.10.3** [Admin (non-owner)] Same as Authenticated (non-owner) (AC 1.10.2).
- **AC 1.10.4** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not authenticated, **when** I call bookmark or unbookmark, **then** the API returns 401.
- **AC 1.10.6** [Authenticated] **Given** I bookmarked a collection that is now private and I am not its owner (bookmark written before #289, or the owner later made it private), **when** I call `GET /collections/bookmarks`, **then** that collection is omitted from the list (its name, creator and item count are not revealed); my bookmark row is kept and can still be removed with `DELETE /collections/{id}/bookmark`. Private collections I own, and public ones, are listed (#298).

**UI**

- **AC 1.10.5** [Owner / Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** I have access to the collection, **when** I use the bookmark control, **then** the UI calls the API and shows bookmarked state; unbookmark is available per API.

---

### AC 1.11 Collection description

**API**

- **AC 1.11.1** [Owner] **Given** I am the owner, **when** I create or update a collection with an optional `description`, **then** the API stores it; discover search matches by name or description.

**UI**

- **AC 1.11.2** [Owner] **Given** I am creating or editing a collection, **when** I set or clear the description, **then** it is shown on the collection page and used in discover per API.

---

### AC 1.12 Collection ratings

**API**

- **AC 1.12.1** [Owner / Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** I am authenticated (including owner), **when** I call `POST /collections/{id}/rating` with `{ "stars": 1..5 }`, **then** the API stores at most one rating per user per collection; submitting again updates my rating.
- **AC 1.12.2** [Anyone] **Given** the collection exists, **when** I call `GET /collections/{id}/rating`, **then** the API returns count, average stars, and (if authenticated) my rating (myStars). **Given** the collection is private and I am not the owner (including an admin, or anonymous), **then** the API returns the same empty-body 404 as a missing id (#298).

**UI**

- **AC 1.12.3** [Anyone] **Given** I am viewing a collection, **when** the page loads, **then** I see rating stats (count, average) per API; if authenticated, I can set or change my rating (1-5 stars) and the UI calls the API.
- **AC 1.12.4** [Anonymous] **Given** I am viewing a collection rating UI that is read-only because I am not signed in (for example a Discover collection card or a collection study header), **when** I hover the stars, **then** I see a hint that signed-in users can rate the collection.

---

### AC 1.13 Who bookmarked (owner only)

**API**

- **AC 1.13.1** [Owner] **Given** I am the owner, **when** I call `GET /collections/{id}/bookmarks`, **then** the API returns the list of users who bookmarked (userId, name when available, bookmarkedAt).
- **AC 1.13.2** [Authenticated (non-owner)] **Given** I am not the owner, **when** I call `GET /collections/{id}/bookmarks`, **then** the API returns 404 (identical to a missing id) when the collection is private, or 403 ProblemDetails (`title` "Forbidden", `detail` "Only the collection owner can see who bookmarked it.") when it is public. Admins are non-owners. Behavior change (#289): a non-owner previously received 400.

**UI**

- **AC 1.13.3** [Owner] **Given** I am on the collection page as owner, **when** the page loads, **then** I can see the "Who bookmarked" list (users who found and bookmarked this collection); non-owners do not see this section.

---

### AC 1.14 Export and print a collection (#258)

All of this is built in the browser from the items the collection page has already loaded. There is no backend endpoint and no API change.

- **AC 1.14.1** [Anyone who can view the collection] **Given** I am viewing a collection that has at least one item (as the owner, or because it is public), **when** the page loads, **then** I see an "Export and print" group with **Download CSV**, **Download TSV**, **Quizlet text**, **Print** and a "Hide answers when printing (worksheet)" checkbox. All are buttons or a checkbox with visible labels, operable by keyboard, and wrap without horizontal overflow at 320px. The group is not shown for an empty collection. Downloads come from all loaded items, not only the current page.
- **AC 1.14.2** [Anyone who can view the collection] **Given** I use an export button, **then** the file is named after the collection (`my-collection.csv`, `.tsv`, `.txt`; `collection.*` when the name has no letters or digits). **CSV** and **TSV** (UTF-8 with a byte-order mark so spreadsheets read accents) have the header `question, correct answer, incorrect answers, explanation`, one row per item, the incorrect answers in one column joined with ` | `, and fields quoted (`"` doubled) when they contain the separator, a double quote or a line break. Against spreadsheet formula injection, a CSV/TSV cell that starts with `=`, `+`, `-`, `@`, a tab or a carriage return gets a leading `'`; the importer removes that `'` again when it is followed by one of those characters. **Quizlet text** is `question TAB correct answer` per line with no header or quoting (tabs and line breaks inside a field become spaces); incorrect answers and explanations are not included. Each format can be pasted or uploaded back through **AC 2.6.9** and gives the same questions and answers (a round-trip test enforces this).
- **AC 1.14.3** [Anyone who can view the collection] **Given** I choose **Print**, **then** the browser print dialog opens for a print-only view that lists every item as a numbered list with its question, answer and explanation (empty explanations are omitted); the site navigation, footer, banners and the rest of the page are hidden when printing. **Given** "Hide answers when printing (worksheet)" is checked, **then** the printout shows each question with a blank "Answer:" line and shows neither answers nor explanations.
- **AC 1.14.4** [Anyone who can view the collection] **Given** an item has a curated question picture (AC 2.1.19, #407), **when** I print (worksheet mode included), **then** the picture is printed under the question in a fixed 192x128 px box (`object-fit: contain`, colors preserved, kept with its question across page breaks), with the item's alt text as `alt`. **Given** the picture key is not bundled in the running build, **then** the printout shows "[Picture unavailable: {alt text}]" instead. **Given** an item has an explanation picture (#403) and I print without worksheet mode, **then** the picture is printed after the answer and explanation (at most 8 cm wide, kept with its question, colors preserved, alt text as `alt`); a worksheet (answers hidden) never prints it, and the Enlarge button is hidden when printing. A "picture question" means an item with a question picture; an explanation picture alone does not make a question a picture question, because the question still works as text. **Given** I click **Download CSV**, **Download TSV** or **Quizlet text** and at least one item is a picture question, **then** a dialog "Some questions have pictures" opens instead of downloading: "N of M questions include a picture. CSV, TSV and Quizlet files can only hold text, so pictures can't be included." (plus "Explanation pictures are not included either." when some item also has an explanation picture), with two radio options in a `radiogroup` named "Export options": **Skip N picture questions** (default; exports the other M-N questions) and **Export text only (pictures not included)** (all questions as text; enabled only after ticking "I understand pictures won't be in this file, so some questions may not make sense without them."; until then the primary button is disabled with "Tick the box to continue."). **Given** every question is a picture question and Skip is selected, **then** the primary button is disabled with "Every question here has a picture, so there is nothing to export as text. Choose "Export text only" or cancel." The first radio has the initial focus, Cancel and Escape close the dialog without downloading and return focus to the button that opened it, the primary button is named "Download CSV", "Download TSV" or "Download Quizlet text", and after a download a polite status reads "Downloaded N questions. M picture questions skipped." or "Downloaded N questions as text only." **Given** no item is a picture question, **then** the files download immediately with no dialog; Print and the JSON export (AC 2.2.7.10) never show the dialog. (frontend tests: QuestionImagePlacements.test.tsx, CollectionExportControls.test.tsx, exportFormats.test.ts)

---

## 2. Items

### AC 2.1 Creating and editing items

**UI**

- **AC 2.1.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I am creating or editing an item, **when** I use the form, **then** I see a **Save to** section (AC 2.1.15) containing the **Topic** * group (AC 12.9), with **Additional keywords** (optional) under **More options**. The Topic group offers suggested topic chips (Create only, from the question, answer and explanation, AC 12.10), a searchable picker (AC 12.15) and a collapsed **Browse all topics** disclosure that keeps the **Category** *, **Primary topic (rank 1)** * and **Subtopic (rank 2)** * selects; once a full path is chosen it shows as one removable breadcrumb (AC 12.13). I must choose a full category > L1 > L2 path from values returned by **`GET /taxonomy`** (required L1/L2 path); navigation is not free-typed and no value is ever chosen for me. When I submit without a complete path, the form blocks the save, shows "Choose a topic: pick a suggestion, search, or browse all topics." on the group and moves focus to the search box (AC 12.14; the category select has no native `required`, because a required control inside the closed disclosure would block the submit invisibly). On Edit the stored path shows as the breadcrumb, no chips are offered and no suggestion request is made (AC 12.11). **Additional keywords** may include picks from the taxonomy flat list and optional free text; non-taxonomy extras are persisted as **private pending** per API rules (AC 3.10.4). The chosen rank1 and rank2 are sent as `navigationKeyword1` and `navigationKeyword2` and stored as **NavigationKeywordId1** and **NavigationKeywordId2**; they are included in item keywords on save.
- **AC 2.1.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I am creating or editing an item (any user, including admin), **when** the item form is shown, **then** I see fields for question, answers, an optional group label (AC 2.1.17), explanation, source, navigation keywords, and simple tags, ordered question and answers first, then **More options**, then **Save to** (AC 2.1.15). The form does **not** include Factual Risk or Review Comments inputs; those fields are internal and not surfaced in the item create/edit UI or API.
- **AC 2.1.3** [Admin] **Given** I am creating or editing an item as an **admin**, **when** the item form is shown, **then** I see the same fields as regular users, plus the ability to toggle `IsPrivate` and set `ReadyForReview`. Factual Risk and Review Comments are not exposed in this form.
- **AC 2.1.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I am creating or editing an item as a **regular user**, **when** I want admins to review my item to make it public, **then** I can toggle a checkbox labeled (or equivalent to) **“Request admin review to make this item public”**; turning it on marks the item as ready for admin review and adds it to the admin review board; turning it off removes it from the review board if it has not been approved yet.
- **AC 2.1.8** [Authenticated] **Given** I am creating or editing an item, **when** every incorrect-answer field is blank and I submit, **then** the form blocks submission with "Please provide at least one incorrect answer". When an incorrect answer equals the correct answer (case-insensitive, after trimming), or two incorrect answers equal each other, the form blocks submission with "Incorrect answers must be different from the correct answer." / "Incorrect answers must be different from each other." (checked in that order, blanks ignored, mirroring **AC 2.1.7**). This also applies when editing a legacy item that already has such a list: the edit cannot be saved until it is fixed (#276).
- **AC 2.1.9** [Authenticated] **Given** I edit an item, **when** the form loads, **then** it shows one incorrect-answer field per stored incorrect answer, with a minimum of three fields (an item with 4 incorrect answers, e.g. from an admin bulk import, study-guide accept or seed, shows all 4; items with 1-3 show three fields as before); the Create Item form always shows three fields. **When** I save, blank incorrect-answer fields are dropped client-side (their speech metadata is discarded and the remaining answers' speech is re-indexed, AC 3.12.7) and every non-blank answer, including a 4th, is sent in `PUT /items/{id}`; the API accepts at most 4.
- **AC 2.1.14** [Authenticated] **Given** I open `/items/{id}/edit` for an item I do not own and I am not an admin, **when** my user id has loaded, **then** the page shows "You can only edit your own items." instead of the form (a spinner is shown while the id loads; if the id fails to load the form is shown and the server enforces access). **When** a save returns 403, 404 or 401 the form shows "You can only edit your own items.", "This item no longer exists or you don't have access to it." or "Your session has expired. Please sign in again." respectively; **when** deleting from the item detail page fails, an inline message shows "You can only delete your own items." (403), "This item no longer exists." (404), "Your session has expired. Please sign in again." (401) or "Failed to delete item." A 403 or 404 does not clear tokens or redirect (AC 4.3.4); an unrecoverable 401 follows the existing AC 4.3.4 redirect.
- **AC 2.1.15** [Authenticated] **Given** I am creating or editing an item, **when** the form is shown, **then** the fields appear in this order (#296): a **Question and answers** section (`h2`) with **Question** *, **Correct Answer** * and the **Incorrect Answers** fields; a **More options** button (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`) that discloses **Group label** (first), **Explanation**, **Source**, **Additional keywords** and the optional **Pronunciation and language** inputs (AC 3.12.3); and a **Save to** section (`h2`) with the **Topic** group (AC 12.9) and the visibility checkboxes (AC 2.1.3, 2.1.4). **More options** is collapsed by default on Create. It opens by itself on Edit when the item has a group label, explanation, source, extra keyword or speech value, and on either page when a save error names a field inside it; such an error opens it when it appears, and I can close it afterwards. Labels are unchanged; the payload sent to `POST /items` / `PUT /items/{id}` gains only `groupLabel` (AC 2.1.18) (frontend tests: ItemForm.test.tsx).
- **AC 2.1.16** [Authenticated] **Given** I am on `/create/question`, **when** I look at the actions, **then** there are **Cancel**, **Save and add another** and **Save and study**; both save buttons are submit buttons of the form, so the browser's required-field checks and the same validation as before (AC 2.1.8, 12.14) run for either and are disabled while a save is in flight, so a second click cannot submit twice. **Save and add another** saves, then clears the question, answers, explanation and speech fields while keeping the category, primary topic, subtopic, extra keywords, source, group label and visibility choices, moves focus to **Question**, and shows "Saved. Add the next question." in a non-blocking `role="status"` region (cleared on the next save); no navigation happens. **Save and study** (also what Enter in a field submits) saves, waits for the item to be added to my active collection, then opens `/explore/collections/{activeCollectionId}/item/{itemId}`; with no active collection it opens `/items/{itemId}` as before. On Edit there is a single **Save** button (**Saving...** while pending) and behavior after saving is unchanged (frontend tests: CreateItemPage.test.tsx, ItemForm.test.tsx).
- **AC 2.1.17** [Authenticated] **Given** I open **More options** (AC 2.1.15), **then** its first field is the optional **Group label (optional)** text input (placeholder "e.g., Mammals", at most 30 characters) with the help text "Used by Sort It. Items with the same label form a group. Up to 30 characters." linked to the input with `aria-describedby`. The closed-panel hint reads "Group label, explanation, source, extra keywords and pronunciation (all optional)." A server error naming the group label opens the panel. On Create a blank label is omitted from `POST /items` and a filled one is sent trimmed; on Edit the stored label is shown, the label is sent trimmed with every save, and an empty value is sent as `""` to clear it. (frontend tests: ItemForm.test.tsx, CreateItemPage.test.tsx, EditItemPage.test.tsx)

**API**

- **AC 2.1.5** [Authenticated] **Given** I am creating an item via `POST /items`, **when** I include `readyForReview = true` in the request body, **then** the API stores this flag on the item (e.g. `ReadyForReview = true`) while keeping `IsPrivate = true`; only admins can later change the item to non-private.
- **AC 2.1.6** [Authenticated] **Given** I am updating my own item via `PUT /items/{id}`, **when** I include `readyForReview = true` or `false` in the request body, **then** the API updates the stored review flag but does **not** allow me to change `IsPrivate` from true to false unless I am an admin. (Owner or admin only; see 2.1.10-2.1.13.) **Re-review rule (#290):** when a non-admin owner saves an edit of an item that is currently public (for example an approved item), the API sets it back to private (admin review is needed again); an admin's edit keeps the `isPrivate` value sent. The edit page shows "Saving changes will make this item private again until an administrator approves it." to a non-admin owner of a public item before saving (no notice for admins or private items), and after a successful save shows "Your changes were saved. This item is now private again until an administrator approves it." with a Back to categories button instead of redirecting. **Keyword ownership (#291):** non-taxonomy keywords created or reused during `PUT /items/{id}` are resolved for the item's creator (`Item.CreatedBy`), not the caller, so when an admin edits another user's item the new private keywords belong to the item's owner (visible to them under K2) and the admin's own private keywords are not attached; edits by the owner are unchanged.
- **AC 2.1.7** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, `POST /items/bulk` (admin only, AC 2.2.6.17) or `POST /items/upload-to-collection`, **when** any item has 0 or more than 4 incorrect answers, or any incorrect answer that is blank or whitespace-only, **then** the API returns 400 and saves nothing (the whole batch for bulk and upload). Study-guide import chunk and dedup submission reject the same input with their existing per-item validation error ("Item N: incorrect answers cannot be blank." / "incorrectAnswers must have 1 to 4 items."); at finalize such items are left out of the import, and if none remain finalize returns 400 `Import.NoItems`. A group label (AC 2.1.18) is rejected with 400 and nothing saved when it is longer than 30 characters after trimming ("Group label must be 30 characters or fewer.") or contains a line break, control or invisible formatting character ("Group label can't contain line breaks."); this applies to `POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, `POST /items/bulk` and `POST /items/upload-to-collection`. Existing items with no usable distractor are not migrated; they are left out of quiz surfaces by **AC 3.6.17**. Item writes (`POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, `POST /items/bulk`, `POST /items/upload-to-collection`, the edited candidates of `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/accept`, and seed sync) also reject, with 400 and nothing saved, an incorrect answer equal to the correct answer ("Incorrect answers must be different from the correct answer.") or two incorrect answers equal to each other ("Incorrect answers must be different from each other."); comparison trims and ignores case, blanks are ignored, and the correct-answer message is reported first. `PUT /items/{id}` rejects a legacy item whose stored list has such duplicates until it is fixed. Study-guide import chunk/dedup submission reports the same rules per item ("Item N: incorrect answers must be different from the correct answer." / "Item N: incorrect answers must be different from each other."); at finalize, stored items that break them (e.g. chunks accepted before this rule) are left out of the import, and if none remain finalize returns 400 `Import.NoItems`.
- **AC 2.1.10** [Authenticated] **Given** I am the owner of an item or an admin, **when** I call `PUT /items/{id}`, `DELETE /items/{id}` or `PUT /items/{id}/category`, **then** the request is processed as before (an admin may modify any item, including another user's private item).
- **AC 2.1.11** [Authenticated] **Given** an item is private and I am neither its owner nor an admin, **when** I call any of those three endpoints, **then** the API returns 404 with the same status and empty body as for a non-existent id, and nothing changes (no item, category, keyword or audit row is created or modified).
- **AC 2.1.12** [Authenticated] **Given** an item is public and I am neither its owner nor an admin (including when my user id cannot be resolved), **when** I call any of those three endpoints, **then** the API returns 403 with a ProblemDetails body (title "Forbidden", detail "You can only edit or delete your own items.") and nothing changes. Request-body validation (400) on `PUT` still runs first because it touches no data. Behavior change (#286): previously any user could update, delete or recategorize any item, and a PUT by a non-admin forced the item private. The 403 body differs from comments/ideas, which return an empty 403. A private item that is visible through its creator's public collection still returns 404, not 403, to non-owners.
- **AC 2.1.13** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not signed in, **when** I call `PUT /items/{id}`, `DELETE /items/{id}` or `PUT /items/{id}/category`, **then** the API returns 401 before body validation or any data access. Behavior change (#286): `PUT /items/{id}` previously required no authorization and returned 400.
- **AC 2.1.18** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, `POST /items/bulk` or `POST /items/upload-to-collection` with an optional `groupLabel` (#359), **then** it is trimmed and stored (a blank value on create is stored as no label), at most 30 characters after trimming and without line breaks, control or invisible formatting characters (AC 2.1.7). On `PUT /items/{id}` an omitted or `null` `groupLabel` keeps the stored label and a blank one clears it. `groupLabel` (null when none) is returned by `POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, `GET /items/{id}`, every item list response (collections, random, daily and review queue items included) and `GET /admin/items/review-board`, where the Review Board shows "Group: {label}" for an item that has one. The column is nullable `varchar(30)` (migration `AddItemGroupLabel`); there is no index, no new endpoint and no authorization change. Seed sync preserves an existing label and export omits it (follow-up). (backend tests: GroupLabelHttpTests, GroupLabelReadTests, GroupLabelPostgresTests; frontend test: ReviewBoardPage.test.tsx)
- **AC 2.1.19** [Anyone] **Given** an item has a curated question picture (#407) or explanation picture (#403), **then** every item read returns `questionImage: { key, altText }` and `explanationImage: { key, altText }` (each `null` when none): `GET /items`, collection items, random, daily and review-queue items, `GET /games/roster`, `GET /items/{id}` and `GET /admin/items/review-board`, and the approve/reject responses (`PUT /admin/items/{id}/approval`, `PUT /admin/items/{id}/rejection`). Three admin reads return `questionImage` only (question picture only; no `explanationImage`): `GET /admin/daily-quiz`, `POST /admin/daily-quiz/{date}/items/{itemId}/exclude` and `GET /admin/analytics/question-quality` (each `null` when the item has none or no longer exists). The question image is stored as two nullable columns, `QuestionImageKey` `varchar(100)` and `QuestionImageAlt` `varchar(300)` (migration `AddItemQuestionImage`, additive), guarded by the check constraint `CK_Items_QuestionImage_Alt`; the explanation image is stored the same way in `ExplanationImageKey` and `ExplanationImageAlt` (migration `AddItemExplanationImage`, additive) with `CK_Items_ExplanationImage_Alt`, so a key and its alt text exist together or not at all. A key looks like `curated:folder/name` (lowercase letters, digits and hyphens; at least one `/`; at most 100 characters); alt text is required, 1 to 300 characters, with no line breaks or control characters. An explanation picture may provide the whole explanation without prose. The explanation alt text may name the answer because the picture is only shown after the answer is revealed; the question alt text must not (AC 5.2.23). The key names a picture bundled with the web build (AC 3.21.1); the API stores only the reference. Duplicate identity is still (question text, question image key); the explanation image is not part of it. (backend tests: QuestionImageTests, QuestionImagePostgresTests) Uploaded pictures (#400, AC 3.25) are returned in the same `questionImage`/`explanationImage` shape with `key` `media:{assetId:N}`, optional `attribution` and `longDescription`, plus the item picture `revision` for current-context reporting; they exist only for items that pass the gate of AC 3.25.7 and are fetched through the delivery routes of AC 3.25.5, not resolved from a bundle.
- **AC 2.1.20** [Authenticated] Bulk and upload-to-collection accept missing/null pictures or strict same-owner uploaded `{assetId,altText,attribution?,longDescription?}` references (AC 3.27.4). Unsupported/foreign/unavailable media is rejected before Upload/item/collection mutation. Curated keys, URLs, storage keys and bytes remain unsupported. PUT omitted/null keeps a slot; `{remove:true}` clears an uploaded slot; a curated slot remains seed managed. Explanation prose may be empty with either kind of explanation picture. `starterWorld`/`wowLine` remain seed managed and ignored on ordinary PUT.
- **AC 2.1.21** [Authenticated] **Given** I create an item with `POST /items` or `POST /items/bulk`, or edit one with `PUT /items/{id}`, **then** duplicates are an existing item of the same creator in the destination category with the same question (trimmed, case-insensitive) or the same SimHash, **and** the same question picture identity (no picture equals no picture). PUT excludes itself and uses the original creator even for admin edits; authorization is checked first. A duplicate PUT or single POST returns 400 `Item.Duplicate` with the existing category-specific explanation, before changing the item, keywords, pictures, image-review revision/trail or audit; bulk skips duplicates as before. The edit form displays the duplicate explanation, retains entered values and permits correction and retry. All three paths compute SimHash and bucket from only `question.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()`, excluding answers and explanation. Picture identity is the curated key, `sha256:` plus the SHA-256 of uploaded normalized asset bytes (equal bytes match across asset ids), or none; PUT uses the effective retained/replaced/removed question picture. Explanation pictures never affect duplicates. Text-only copies of picture questions and questions with different picture identities are allowed; bulk accepts text-only items and existing uploads remain pictured. Seed sync never deduplicates. **Historical signatures (#466):** successful normal edits recompute that item's signature and bucket; no global backfill is performed. All three paths compare all creator/category peers using the shared .NET normalization, including Unicode boundary whitespace, so database trimming/casing and stale legacy buckets cannot hide exact-question duplicates. Fuzzy-only historical matches with different question strings can remain undetected until their signatures are corrected by ordinary edits.
- **AC 2.1.22** [Authenticated] **Given** I open `/items/{id}/edit` for an item that has a curated question picture, **then** the page shows the picture above the form as a read-only preview with the note "Picture comes from seed data; saving keeps it." (no inputs; AC 2.1.20). **Given** the item has an explanation picture (#403), **then** the page also shows it, labelled "Explanation picture (read-only)", with a note that saving keeps it and saving may leave the explanation prose empty; the Enlarge button works there. Items without pictures show nothing extra. (frontend test: EditItemPage.test.tsx)

### AC 2.2 Create hub and Study Guide entrypoint

**UI**

- **AC 2.2.1** [Anyone] **Given** I open `/create`, **then** I see one `h1` "Create" and a hub of options rather than a form; the page is public and uses the dark theme (AC 3.0.11). The primary card (`h2` "Turn notes into a quiz") has a static, non-interactive example question and a **Make a quiz** button to `/create/notes` (copy: "Upload a study guide or paste your notes. Quizymode makes quiz questions and saves them to a private collection you can edit."; the buttons are at least 44 px tall); the secondary card (`h2` "Write a question") has a **Write a question** button to `/create/question`. Below them are small text links (each keeping the page's `category`/`keywords` query) **Import questions from text or a file** (`/create/import`). Any query string on `/create` (for example `category`, `keywords`) is forwarded to the **Write a question** link. When signed in, the **Main navigation** shows one **Create** item (desktop and mobile) in place of the former **Add Items** and **Generate Quiz** items; it is highlighted on `/create*`, the legacy create routes (`/items/add`, `/items/create`, `/add-new-item`, `/items/bulk-create`, `/items/upload`) and `/study-guides/*`, but not on `/items` or `/items/:id`.
- **AC 2.2.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I am signed in and open `/create`, **then** a **Recent study guides** section (`h2`) lists up to my 3 most recently active study guides (from `GET /study-guides/documents`, newest `lastActivityUtc` first) with title, "N questions saved · Last activity <date>" and a **Continue** link (`aria-label` "Continue <title>") to `/study-guides/documents/{id}`, plus a **My study guides** link to `/study-guides/documents` (hidden when there are none). While loading a spinner is shown; if the request fails the message "Couldn't load your recent study guides." appears with a retry action and the cards above stay usable; with none, "No study guides yet. Make your first quiz above." is shown.
- **AC 2.2.3** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not signed in and open `/create`, **then** the same cards are shown with a status banner "Sign in to create quizzes and questions." with **Sign In** and **Sign Up** links; the recent study guides section is not shown and no study guide request is made. The sub-pages `/create/notes` (Make a quiz, AC 6.6.20), `/create/question` (single-item create form) and `/create/import` (Paste text / JSON import into a new collection, AC 2.6.5 and 2.6.9, opened on the Paste text tab) require sign-in (anonymous visitors go to sign in and return to the same path and query).
- **AC 2.2.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I am on `/create/question`, **then** a **Back to Create** link returns to `/create` keeping the current query string (`category`, `keywords`), and `category`/`keywords` from the URL pre-fill the form. The page title fallbacks are Create (`/create`), Turn notes into a quiz (`/create/notes`), Add Items (`/create/question`) and Import Questions (`/create/import`).
