### AC 3.8 Admin: categories and keywords (create, update, delete)

**API**

- **AC 3.8.1** [Admin] **Given** I am an admin, **when** I call create/update/delete endpoints for categories or keyword relations (e.g. CreateCategory, UpdateCategory, DeleteCategory, CreateCategoryKeyword/create relation, UpdateCategoryKeyword, DeleteCategoryKeyword), **then** the API allows the operation; categories remain public.
- **AC 3.8.2** [Authenticated (non-admin)] **Given** I am authenticated but not an admin, **when** I call create/update/delete for categories or category keywords, **then** the API returns 403 Forbidden.
- **AC 3.8.3** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not authenticated, **when** I call those endpoints, **then** the API returns 401.

**UI**

- **AC 3.8.4** [Admin] **Given** I am an admin, **when** I use the Admin Categories / Keywords UI, **then** I can create, update, and delete categories and keyword relations (parent/child per category, sort order, description); the UI reflects API rules.

---

### AC 3.9 Filters on the Categories page

On the Categories page (Sets view and List view), a **filter dialog** lets the user change the category/keyword scope and apply **scope filters** (item type, search, rating). Filters are reflected in the URL; applying them navigates and updates the **scope**. The dialog is opened by a **Filters** button and closes via **Show results** or the close (×) button. On all screen sizes it appears as a centered modal (not a bottom sheet).

**Filter dialog (open/close and active state)**

- **AC 3.9.1** [Anyone] **Given** I am on the Categories page in Sets or List view, **when** the page loads, **then** I see a **Filters** button in the control bar; clicking it opens the filter dialog as a centered modal. The dialog closes when I click **Show results**, press Escape, or click the backdrop. When I click **Show results** after applying changes, the dialog closes.
- **AC 3.9.2** [Anyone] **Given** I am on the Categories page, **when** any filter is in effect (e.g. I am not on "All categories", or I have path/query keywords, or I have scope filters such as item type, search, or rating), **then** the Filters button shows an **Active** indicator (badge). Inside the dialog a **Clear all** link resets all filter controls to the default or current-scope state.

**Keyword tag filters (Sets and List view)**

- **AC 3.9.2a** [Anyone] **Given** the filter dialog is open in **List** view, **then** I see a **Keywords** section at the top with a search input. As I type, a dropdown suggests up to **5** matching keyword names from the currently loaded items (in-memory match, no network call). Selecting a suggestion adds that keyword to the active query filters immediately (the URL updates); the input resets so I can add more keywords. Active keyword filters are shown as removable chips beneath the input. Pressing **Enter** in the input field adds the typed text as a keyword filter when it exactly matches a suggestion; if no suggestions exist, pressing Enter adds the free-text value as a filter.
- **AC 3.9.2b** [Anyone] **Given** the filter dialog is open in **Sets** view for a specific category, **then** I see a **Keywords** section at the top with a search input. As I type, a dropdown suggests up to **5** matching item-level tag keyword names fetched from the API for that category (cached per category, no call for "All categories"). Selecting a suggestion, or pressing **Enter** to submit free text, adds that keyword to `?keywords=` in the URL immediately while keeping the Sets view (`?view=sets`); the input resets so I can add more. Active keyword filters are shown as removable chips beneath the input; clicking a chip removes that keyword from the URL.

**Category and keyword filters (navigation scope)**

- **AC 3.9.3** [Anyone] **Given** the filter dialog is visible, **when** I use the filters, **then** I see: (1) a **Category** dropdown (e.g. "All categories" or a specific category), (2) an optional **Primary topic** (rank-1 keyword) control, and (3) an optional **Subtopic** (rank-2 keyword) control, disabled until a primary topic is selected. I can click **Apply** to navigate to the selected category/keyword scope; the URL path and optional query keywords are updated, page is reset to 1, and the dialog closes.
- **AC 3.9.4** [Anyone] **Given** I have selected "All categories" in the filter dialog and optionally one or more keywords, **when** I click Apply, **then** the scope becomes all categories with those keywords as query filters (e.g. slug `all` or `/categories` with `?keywords=...`); the path does not include category/keyword segments.

**Scope filters (item type, search, rating)**

- **AC 3.9.5** [Anyone] **Given** the filter dialog is visible, **when** I add **scope filters**, **then** I can add one or more of: **item type** (e.g. all / public / private), **search** (text), and **rating** (min, max, include unrated, only unrated). Each added scope filter is shown with a remove control. When I click **Apply**, the selected scope filters are reflected in the URL (e.g. `filterType`, `search`, `ratingMin`, `ratingMax`, `ratingUnrated`, `ratingOnlyUnrated`) and applied to the scope. Item type is sent to the items API where supported; search and rating may be applied client-side to the current result set where applicable.
- **AC 3.9.6** [Anyone] **Given** I am on the Categories List view with scope filters applied (e.g. search or rating), **when** the list is loaded, **then** the items shown are narrowed by those filters; pagination applies to the filtered set. Changing or clearing scope filters and applying updates the list accordingly.
- **AC 3.9.6a** [Anyone] **Given** I am paging through list-mode items in Categories or Collection detail, **when** the current page renders, **then** I see paging controls above the list and a **Previous / Next** pager below the list so I can continue navigating without scrolling back to the top.

**URL and scope**

- **AC 3.9.7** [Anyone] **Given** I have applied filters on the Categories page, **when** the URL is updated, **then** the category/keyword scope is encoded in the path and (for query keywords) in the `keywords` query parameter; scope filter parameters (e.g. `filterType`, `search`, `ratingMin`, `ratingMax`, `ratingUnrated`, `ratingOnlyUnrated`) are encoded in the query. Refreshing or sharing the URL restores the same filter state. When I navigate to a different category/keyword path (e.g. via breadcrumb or bucket), scope filter query parameters may be cleared so the new path has a clean scope unless the UI preserves them.

### AC 3.10 Taxonomy (YAML), public API, and item ingress

**Design:** The canonical category tree and navigation keyword slugs are defined in `docs/quizymode_taxonomy.yaml`. At API startup the file is loaded into an in-memory taxonomy registry. Database categories and `KeywordRelation` navigation rows are seeded from that same YAML (plus per-category **other** where applicable). Item create, update, bulk add, collection upload, and study-guide finalize must not introduce new public categories or ad-hoc public navigation paths.

**API**

- **AC 3.10.1** [Anyone] **Given** the API is running, **when** I call `GET /taxonomy` (no auth required), **then** the response lists all taxonomy categories with nested L1 → L2 navigation slugs (and optional flat slug lists) sourced only from the in-memory registry, not from ad-hoc DB growth.
- **AC 3.10.1a** [Anyone] **Given** I browse category navigation in the SPA, **when** taxonomy-backed pages load, **then** they use `GET /taxonomy` as the canonical tree of category to primary-topic (L1) to subtopic (L2) paths for valid `/categories/...` navigation scopes.
- **AC 3.10.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I create or update an item (single `POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, `POST /items/bulk` (admin only), or upload-to-collection), **when** I supply a category name, **then** it must match a taxonomy category **and** exist already as a **public** row in `Categories`; the API does **not** create new global categories as a side effect of item ingress.
- **AC 3.10.2a** [Admin] **Given** I call the repo-managed seed-sync endpoints (`POST /admin/seed-sync/preview` or `POST /admin/seed-sync/apply`), **when** an item payload supplies a category name, **then** it must match a taxonomy category **and** an existing public `Categories` row; seed sync does not create new public categories.
- **AC 3.10.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I supply `navigationKeyword1` and `navigationKeyword2` on item ingress, **when** the pair is not a valid (L1, L2) path for that category in the taxonomy, **then** the request fails validation; bulk, import, and admin seed-sync flows do **not** auto-create missing `KeywordRelation` rows for navigation.
- **AC 3.10.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I attach extra keywords beyond the two navigation slugs in the regular user-facing item ingress flows (`POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, `POST /items/bulk`, upload-to-collection, or study-guide finalize), **when** a keyword slug matches a taxonomy slug for that category, **then** it is attached as a **public** keyword without review pending; **when** it does not match taxonomy, **then** it is stored as a **private** user keyword with **review pending** (including for admins using those same non-seed-sync flows). Extras are matched on **exact** slug equality only (edit-distance similarity is not used).
- **AC 3.10.5** [Anyone] **Given** Discover item filters use `category` and `keywords` (L1/L2), **when** the keyword path is invalid for that category under the same taxonomy rules as items, **then** the API returns **400** (see also AC 1.9.1b).

**UI**

- **AC 3.10.6** [Authenticated] **Given** I use Create Item, Edit Item, or import flows that pick category and navigation, **when** the form loads, **then** the shared **Topic** group (AC 12.9) is driven from `GET /taxonomy` (fixed taxonomy categories): the searchable picker lists the taxonomy's category > L1 > L2 paths and the **Browse all topics** selects (**Category**, required **Primary topic** and **Subtopic**) are populated from that taxonomy for the selected category; I cannot type arbitrary navigation slugs for L1/L2. Every screen that reads `GET /taxonomy` caches it under the per-user key `["taxonomy", userId or "anon"]`, and sign-out removes it (AC 12.17). **Additional keywords** may suggest taxonomy slugs; free-text extras are treated as private pending on the server per AC 3.10.4.
- **AC 3.10.7** [Anyone] **Given** the taxonomy, **then** `tech` has the primary topic `genai-for-devs` ("AI for developers") with eight repo-managed sets of 14 items each, written for experienced developers new to AI: `llm-basics`, `tokens-context`, `embeddings`, `rag`, `prompts-tools`, `ai-agents`, `model-tuning` and `llm-ops` (plus the usual empty `general-genai-for-devs` bucket). Every item has an explanation and a `source` URL that supports it (`data/seed-source/items/tech/tech.genai-for-devs.*.json`). The slug deliberately avoids the bare token `ai`, so topic search for "artificial intelligence" still returns `tech › ai-ml` first (AC 11.1); `genai-for-devs` matches through its description and ranks after it. The sets reach production through seed sync, which applies the branch's taxonomy SQL before the items (AC 5.2).

### AC 3.11 Languages taxonomy and language learning item conventions

**Context:** The `languages` category is structured to support language learning experiences across multiple world languages. The taxonomy uses a two-tier keyword hierarchy; item-level keywords (additional keywords beyond the two navigation slugs) carry frequency and situational metadata.

**Taxonomy structure:**

- Each promoted language (English, Spanish, French, German, Italian, Russian, Japanese, Latvian, Ukrainian, Bulgarian, Latin, Romanian, Portuguese, Arabic, Chinese, Korean) has its own **rank-1 keyword** under the `languages` category.
- The `languages` category exposes only **language-name rank-1 keywords** plus one catch-all rank-1 bucket: `other-langs`.
- Each promoted language keeps language-specific rank-2 subtopics such as `core`, `phrases`, `vocab`, `grammar`, `idioms`, `conversation`, and `travel` where applicable. Some languages have additional subtopics (for example `hiragana`, `katakana`, `kanji` for Japanese; `expressions` for Latin; `reading`, `writing`, `pronunciation`, and `listening` for English; `characters` for Chinese; `hangul` for Korean).
- Less-developed or uncategorized language content stays under the `other-langs` rank-1 keyword and can be promoted later as content grows.
- Legacy non-language rank-1 buckets under `languages` (`esl`, `travel`, `vocab`, `grammar`, `idioms`, `general`, `mixed`) are not valid browse roots anymore. Existing rows were migrated to a language-specific path (usually `english/...`) or to `other-langs/mixed` (#78/#81); new content with those paths is rejected (**AC 3.11.4**).

**Rank-2 subtopic definitions:**

| Subtopic | Scope |
|---|---|
| `core` | Essential beginner content: numbers 1–20, greetings, yes/no, please/thank you, introductions, water/bread |
| `phrases` | Situational phrases: café/restaurant orders, directions, prices/shopping, hotel check-in, emergency |
| `vocab` | Thematic vocabulary lists |
| `grammar` | Grammar rules and patterns |
| `idioms` | Common idioms and expressions |
| `conversation` | Conversational practice dialogs and patterns |
| `expressions` (Latin only) | Famous Latin expressions with English translation and context |

**Item-level keyword conventions for language learning items:**

Items in the `languages` category should use item-level keywords (the `keywords` array in seed data / additional keywords in the UI) to carry:

- **Frequency tiers:** `freq-100` (top ~100 essential items per language), `freq-500`, `freq-1000` — used to filter curated vocabulary sets without constraining navigation paths to exact counts.
- **Situational tags:** `situation:cafe`, `situation:directions`, `situation:prices`, `situation:greetings`, `situation:intro`, `situation:hotel`, `situation:emergency` — allow cross-language situational filtering.
- **CEFR levels:** `cefr:a1`, `cefr:a2`, `cefr:b1`, `cefr:b2`, `cefr:c1`, `cefr:c2` — indicate difficulty level.

These item-level keywords are **not** navigation keywords. They are stored as public taxonomy-matched keywords when they match the taxonomy slug list, or as private pending keywords otherwise. They are available as item tag filters in Discover and the Categories keyword filter.

**ACs:**

- **AC 3.11.1** [Anyone] **Given** a user browses `/categories/languages/{language}/core`, **when** the scope loads, **then** it shows items whose `navigationKeyword1` = that language and `navigationKeyword2` = `core`; items tagged `freq-100` are retrievable by adding `?keywords=freq-100` as an item tag filter.
- **AC 3.11.2** [Anyone] **Given** a user browses `/categories/languages/latin/expressions`, **when** the scope loads, **then** it shows items in the famous Latin expressions set; each item's question is the Latin phrase and the correct answer is the English translation with context.
- **AC 3.11.3** [Anyone] **Given** `other-langs` rank-1 still exists as a catch-all, **when** items reference `navigationKeyword1 = "other-langs"`, **then** those items remain accessible at `/categories/languages/other-langs/mixed`; Arabic, Chinese, Korean, and Portuguese are now promoted to their own rank-1 groups and their `core` subtopic is accessible at `/categories/languages/{language}/core`.
- **AC 3.11.4** [Item author/Admin/System] **Given** a seed-sync item, `POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, `POST /items/bulk` (admin only), `POST /items/upload-to-collection`, or a study-guide accept uses a retired `languages` path (rank-1 `esl`, `general`, `grammar`, `vocab`, `idioms`, `travel` or `mixed`, or `other-langs/<promoted language>`), **when** it is processed, **then** the API rejects the request with a 400 invalid-navigation-path validation error and saves nothing: `Item.InvalidNavigationPath` for item, bulk and seed-sync ingest (and the study-guide accept path that creates items through bulk create), and the feature's own code for study-guide document creation (`StudyGuideDocument.InvalidNavigationPath`), because the path is not in the taxonomy. API startup and seed-sync apply no longer run a Languages data normalization; legacy rows were migrated once (#78, #81) and cannot be recreated (#326).

---

### AC 3.12 Item pronunciation and language-aware speech

**Context:** Items can carry optional per-field speech metadata (`pronunciation`, `languageCode`) to support native-script content and browser text-to-speech (TTS). This is designed for items whose question or answer _is_ a foreign-language word or phrase, not for items that already describe that word in English.

**ACs:**

- **AC 3.12.1** [Anyone] **Given** an item has speech metadata on question, correct answer, or an incorrect answer — each field carrying an optional `pronunciation` string and an optional BCP-47 `languageCode` — **when** the browser supports the Web Speech API, **then** the Speak button reads the field's text using a voice matching `languageCode`; if no matching voice is available and `pronunciation` is set, the `pronunciation` string is spoken instead; if neither applies, standard English TTS is used.
- **AC 3.12.2** [Anyone] **Given** an item field has a `pronunciation` value that differs from the displayed text (e.g. romaji under a kanji question), **when** the field is rendered in Explore card view, Quiz option, or Item Detail, **then** a muted hint line showing the pronunciation is displayed directly beneath the field text.
- **AC 3.12.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I use Create Item or Edit Item, **when** the form loads, **then** optional pronunciation and language-code inputs are available for question, correct answer, and each incorrect answer; leaving them blank persists no speech metadata.
- **AC 3.12.4** [Admin/Seed] **Given** a repo-managed seed JSON file includes `questionSpeech`, `correctAnswerSpeech`, or `incorrectAnswerSpeech` fields, **when** seed-sync apply runs, **then** those fields are normalised (trimmed, truncated to limits) and persisted; a seed file that omits these fields does not clear existing speech metadata on a re-sync.
- **AC 3.12.5** [Design intent] Speech metadata should be set only when the question or answer **is** a native-script word or phrase (e.g. `"黒い"`). Items whose question is already a full English sentence wrapping a foreign word (e.g. `"What does '黒い' mean?"`) should omit `languageCode` — the TTS already reads the sentence correctly as English — and may omit `pronunciation` too.
- **AC 3.12.6** [Anyone] **Given** a text field (question, answer, explanation) contains inline foreign-phrase markup in the format `{{lang|native|translit|pronunciation}}`, **when** the text is rendered in any item view (Explore, Quiz, Item List, Item Detail), **then**: (a) the native script is displayed inline where the markup appears; (b) if `translit` or `pronunciation` is non-empty, a muted hint line is rendered beneath showing `translit · pronunciation` (or whichever parts are present); (c) the surrounding English text is shown as-is; (d) the Speak button reads each segment in its correct language — English segments with the default voice and foreign segments with the voice matching `lang` (BCP-47); (e) text without any markup is rendered and spoken identically to before this feature. The format is: `lang` = BCP-47 tag (e.g. `ja-JP`, `ru-RU`), `native` = text displayed and spoken in target language, `translit` = romanization (optional, shown in hint), `pronunciation` = English-speaker-friendly guide with stressed syllable in CAPS (optional, shown in hint after `·`).
- **AC 3.12.7** [Authenticated] **Given** I use Create/Edit Item and leave an incorrect-answer field blank (empty or whitespace), **when** I submit, **then** the blank field is dropped and the remaining incorrect answers' pronunciation/language are sent under each answer's new position (e.g. A, blank, C with speech on C sends `incorrectAnswers` `["A","C"]` and `incorrectAnswerSpeech` `{1: ...}`); speech never attaches to another answer and the request is never rejected for an out-of-range key; speech entered under a blank answer is discarded (#269).

### AC 3.13 Daily quiz (DAILY-001, #119)

**API**

- **AC 3.13.1** [Anyone] **Given** I call `GET /api/daily-quiz`, **then** I receive today's UTC date (`yyyy-MM-dd`) and one global set of at most 5 public items, identical for every caller that day. The set is chosen the first time the day is read and then stored with a header row, so concurrent first reads produce one set and a day with nothing eligible stays empty without being recomputed (it is not refilled later, even if content becomes eligible that day). Signed-in callers also receive their completion for the day (or null) and their streak; anonymous callers receive null for both.
- **AC 3.13.2** [System] **Given** a day's set is created, **then** eligible items are public, in a public category, have at least one incorrect answer, are not flagged **check answer key** by the question quality report (ANALYTICS-009, default 30-day window), and are not part of any other stored daily set within 30 days before or after that date; eligible items are ordered by the first 8 bytes of SHA-256(`"yyyy-MM-dd:" + itemId`) ascending (ties by item id) and the first 5 are taken. The day boundary is UTC midnight. "Has at least one incorrect answer" means at least one **playable** distractor per **AC 3.6.17** (a blank-only or correct-answer-equivalent list is not eligible), so the client total always equals the stored set size (#250).
- **AC 3.13.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /api/daily-quiz/completions` with `{ date, correct, total, skipped }` (`skipped` is optional and defaults to 0; #403), **when** the date is today or yesterday (UTC, so a run that crosses midnight still counts), that day's set has items, `total` equals its number of active items, `0 <= skipped <= total` and `correct <= total - skipped`, **then** my first completion for that date is stored (201) and later calls return the stored one unchanged (200, `created=false`); both return my streak and the completion (`correct`, `total`, `skipped`, `completedAt`). `skipped` counts questions skipped because their picture was unavailable (AC 3.21.10); `total` stays the whole set. It is stored in `DailyQuizCompletions.Skipped` (`int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`, migration `AddItemExplanationImage`) with the check constraint `CK_DailyQuizCompletions_Skipped` (`Skipped >= 0 AND Correct + Skipped <= Total`); `CK_DailyQuizCompletions_Counts` is unchanged. Otherwise the API returns 400 and stores nothing; anonymous callers get 401. A run where every question was skipped still posts `{ correct: 0, total, skipped: total }`, counts for the streak (the learner showed up and the failure was not theirs) and shows "No questions were scored"; no study session is saved for it. The done card shows `correct / (total - skipped)` plus "N questions skipped because the picture was unavailable. Not scored."; guests keep `skipped` in their local completion record. (backend tests: DailyQuizTests, LearningLoopHttpTests; frontend tests: DailyQuizPage.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.13.4** [Any user] **Given** completion dates, **then** the streak is the number of consecutive UTC days with a completion ending today, or ending yesterday when today is not completed yet. One completed daily quiz is enough for a streak day.
- **AC 3.13.5** [Admin] **Given** I call `GET /api/admin/daily-quiz?date=yyyy-MM-dd` (today to 7 days ahead, default today; the set is created if missing) or `POST /api/admin/daily-quiz/{date}/items/{itemId}/exclude`, **then** I see the day's active and removed items, each with its `questionImage` (question picture only; `null` when none or the item no longer exists, AC 2.1.19), and removing an active item marks it excluded for that date and fills its slot with the next eligible item in seed order; an item not active in that day's set returns 404, a simultaneous change to the same day by another admin 409, a date outside the window 400, and non-admins 403. Completions already recorded for that date keep their streak credit.

**UI**

- **AC 3.13.6** [Anyone] **Given** I open `/daily`, **then** I can answer today's questions with the same answer locking, results, and **Review mistakes** behaviour as a quiz run (AC 3.6.8-3.6.11), without per-item URLs. The first completed run of the day counts: signed in, the SPA posts the completion (AC 3.13.3) and, when it was newly stored, saves the run as a study session with no source so its misses join my review queue; anonymous, the score is stored by date in `localStorage` `quizymode.daily.v1` (version 1, at most 60 dates, first score per date wins, corrupt data ignored, separate from `quizymode.guest.v1` so **Clear guest history** keeps its meaning) and the page notes that the streak is saved in this browser only. Anonymous daily runs are not added to guest history.
- **AC 3.13.7** [Anyone] **Given** today's daily quiz is already counted for me, **when** I open `/daily`, **then** I see **Done for today** with my score and a **Practice again** action; practice runs never change the stored score or streak. Loading, empty ("There is no daily quiz today"), save-failure, and load-error (with **Try again**) states are shown.
- **AC 3.13.8** [Admin] **Given** I open `/admin/daily-quiz` (linked from the admin dashboard), **then** I can pick a UTC day from today to 7 days ahead, see its questions with category and a link to the item, and **Remove** one; the list then shows the replacement and a **Removed for this day** list. **Given** a question has a picture, **then** active rows show a small thumbnail before the question, removed rows an extra-small one, and the Remove button's accessible name becomes `Remove from daily set: {question} (picture: {alt text})` (#403). (frontend test: AdminDailyQuizPage.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.13.9** [Anyone] **Given** I finish the daily quiz (counted or practice run), **when** I share, **then** the text is line 1 `Quizymode Daily {date} {n}/{t}`, line 2 one U+1F7E9 (correct) or U+1F7E5 (incorrect) per question in roster order with no separators, and line 3 `{origin}/daily?utm_source=share&utm_medium=share&utm_campaign=quiz_result&utm_content=daily`. No question text is included, and the QR code is available (**AC 3.19**).

### AC 3.14 Review queue (REVIEW-001, #117)

**API**

- **AC 3.14.1** [Authenticated] **Given** a newly saved live quiz run (AC 3.6.14) has missed items that exist, **then** each is (re)scheduled in my review queue at the first interval, due one day after the run was saved (server time), including items already queued at a later interval. Items that are not quiz-playable per **AC 3.6.17** are skipped and never queued. The queue is private to me, keyed by item, and saved after the study session so a queue conflict never loses the session.
- **AC 3.14.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `GET /api/review-queue`, **then** I receive at most 20 due items (due time at or before now), oldest due first, plus my due count, total count, and the next future due time. Queued items that were deleted or are private to someone else are removed from my queue silently; items changed since they were queued (their `UpdatedAt`, which admin moderation or recategorization also sets) restart at the first interval and are due the next day, like a fresh miss. `GET /api/review-queue/summary` returns only the counts and next due time for available items. Queued items that are not quiz-playable per **AC 3.6.17** are removed silently and are never served or counted in the due or total counts (#250). They are also never enqueued (**AC 3.14.1**, **AC 3.14.5**, #251).
- **AC 3.14.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /api/review-queue/{itemId}/answers` with `{ isCorrect }` for a due item, **then** a correct answer advances it one interval (1, 3, 7, 14, 30 days) and a correct answer at 30 days removes it (`graduated=true`); an incorrect answer resets it to 1 day. An entry that is not due yet is left unchanged (`applied=false`), so repeated submits are safe; an item not in my queue returns 404; anonymous callers get 401. Correct answers in ordinary quizzes do not change the queue.

**UI**

- **AC 3.14.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I open `/review` (sign-in required), **then** the current due items load fresh on every visit and stay fixed while I answer (no refetch on window focus); each first answer is saved as I choose it, and a live summary shows how many moved to a longer interval, came back for tomorrow, or are done. When more items are due than shown, the page says so. A failed save is reported and the item stays due. With nothing due, the page shows how many are scheduled and when the next is due (or how the queue fills), with links to the daily quiz and categories. Review runs report quiz analytics with context type `review` and are not saved as study sessions.

**Flashcard self-grading (#251)**

- **AC 3.14.5** [Authenticated] `POST /api/review-queue/{itemId}` with no body, on an item I can see outside any collection (public, or my own private item) that is quiz-playable per **AC 3.6.17**:
  - It queues the item at the first interval, due one day after the call (server time). This also applies when the item is already queued at a later interval, which resets it like a quiz miss (**AC 3.14.1**).
  - Repeat calls keep exactly one entry.
  - It returns 200 with `itemId`, `intervalDays=1` and `dueAt` (UTC).
  - A missing item, a private item owned by someone else (even one visible through a shared collection) and an item that is not quiz-playable all return 404 with the same body and create no entry.
  - Anonymous callers get 401.
  - More than 300 calls by the same user in 10 minutes returns 429 with the message "Too many Review queue updates. Please slow down."; the limit is per user.
- **AC 3.14.6** [Anyone] In Flashcards (all scopes), after I reveal a card, a group labelled "How well did you know this?" shows **Got it** then **Still learning**. It is not nested in the flip buttons and is hidden on the front face.
  - The chosen grade has `aria-pressed=true` and a check icon. Choosing the other grade replaces it, and choosing the pressed one does nothing.
  - The shown grade follows the card id.
  - Grading never advances the card, and Next works without grading.
  - **Got it** never calls the review-queue API and never removes or promotes a queue entry, including after Still learning.
- **AC 3.14.7** [Authenticated] **Still learning** adds the card to the session pile at once. If this item has not already been enqueued during this page visit, it calls **AC 3.14.5**. Status messages appear in a persistent `role="status"` line:
  - Success shows "Added to Review", which clears after 3 s or on card change.
  - A network, 5xx or 429 failure shows "Couldn't add to Review. It stays in this session's pile." with **Retry**.
  - A 404 shows "This card can't be added to Review" without Retry.
  - The card stays in the pile in every case.
- **AC 3.14.8** [Anyone] On the last card, Next becomes **Finish**, which is always enabled, and the right arrow key acts as Finish there. Finish shows a summary with a focused heading:
  - The heading is "Deck complete", or "Round complete" in a loop.
  - It shows "{g} got it · {s} still learning · {n} not graded"; counts are per round.
  - **Study N still-learning cards** (singular for 1) runs only those cards, in deck order, with fresh grades: cards left ungraded in a loop round are not carried over. The loop shows "1 of N", has a "Still learning" round label, and uses per-item URLs that start at the first loop card. Loops repeat until none remain, which then shows "All caught up. Nice work."
  - **Restart deck** always starts a new run: new run id, pile cleared, first card.
  - The pile and grades are per run, are not persisted, and are cleared by a reload, by leaving the page or by changing the deck's scope.
  - The stored navigation context, comment navigation, reveal and grade positions, and the switch to Quiz mode always use the full deck, never the loop subset.
- **AC 3.14.9** [Anonymous] Grading works with the in-session pile only and makes no review-queue API call. When at least one card is still learning, the summary shows one `SignupPrompt` with the reason `review-later` offering to keep these cards in a Review queue after creating an account. Nothing is written to guest storage or imported on sign-in.
- **AC 3.14.10** [Anyone] Collection-scope Flashcards show a **Shuffle** switch (`role=switch`, default off). It is saved in `localStorage` `quizymode.flashcards.shuffle.v1`, and if storage fails it works for the session only.
  - With shuffle on, the deck is shuffled once per run, before the first URL sync. The first URL is the shuffled first card.
  - A card in the URL at load (deep link or reload) stays first, and the rest are shuffled.
  - Moving between cards never reshuffles, and a refetch that drops a card keeps the remaining cards in the same order.
  - Toggling starts a new run with the current card first: index 0, pile and grades cleared, new run id. If any card in the run is graded, a confirm dialog appears first, and Cancel changes nothing.
  - Category and whole-bank scopes show no Shuffle switch.
- **AC 3.14.11** [Authenticated] **Given** I finish a review-queue run, **then** the results view shows no **Share result** button and no placeholder (**AC 3.19**).
- **AC 3.14.12** [Authenticated] **Given** the review queue shows 5 or more due questions (`dueCount`) and I have not answered any yet, **then** a card above the quiz reads "{n} due. Ready for a boss fight?" with a **Boss fight** button (the normal quiz stays below). **When** I press it, **then** the same quiz continues in boss mode: the card is replaced by a "Boss fight" heading (focused) with "{hp} / {max} HP", a bar (`role=meter`, "Boss HP 12 of 20") and, when more are due than served, "{dueCount} due, fighting {n}" instead of "Showing n of m due questions". Max HP is the number of questions served (at most 20). A correct first answer is a hit (HP minus 1, a shake unless reduced motion, "Hit!" for about 0.8 s); a wrong one is a "Missed" with no HP change. A polite live region announces "Hit! Boss HP 11 of 20." or "Missed. Boss HP 12 of 20.". Answers are saved exactly as in the normal review (AC 3.14.3: once per item, same arguments, same tally and failure message) and boss mode adds no analytics events. HP and the outcome follow the first quiz run only; Play again later in the results does not change them. When every question has an answer the header is replaced by "Boss defeated!" (HP 0) or "The boss escaped with n HP" (announced in the live region; focus does not move) while the quiz and its results stay available below. After the pending saves finish, the page reads the queue summary once and adds a line: 5 or more due "Another boss is ready now." with a **Boss fight** button; 1 to 4 due "{n} still due."; none due "Next boss arrives {date}." from the next due time, or "No boss scheduled."; "Checking..." while loading and no line if the summary fails. **Browse categories** and **Play the daily quiz** links are always offered. The outcome **Boss fight** button reloads the queue and starts a fresh fight with full HP and a clean tally. (frontend tests: useReviewAnswers.test.tsx, ReviewQueuePage)

### AC 3.15 Topic interests (PERSONAL-001, #116)

- **AC 3.15.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `GET /api/users/me/interests`, **then** `categories` contains my ordered public topic paths `category[/l1[/l2]]` (up to 10). Category-only paths remain supported. Each refinement references navigation keyword ids and must still have public parent/child relations; deleted, private or unavailable categories or refinements are omitted, never widened to a parent. **Given** I `PUT` `{ categories }`, **then** the same bounded path syntax is accepted (category slug up to 64 characters, each navigation segment alphanumeric/hyphen up to 30); duplicates, parent/child overlaps, unknown or unavailable paths or more than 10 return 400 without changing existing selections. An empty list clears interests. Anonymous callers receive 401. The database uses a surrogate id and a unique `(UserId, CategoryId, NavigationKeywordId1, NavigationKeywordId2)` index with NULLS NOT DISTINCT. The migration preserves legacy category rows; rollback preserves those rows and refuses to discard any refined selections (#383).
- **AC 3.15.2** [Anyone] **Given** I call `GET /api/home/popular-categories`, **then** I receive up to 6 public category slugs ranked by completed category quiz runs (Learn runs are not counted, AC 3.17.11) in the last 30 days (then runs started), from the learning report aggregation with bots and internal traffic excluded; only current public categories are returned, never counts, and the result is cached for an hour.
- **AC 3.15.3** [Anyone] **Given** I open the **My topics** editor (from Home, the Games hub or my profile, AC 3.15.5), **then** it is a labelled modal dialog (a bottom sheet on phones) with the helper "Pick up to 10. Your picks come first on Home." and a live "{n} of 10" counter, listing the bundled home categories as toggle chips (at least 44px tall, selected ones marked with a check; at most 10 selectable; the rest disable at the limit with an explanation), closing with **Cancel**, the backdrop, or Escape. Keyboard focus is trapped inside (Tab and Shift+Tab wrap, including across the loading-to-chips swap), starts on the first enabled chip (**Cancel** while loading, **Retry** when the load failed), and returns to the opener on close (to the nav account/menu trigger when the opener is gone, never the page body). **Save topics** stores the list with the API when I am signed in, or in `localStorage` `quizymode.interests.v2` (version 2; legacy v1 category-only choices remain readable until the next successful save) when anonymous; a failed save keeps the dialog open with an error. **Given** I am signed in and my saved topics are still loading, **then** the dialog shows a loading placeholder and **Save topics** is disabled; once they load the chips are prefilled with them. **Given** they failed to load, **then** the dialog shows **Your topics could not be loaded.** with **Retry** (which reloads them and then prefills the chips) instead of the chips, and **Save topics** stays disabled, so a failed load can never replace my saved topics (#380).
- **AC 3.15.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I sign in with browser-local topic choices, **when** the once-per-page-session guest claim runs (AC 3.6.16), **then** valid paths in the current public taxonomy are appended after my account choices, capped at 10. Account choices win on duplicates or overlapping parent/child paths, so claiming a broad guest category never broadens a saved account refinement. The merged list is cached immediately after the server accepts and only then is the local copy cleared; a failure keeps it for a later retry. No message is shown (#383).
- **AC 3.15.5** [Anyone] **Given** I want to change my topics, **then** the **Change topics** entry points are: the **For you** lane on Home, the **My topics** bar on the unscoped Games hub (AC 3.20.4), the **My topics** row in the signed-in profile modal (AC 4.5.4), and **Choose topics** on the onboarding card (AC 3.0.25). All open the one shared editor (AC 3.15.3), mounted once by the site layout, and never stack two dialogs: the profile modal closes first. A failed topics load shows an error with **Retry** rather than silently looking empty. (frontend tests: TopicPicker, UserProfileModal, GamesHubPage, useInterests) **Refine a topic** progressively reveals the current taxonomy category, optional L1 and optional L2 selects, with 44 px controls. Adding a path explicitly replaces overlapping selections. Refined selections display their complete breadcrumb and a remove action; their Home, profile and Games labels and links retain that path. A failed taxonomy load offers Retry refinements without removing saved choices or preventing category-only editing (#383).
- **AC 3.15.6** [Anyone] **Given** my completed category runs (server sessions when signed in, this browser's guest history otherwise) include at least 2 in the last 30 days rolled up to the same L1 topic (`category` or `category/l1`; collection, global, Daily and Review runs are not attributable and are ignored), my topics are loaded with fewer than 10 chosen, and that topic does not overlap one of my chosen topics, **then** the Home **For you** lane shows one suggestion card "Because you played {topic}, add it to My topics?" for the top candidate (ranked by run count, then most recent run, then id; the top 3 qualify, one card is shown at a time). **Add topic** saves it to my topics through the normal save (a failure shows "Could not save. Try again."); **Not interested** hides it and remembers that topic in `localStorage` `quizymode.topicSuppressions.v1` on this device. Inference never adds or broadens my topics by itself. When signed in, the candidates come from the server aggregate `GET /users/me/topic-activity` (the same rule and ordering, with my chosen and suppressed topics removed on the client). At most **one new card per UTC day** per device (`localStorage` `quizymode.topicSuggestionCard.v1`); a card already on screen stays until I add it or choose Not interested, and a dismissed card does not let a second new card appear the same day. Not yet built from #385: game runs in the server aggregate (AC 3.15.7), the cross-device suppression table and reset (suppressions are still per device), and the privacy-policy bullet. (frontend tests: topicSuggestions, topicSuggestionCard, PersonalizedLanes)
- **AC 3.15.7** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `GET /api/users/me/topic-activity`, **then** I receive `{ windowDays: 30, topics: [{ path, runs, lastCompletedAt }] }` built only from my own saved study sessions (Quiz and Learn) completed in the last 30 days whose source is a category, rolled up to `category` or `category/l1` (collection, global, Daily and Review runs are ignored), keeping topics with at least 2 runs, ordered by runs, then most recent run, then path, at most 10. Other users' runs never appear; anonymous callers receive 401. Game runs are not included yet and the SPA does not call it yet (it still computes from `GET /study-sessions`). (backend tests: GetTopicActivityTests)

### AC 3.16 Study keyboard shortcuts (#252)

- **AC 3.16.1** [Anyone] In Quiz, Daily quiz and Review, before the current item is answered, pressing 1-4 or A-D selects that option, with the same scoring, locking, analytics and feedback as a click.
  - Digits on the top row or numpad work with or without Shift, so AZERTY users can use them.
  - A key for an option that does not exist does nothing. Answer keys do nothing once the item is answered, so it is never scored twice.
- **AC 3.16.2** [Anyone] In Quiz, Daily quiz and Review:
  - Left/Right arrows act exactly like the visible Previous and Next buttons and are inert when those buttons are disabled.
  - After answering, Enter opens **View results** if every item is answered, otherwise it acts as **Next** when Next is enabled. The right arrow never opens results.
  - In Daily quiz and Review, focus still moves to the "Question X of N" heading after navigation.
  - On the results, mistake-review and summary views no answer, arrow or Enter shortcut is active.
  - In a started Test (AC 3.18) 1-4 / A-D select and re-select (the last answer counts), Enter and the right arrow only ever mean Next, F toggles the review flag, and no key opens or confirms Submit. Nothing is active on the Test setup card.
- **AC 3.16.3** [Anyone] In Flashcards, Space or Enter flips the card in both directions. Flipping to the answer fires the same reveal as a click.
  - Left/Right arrows go to the previous and next card when those buttons are enabled; on the last card the right arrow is **Finish** (**AC 3.14.8**).
  - Handled keys call preventDefault, so Space does not scroll the page.
- **AC 3.16.4** [Anyone] In Flashcards, after the answer is shown and the Got it / Still learning buttons are visible, 1 = Got it and 2 = Still learning, through the same handlers as the buttons. On the end-of-deck, results and mistake-review screens, only `?` is active.
- **AC 3.16.5** [Anyone] A shortcut is ignored when:
  - focus is in an input, textarea, select, contentEditable, `role=textbox` or `role=combobox`
  - IME composition is in progress (`isComposing` or keyCode 229)
  - the key is an auto-repeat
  - the event was already `defaultPrevented`
  - Ctrl, Alt or Meta is held
  - any `role=dialog` or `aria-modal` element is mounted (comments drawer, collections modal, profile modal, feedback dialog, collection details, share dialog, the shortcuts dialog)
  - Space or Enter is pressed while an **enabled** button, link, `role=button`, `role=switch` or summary has focus. That control activates exactly once.
  - Space or Enter is pressed on a **disabled** focused control: this does not block the shortcut.
  - An arrow key is pressed while a slider, range, listbox, radiogroup or tablist has focus.
- **AC 3.16.6** [Anyone] Every active flashcard and quiz-question screen (StudyShell header and the Daily quiz / Review header) shows a **Keyboard shortcuts** button. It is icon-only below `sm` and labelled "Shortcuts" from `sm` up, has `aria-label="Keyboard shortcuts"`, and is shown on all pointer types.
  - The button, or `?` on any study screen including results and end-of-deck, opens a modal dialog (`role=dialog`, `aria-modal`, labelled by its title).
  - The dialog lists only the bindings active on the current screen. When none apply it shows "No other shortcuts on this screen."
  - Initial focus is on the **Single-key shortcuts** switch, and Tab and Shift+Tab cycle within the dialog.
  - Escape, Close or a backdrop click closes it, and focus returns to the element that had focus when it opened.
- **AC 3.16.7** [Anyone] The **Single-key shortcuts** switch (`role=switch`, `aria-checked`) is on by default.
  - It persists in localStorage `quizymode.study-hotkeys.v1` (values on/off), survives a reload, and syncs across tabs.
  - When it is off, no single-key shortcut fires, including `?`, but the Keyboard shortcuts button still opens the dialog.
  - If storage is unavailable, the switch still works for the current tab, and a status note says the setting could not be saved.
- **AC 3.16.8** [Anyone] The ItemCollectionsModal, UserProfileModal and the "Collection details" overlays on the Flashcards and Quiz pages expose `role=dialog` and `aria-modal=true` with an accessible name. Quiz option buttons expose `aria-keyshortcuts` (for example "1 A").

### AC 3.17 Learn mode (#254)

- **AC 3.17.1** [Anyone] /learn/collections/:collectionId/:slug? and /learn/:category? (with keywords as on Quiz) load the playable multiple-choice items in scope (#250 predicate). A collection uses its full playable roster, shuffled by run, capped at 50, with the caption "Learning 50 of N" when capped. Category/global scopes use "Items to learn" (1-50, default 10), and M is the playable count actually returned. Zero playable items shows "Nothing to learn here yet" with **Open flashcards** and no run. A load error shows a role=alert with **Try again**. When Make a quiz (AC 6.6.31) opens Learn after some sections could not be made, the navigation state carries a one-line `role="status"` notice ("2 of 3 sections were made. 1 section couldn't be made and wasn't counted against your limit. Retry from your study guide.") with an **Open study guide** link and a **Dismiss** button; it is captured once and removed from the history state, so a refresh does not show it again. (page test)
- **AC 3.17.2** A round holds min(7, unmastered) items: attempted-but-unmastered items first (oldest attempt first), then unseen items in roster order. With 2 or more items, a round never starts with the item that ended the previous one. 1-item rounds work. (reducer: 1, 2, 3, 7, 8 and 50 items)
- **AC 3.17.3** A correct first-ever attempt masters an item. A miss resets the streak, and then 2 consecutive correct answers (one per round) master it. Every attempted unmastered item appears in every later round, and there are never more than 7. The run finishes when all M are mastered. (reducer)
- **AC 3.17.4** answer is accepted only for the current item, once per round. Continue/Enter does nothing until the current item has been answered. next-round works only from the recap. The final mastering answer shows feedback, and Continue goes to the finish screen (no final recap). (reducer and page test with the Enter hotkey)
- **AC 3.17.5** Options are seeded by runId and attempt number: stable on reload, re-seeded on each re-ask (2-option items may repeat their order). Quiz and FixedSetQuiz option order is unchanged (golden test). (unit)
- **AC 3.17.6** The "Mastered N of M" role=progressbar (aria-valuenow N, aria-valuemax M) updates on each mastering answer. Feedback is announced through aria-live=polite with the remaining correct answers needed. The recap shows "Round R complete", the round's correct count, and a per-item text status. The finish screen shows "You've mastered all M items" (singular for 1), "First try: C / M" with a percentage, **Learn again** (new runId) and the browse link. (page test)
- **AC 3.17.7** Progress is kept in localStorage quizymode.learn.v1 (version 1): at most 3 runs, LRU, 7-day TTL, 256 KB cap (evict the oldest run before a write fails). Restore needs the same schema, identity, normalized scope, and valid progress (keys within itemIds, well-formed attempts, mastered/streak consistent with attempts, finished only when all items are mastered, completionEmitted only with completedAt). A collection run restores if every stored item is still in the fetched playable roster. Otherwise, or on corrupt data, it starts fresh with the amber notice. Expired and other-identity runs start silently. On a storage failure, play continues in memory and the status line is shown. (storage unit test and page test)
- **AC 3.17.8** [Privacy] Question/answer text is snapshotted only for guest category/global runs. Signed-in runs store IDs and progress (attempts keep the selected option index, never answer text) and refetch items on restore; any inaccessible item restarts the run with the notice. Entries of other identities are purged on sign-out (before the Cognito/Google redirect) and on settled app load. This amends AC 3.6.12 for Learn. (unit test, including the Google logout path)
- **AC 3.17.9** [Authenticated] A run started and finished under the same identity sends exactly one POST /api/study-sessions: runId, completedAt, total = M, correct = first-try correct, missedItemIds = first-try misses, and the source as in AC 3.6.14. Misses enter the review queue (AC 3.14.1). Restore, remount and retry never create a second session (idempotent by runId). A failed save shows "We couldn't save this session." with **Try again**, which uses the same runId. Leaving early saves and queues nothing. Learn does not dispatch quizymode:quiz-completed. (page test asserting a single call and its payload; backend test of the enqueue for a 50-item payload)
- **AC 3.17.10** [Anonymous] A finished guest run is upserted into quizymode.guest.v1 per AC 3.6.13. Both the guest entry and the study-session payload (AC 3.6.14) carry `mode: "learn"` for a Learn run, and Quiz runs carry `quiz`. Continue (3.0.21), "Your last quiz" and Practice again (3.0.15, 3.0.16) open Learn (`/learn/...`) for Learn runs and Quiz otherwise; attempted/inferred For-you categories (3.0.22) count both modes. (tests: guest storage v1 read as quiz, Learn page sends mode learn, home surfaces link to /learn, backend persists and validates mode)
- **AC 3.17.11** Analytics: quick_quiz_started, question_answered (first attempt per item only, position = roster index + 1) and quiz_completed (score = first-try correct, total = M) use context type learn, with id collection:GUID or category:NAME when it matches the pattern, and none for global scope. The API accepts learn (amend 5.4.2). The learning report shows these runs as mode learn and keeps them out of quiz drop-off and abandonment. Popular categories (3.15.2) still count Quiz category runs only. (backend tests: TrackAnalyticsEvents accepts learn; GetLearningReport separates the modes; ComputePopularCategories ignores learn)
- **AC 3.17.12** Mode switchers show **Learn** after Quiz on category (Sets, List, Flashcards, Quiz, Learn), leaf and collection scopes (amend 3.6.3 and 3.6.3a); **Games** follows Learn (AC 3.20.1). The intro copy distinguishes Quiz (one pass), Learn (repeat until mastered) and Review (spaced, due items). At 360/375 px the strip scrolls, the page is not wider than the viewport, the active tab is scrolled into view, and an edge cue shows when tabs overflow. (categoryScopeMode test; Playwright at 375 px) The introductory copy states **Learn, Test and Review are free.** (#362).
- **AC 3.17.13** #252 hotkeys work in Learn: 1-4 / A-D answer, Enter continues (and respects the guard). They are off when shortcuts are disabled, and buttons expose aria-keyshortcuts. Focus moves to the question heading, the recap h2 or the finish h2 on each view change. (page test)

### AC 3.18 Test option on Quiz: exam simulation (#255)

Test is a mode of Quiz, not a separate page. It is an honour-system simulation: the answers are present in the API payload and in `sessionStorage`; the requirement is only that the UI never renders them before submit.

- **AC 3.18.1** [Anyone] **Given** I am on Quiz for a collection, category or global scope, **when** I choose **Test** in the *Quiz mode* radiogroup or open a URL with `?mode=test`, **then** Test opens.
  - **Practice** removes `mode` from the URL.
  - `mode=test` is kept across Next, Prev, palette jumps, the initial item-URL sync and quiz-size changes. It is never carried onto Explore, List or Sets URLs.
  - Switching mode with at least one answer opens a confirmation (**Keep current run** / **Switch and restart**); confirming starts a new run ID. With no answers, the switch happens silently.
  - Daily and Review have no Test option.
- **AC 3.18.2** [Anyone] **Given** Test is selected, **then** a setup card shows the question count, the **Time limit** choices (No limit by default / 1 min per question / 2 min per question / Custom 1-600 minutes) and **Start test**. The introductory copy states **Learn, Test and Review are free.** (#362).
  - The per-question presets are capped at 600 minutes.
  - A custom value outside 1-600 shows "Enter 1 to 600 minutes." and blocks Start.
  - No question, palette or timer appears before **Start test**, and no `quick_quiz_started` is sent.
  - The roster uses the #250 playable multiple-choice filter (**AC 3.6.17**), capped at the first 500 roster items ("Your test will use 500 of N"). An empty roster shows "No multiple-choice questions are available for Test here." with no run.
- **AC 3.18.3** [Anyone] **Given** a started, unsubmitted Test, **then** none of the following appear:
  - correctness styling or the correct-answer panel
  - the explanation or the "Score:" line
  - the item-details link, ratings, comments or collection controls
  - option pronunciation hints or "Read answer aloud"
  - the List/Explore/Sets switch (it is hidden while the Test runs)

  The selected option is marked with `aria-pressed="true"` and neutral styling. Choosing a different option replaces my answer, and the last answer before submit is scored. **Quiz size** is disabled.
- **AC 3.18.4** [Anyone] **Given** a started Test, **then:**
  - **Flag for review** (`aria-pressed`, and the F key when single-key shortcuts are on) toggles a flag on the current question.
  - The palette lists every question as a button named like "Question 7, answered, flagged, current", with icon and text cues as well as colour.
  - It shows answered, unanswered and flagged counts.
  - Activating an entry opens that question. On narrow screens the palette opens as a modal sheet; Escape closes it and focus returns to its toggle.
- **AC 3.18.5** [Anyone] **Given** a started Test, **when** I choose **Submit test**, **then** a modal dialog opens. It shows "N unanswered and M flagged" (or "All N questions are answered."), with focus on **Return to test**.
  - **Return to test** and Escape close it without changing anything.
  - **Show unanswered** / **Show flagged** jump to the first such question.
  - **Submit test** scores unanswered questions as wrong and moves focus to the **Test complete** heading.
  - No keyboard shortcut (including Enter or Right arrow) opens or confirms Submit. In Test, Enter and Right arrow only ever mean Next.
- **AC 3.18.6** [Anyone] **Given** a time limit, **then** a `role="timer"` region (not live) shows the time left, calculated from the stored start time and the wall clock. It changes style at 5:00 and at 1:00, and a polite status message is announced once at each.
  - At zero, including when the tab was hidden or the page is reloaded after the deadline, the Test auto-submits with completion time equal to the deadline.
  - Results then show "Time ran out. Your test was submitted automatically."
  - Answers dispatched at or after the deadline are ignored.
- **AC 3.18.7** [Anyone] **Given** I reload or come back through item URLs during a Test, **when** the schema version 3 snapshot (or a restorable version 2 snapshot, AC 3.6.12) matches the scope, roster, identity and mode, **then** answers, flags, the current question, the time left and the quiz size are restored. The scope includes the quiz size for category and global Tests, so the **Quiz size** control shows the saved size (disabled while the Test is in progress) and no new run or restore notice replaces it. This holds for guests and signed-in users: the quiz page shows a loading spinner until sign-in state settles, and only then reads, restores, replaces, or clears a saved run (#393).
  - The following start a new run, with the notice "We could not restore your earlier test, so we started a new one.":
    - version 1 snapshots
    - a mode mismatch
    - flags that are not unique or not in the roster
    - a limit outside 60-36000 s
    - unparseable or out-of-order timestamps
    - a non-active view without a submit time
    - a Practice run that carries Test fields
  - A started, expired run is restored and then auto-submitted, not rejected.
- **AC 3.18.8** [Anyone] **Given** a submitted Test, **then** results show:
  - correct/total and the percentage
  - the missed count, which includes unanswered questions
  - the "not answered" count (questions skipped for an unavailable picture are not "not answered"; see below)
  - "Time taken m:ss of m:ss" (or just "Time taken m:ss" with no limit)
  - **Review all**, **Review mistakes** (only when something was missed), **Play again** and Browse

  **Review all** lists every question in roster order with my answer, the correct answer, the explanation, a Correct / Incorrect / Not answered text badge and a "Flagged" marker. **Review mistakes** includes unanswered questions marked "Not answered". **Back to results** returns.
  **Unavailable pictures (#403).** Questions whose required picture was unreported, loading, failed, stuck or unknown when I submit (and that I had not answered) are not scored: they are in neither the score denominator (the score reads `correct / (total - not scored)`), the missed count nor the missed list, and the summary adds "N questions skipped because the picture was unavailable. Not scored." (the Review all badge reads "Not scored (picture unavailable)" instead of "Not answered"). If nothing was scored the summary reads "No questions were scored" with no percentage and no session is saved. The timer pauses for the active unresolved required picture from initial loading (AC 3.27.3), and early or timeout submission excludes every unanswered unresolved required picture.
- **AC 3.18.9** [Anyone] **Given** a Test is submitted (manually or by timeout), **then** exactly one completion snapshot is published. Restore, review and remount do not repeat it.
  - It is recorded through the guest history (AC 3.6.13) or the study session (AC 3.6.14) and the review queue (AC 3.14.1), unchanged.
  - Missed item IDs are wrong answers first, then unanswered questions in roster order, with at most 200 sent.
  - There are no API, schema or OpenAPI changes.
- **AC 3.18.10** [Anyone] **Given** Test results, **when** I choose **Play again**, **then** the same roster, mode and time limit return to the setup card under a new run ID, with answers, flags and the clock cleared.
- **AC 3.18.11** [Anyone] **Given** a Test, **then** analytics work as follows:
  - `quick_quiz_started` is sent on **Start test**.
  - At submit, one `question_answered` is sent per answered question, with the final correctness and the roster position; unanswered questions send nothing.
  - `quiz_completed` is then sent with score and total.
  - Nothing is sent per answer change.
  - (This amends AC 5.4.9, 5.5.4 and 5.5.2.)

Verification: `quizRun`/`testRun` reducer, restore and selector unit tests; `QuizModePage.testmode.test.tsx` page tests (setup, deferred feedback, palette, Submit dialog, fake-timer countdown and auto-submit, reload after the deadline, restore rejection, mode switching, analytics order, Play again); 375 px Playwright spec `quiz-test-mode.spec.ts`.

### AC 3.19 Share result and celebration (#253)

- **AC 3.19.1** [Anyone] **Given** I complete a quiz with at least one question (collection, category, global, daily or review), **then** a tier line appears under the unchanged "Quiz complete" heading: 100% "Perfect score!", 80 to 99% "Great job!", 50 to 79% "Good effort. Keep going!", under 50% "Keep practicing. Every miss is a lesson." The tier comes from the rounded `percentage` in `summarizeQuizRun`. **Given** total is 0, **then** there is no tier line and no Share button.
- **AC 3.19.2** [Anyone] **Given** results for a collection whose `isPublic === true`, **when** I click **Share result**, **then** the preview, copied text and native share text are `I scored {n}/{t} on "{name}" on Quizymode. Can you beat me?` followed by `{origin}/quiz/collections/{id}/{slug}?utm_source=share&utm_medium=share&utm_campaign=quiz_result&utm_content=collection`. The URL is built with `buildCollectionStudyPath("quiz", id, name)`, and **Show QR code** is available.
- **AC 3.19.3** [Owner] **Given** results for a collection whose `isPublic` is false, missing, or still loading, **then** the shared text is exactly `I scored {n}/{t} on a Quizymode quiz.`. There is no URL and no QR button, and the dialog shows "Private collection: only your score is shared, with no link." The collection name, id and slug appear nowhere in the textarea, the clipboard payload, the `navigator.share` argument, the dialog title, or any alt text.
- **AC 3.19.4** [Anyone] **Given** category results, **then** the text is `I scored {n}/{t} on a {Category name} quiz on Quizymode. Try this quiz!` plus `{origin}/quiz/{categorySlug}?...&utm_content=category`, with no keywords, search or filter params. **Given** unscoped results, **then** the text is `I scored {n}/{t} on a Quizymode quiz. Try this quiz!` plus `{origin}/quiz?...&utm_content=global`. A visit through any of these links is captured per **AC 5.7.10** and classified as `campaign` per **AC 5.7.4**.
- **AC 3.19.5** [Anyone] **Given** the Share result dialog is open, **then**:
  - **Copy** writes the full text and shows "Copied.". It resets after 3 s or when the dialog closes.
  - A copy failure, or a missing clipboard, shows "Couldn't copy. Select the text above and copy it." and focuses and selects the read-only textarea.
  - **Share via...** appears only when `navigator.share` exists. AbortError is ignored, and any other error falls back to Copy.
  - **Show QR code** appears only for linked scopes, and a QR failure shows "The QR code couldn't be created."
  - Escape, a backdrop click or the close button dismisses the dialog, and focus returns to **Share result**.
  - The dialog is a modal dialog (`role=dialog`, `aria-modal`, labelled), so study shortcuts are inert while it is open.
- **AC 3.19.6** [Anyone] **Given** a 100% score and `prefers-reduced-motion` does not match, **then** one confetti burst fires once per run id from a lazily loaded `canvas-confetti` chunk that is not part of the entry bundle. The canvas is `pointer-events: none`, is never focused, and any load failure is silent. **Given** reduced motion, `matchMedia` unavailable, or a score under 100%, **then** the module is never imported.
- **AC 3.19.7** [Anyone] **Given** I answer a question, **then** the correct option pulses and a wrong selected option shakes using only `motion-safe:` utilities, with a check or X icon (aria-hidden) alongside the existing colours. **Given** reduced motion, **then** nothing animates, and the colour, icon and text feedback are unchanged.
- **AC 3.19.8** [Anyone] **Given** the share logic lives in `useShareActions`, **then** the collection Share dialog still behaves exactly as **AC 1.7.5** describes (same labels, status strings, QR alt text and private-link warning).

### AC 3.20 Games (#352, #353, #354, #355, epic #361)

- **AC 3.20.1** [Anyone] **Given** I am on a category, collection, keyword-filtered or global scope screen, **then** the mode switcher shows a **Games** tab (PuzzlePieceIcon) after **Learn**. **When** I open it, **then** I land on the Games hub for that scope: `/games[/:category]`, `/games/collections/:collectionId/:slug?`, with the `nav` and `keywords` query passed through exactly as for Learn. Match Race lives at `/games/match-race[/:category]` and `/games/match-race/collections/:collectionId/:slug?`; Swipe Blitz, Survival, Bucket Drop and Sort It use the same shapes with `swipe-blitz`, `survival`, `bucket-drop` and `sort-it`. Guests can use the hub and every game, and the other tabs navigate away with the scope preserved. (frontend tests: ModeSwitcher, categoryScopeMode, GamesHubPage; Playwright learn.spec.ts)
- **AC 3.20.2** [Anyone] **Given** a game scope loads, **then** a collection uses all its items and a category or global scope without keyword filters loads up to 200 random items from `GET /games/roster`, which applies the eligibility rule below in SQL before sampling (AC 3.20.26), and a keyword-filtered scope uses the items endpoint like Learn (up to 200 random items, filtered afterwards). An item is eligible when its trimmed raw question is at most 80 and its trimmed raw correct answer at most 25 Unicode code points (foreign-phrase markup counts, and an emoji made of several code points counts more than once), both are non-empty, and it has at least one usable distractor (AC 3.6.17) of at most 25 code points; games never show a longer distractor. Items with a repeated id, a repeated correct answer (trimmed, case-insensitive) or a repeated question are dropped, keeping the first, so every tile on a board is unique. Sort It does not use this pool; it builds its own from the same loaded items (AC 3.20.22). A category or navigation name containing `/` cannot be written as a roster scope token, so that scope loads through the items endpoint instead. Items with a curated question picture (AC 3.21.1, #407) are never eligible: the games show raw question text in tiles, so a flag question without its picture would be meaningless (pictures in games stay out of scope). Each roster scope reports `excludedPictures` (AC 3.20.34). The pool identity also includes the picture key, only as a guard for identical questions that differ by picture. Sort It is unaffected (AC 3.20.22). (frontend tests: gameRoster.test.ts) Unscoped Games visits resolve My topics first per AC 3.20.29; every selected path is sent to the roster endpoint before sampling, without the Home display limit.
- **AC 3.20.3** [Anyone] **Given** fewer than 3 eligible items, **then** the hub (unless the scope has a sortable set, AC 3.20.25) and every game except Sort It (AC 3.20.25) show "Not enough short questions for games here" with an **Open Quiz** link to the quiz for the same scope. **Given** loading fails, **then** an alert ("We couldn't load games." on the hub, "We couldn't load this game." on every game) offers **Try again**, which only refetches. (frontend tests: GamesHubPage, MatchRacePage, SwipeBlitzPage, SurvivalPage)
- **AC 3.20.4** [Anyone] **Given** the hub has enough items, **then** it shows a **Games** heading and cards in this order: **Match Race** ("Match each question to its answer.", best time "Best 0:42.1", preferring the current board size), **Swipe Blitz** ("Right or wrong? Beat the clock.", "Best 23") **Survival** ("Answer until you miss 3.", "Best streak 12") **Bucket Drop** ("Catch the answer before it lands.", "Best 14") and **Sort It** ("Sort each fact into its group.", "Best 9"; shown only when the scope has a sortable set, AC 3.20.25). Each card has an icon, shows its best only when one exists for the scope, and has a **Play** link (accessible name "Play {game}", for example "Play Swipe Blitz") to the game for the same scope. The hub card stays generic: it does not check Bucket Drop's stricter eligibility (AC 3.20.16). The cards sit in one column below 640 px, two from 640 px and three from 1024 px. The hub and every ordinary game page show the active topic controls described in AC 3.20.30. (frontend tests: GamesHubPage)
- **AC 3.20.5** [Anyone] **Given** I open Match Race, **then** the board has 6 pairs at a viewport of 640 px or wider and 3 pairs below it (3 when `matchMedia` is unavailable), decided once when the run is created and never changed mid-run; the actual pair count is the smaller of that target and the eligible items. Items are picked and the question and answer columns are shuffled independently from a seed built from a fresh run id. The clock starts at the first tile selection and stops at the last match; the header shows "matched / pairs · m:ss.t" (the timer is hidden from assistive technology and never announced). (frontend tests: matchRaceRun.test.ts, MatchRacePage)
- **AC 3.20.6** [Anyone] **Given** I tap tiles, **then** I can pick the question or the answer first; tapping another tile of the same column moves the selection and tapping the selected tile again deselects it; tapping a tile of the other column compares the pair. A match turns both tiles green with a check icon and "Matched" for assistive technology, keeps them in place and inert (`aria-disabled`, not focusable, clicks ignored) and focus stays where it was. A miss adds 1 second to the time, turns both tiles red with an x icon and "Wrong", shakes them only when reduced motion is not requested, and clears about 600 ms later or at the next tap. Input is never blocked.
- **AC 3.20.7** [Anyone] **Given** I use a keyboard or a screen reader, **then** tiles are buttons (Enter and Space select) with `aria-pressed`, each column is a labelled group, the page heading takes focus on each new board, and a polite live region announces "Selected. Pick an answer.", "Selected. Pick a question.", "Cleared.", "Matched. 2 of 6.", "Not a match. Plus 1 second." and "Done in 0:12.3." (plus " New best!") without any question or answer text. All targets are at least 44 px high, colors are not the only cue, and there are no hotkeys, speech buttons or pronunciation hints on tiles.
- **AC 3.20.8** [Anyone] **Given** I match the last pair, **then** the board is replaced by "Done!" (focused), the final time including penalties, either "Best m:ss.t" or a "New best!" badge (also on the first run, with a confetti burst unless reduced motion), **Play again** (a new run id and board from the same items, no refetch) and **Back to games**. Best times, scores and streaks are kept per game and scope (Match Race also per pair count) in `localStorage` key `quizymode.games.best.v1` (at most 100 entries across all games, oldest dropped); unreadable, invalid or unwritable storage never throws and shows no error, the best then lasts for the page's life. (frontend tests: matchRaceBest.test.ts, gameBest.test.ts, MatchRacePage)
- **AC 3.20.9** [Anyone] **Given** I finish a game, **then** no study session, guest history entry or `quizymode:quiz-completed` event is produced and nothing is added to the Review queue automatically; the only reporting is the game events of AC 5.4.13. Swipe Blitz, Survival, Bucket Drop and Sort It list my misses on the finish screen (a "Misses" heading and a keyboard-focusable, scrollable region, labelled "Misses", with each missed question and its correct answer in the order first missed). **When** I am signed in and there are misses, **Add misses to Review** queues each missed item once through AC 3.14.5 (an item already queued goes back to the first interval) and the always-present status line reports "Added 4 to Review.", "Added 3 to Review. 1 can't be added." or "This can't be added to Review." / "These can't be added to Review." for items the API answers 404 to (including items in another user's private collection). Any other failure (network, 401, 429, 5xx) shows "Couldn't add 2 to Review." or "Added 2. Couldn't add 1." and the button becomes **Try again**, which retries only the failed items. The button reads "Adding..." while busy and "Added" or "Can't add" when nothing is left to do, stays in place and uses `aria-disabled`. Guests see the misses but no button. (frontend tests: useAddMissesToReview.test.tsx, SwipeBlitzPage, SurvivalPage, BucketDropPage)
- **AC 3.20.10** [Anyone] **Given** I open Swipe Blitz (#354), **then** each card shows a question and either its correct answer or one of its short distractors (AC 3.20.2), chosen 50/50 from a seed of the run id and card number, with a small "Answer?" label above the shown answer. Cards follow a seeded order of the scope's items; no item repeats until all were shown, then the order reshuffles, never showing the same item twice in a row. The card is a focusable group named by its question and described by the answer, and takes focus when the run starts, after each right call and when a reveal ends (never during a reveal), except as described in AC 3.20.11. Play again starts a new run id over the same items without refetching. (frontend tests: swipeBlitzRun.test.ts, deckCycle.test.ts, SwipeBlitzPage)
- **AC 3.20.11** [Anyone] **Given** a card, **then** a right swipe, the Right arrow key or the **Right** button (ArrowRightIcon, `aria-keyshortcuts="ArrowRight"`) says the shown answer is right, and a left swipe, the Left arrow key or the **Wrong** button (ArrowLeftIcon, `aria-keyshortcuts="ArrowLeft"`) says it is not. Arrow keys are ignored with a modifier key, on key repeat, while composing text, when the event was already handled and while typing in an input, textarea, select or editable field, and are detached once the round is over. A swipe must be mostly horizontal and at least 50 px; vertical scrolling still works and nothing follows the finger. The buttons are at least 56 px high, use `aria-disabled` (not `disabled`) during a reveal and when finished, and nothing animates when reduced motion is requested. **When** a button is activated from the keyboard (Enter or Space), focus stays on that button; **when** it is clicked or tapped, or after a swipe, focus moves to the card; an arrow key pressed while a button has focus decides once and leaves focus there. (frontend tests: swipeGesture.test.ts, SwipeBlitzPage)
- **AC 3.20.12** [Anyone] **Given** I make the first decision, **then** a 60-second round starts (until then the hint "Clock starts on your first pick." shows); the header shows "Score n · m:ss" (the time hidden from assistive technology, red at 10 seconds or less) above a decorative time bar. A right call adds 1 and shows the next card at once. A wrong call turns the card red and replaces the answer with "Answer: {correct answer}" for 1 second, during which input is ignored and the clock keeps running. A decision made after time has run out does not count. The clock keeps running if the tab is hidden, and the deadline is rechecked when the tab becomes visible again. If time runs out during a reveal the round ends. A polite live region announces "Score 5.", "Missed. Answer shown.", "10 seconds left." (once) and "Time's up. Score 12." (plus " New best!") without question or answer text. (frontend tests: swipeBlitzRun.test.ts, SwipeBlitzPage)
- **AC 3.20.13** [Anyone] **Given** time runs out, **then** "Time's up!" (focused) shows my score, "{score} of {decisions} right", "Best n" or the "New best!" badge (best score per scope; only scores above 0 are stored, shown or celebrated), **Play again**, **Back to games** and then my misses (AC 3.20.9). (frontend tests: gameBest.test.ts, SwipeBlitzPage)
- **AC 3.20.14** [Anyone] **Given** I open Survival (#355), **then** each question shows its correct answer and up to 3 short distractors (2 to 4 options, shuffled from a seed of the run and question; an item with only one short distractor shows 2 options), 3 hearts ("2 of 3 hearts left" for assistive technology) and "Streak n"; items follow the AC 3.20.10 order rule. Options are plain buttons at least 52 px high with no letters, hotkeys, speech buttons or pronunciation hints. A correct answer turns my option green (with "Correct" for assistive technology), adds 1 to the streak and moves on after about 0.6 s. A wrong answer turns my option red ("Wrong"), the correct one green ("Right answer"), costs a heart, resets the streak and waits for **Next** (**See results** on the third miss), which takes focus and ignores clicks for its first 300 ms. Options use `aria-disabled` once answered. The question takes focus on each new question. A polite live region announces "Correct. Streak 4.", "Wrong. 2 hearts left." / "Wrong. 1 heart left." / "Wrong. No hearts left." and "Game over. Streak 7." (plus " New best!") without item text. Play again starts a new run id without refetching. (frontend tests: survivalRun.test.ts, deckCycle.test.ts, SurvivalPage)
- **AC 3.20.15** [Anyone] **Given** I lose the third heart, **then** the game is reported (AC 5.4.13) and my longest streak of the run is stored as the best streak when it is higher, right away; **See results** then opens "Game over" (focused) with "Streak n", "{correct} of {answered} right", "Best streak n" or the "New best!" badge (a streak of 0 is never stored, shown or celebrated), **Play again**, **Back to games** and my misses (AC 3.20.9). (frontend tests: gameBest.test.ts, SurvivalPage)
- **AC 3.20.16** [Anyone] **Given** I open Bucket Drop (#356), **then** it plays on the items of the scope that have at least 2 usable short distractors (AC 3.20.2) on top of the AC 3.20.2 rules; below 3 such items it shows the empty state of AC 3.20.3. Each question is a card in a play area above three buckets (the correct answer and 2 short distractors, shuffled from a seed of the run and card number). Items follow the AC 3.20.10 order rule. Play again starts a new run id without refetching. (frontend tests: gameRoster.test.ts, bucketDropRun.test.ts, BucketDropPage)
- **AC 3.20.17** [Anyone] **Given** a card, **then** I pick bucket 1, 2 or 3 by tapping it, by pressing the key 1, 2 or 3, or by swiping the play area (at least 50 px and mostly along one axis: left = bucket 1, down = bucket 2, right = bucket 3; up and diagonals do nothing, and the play area does not scroll the page).
