The keys are ignored with a modifier key, on key repeat, while composing text, when the event was already handled and while typing in an input, textarea, select or editable field, and are detached once the game is over. Buckets are real buttons named "1. {answer}" with `aria-keyshortcuts`, at least 64 px high, show their full text in a tooltip, and use `aria-disabled` (not `disabled`) during feedback. (frontend tests: bucketGesture.test.ts, BucketDropPage)
- **AC 3.20.18** [Anyone] **Given** I have not yet made a first pick, **then** the first card stays still at the top and the hint "Tap a bucket, or press 1, 2, 3." shows; the clock starts with the first pick, tap, key or swipe (which answers that first card), like AC 3.20.12. From the second card on, each card falls from the top of the play area to the bottom in 6 seconds, 15% faster after every 5 correct answers (never under 2 seconds). Falling pauses during feedback and while the tab is hidden. A card that lands before a pick costs a life like a wrong bucket. With reduced motion the answer card stays still and a bar shows the time left; the timing is the same and nothing shakes or pulses. (frontend tests: bucketDropRun.test.ts, BucketDropPage)
- **AC 3.20.19** [Anyone] **Given** I pick or a card lands, **then** feedback shows for about 0.8 seconds and the next card starts to fall: a correct bucket turns green with a check and "Correct" for assistive technology and adds 1 to the score; a wrong pick turns red, the right bucket turns green and the card shows "Answer: {correct answer}"; a card that lands shows the same without a red bucket. A miss costs 1 of 3 lives ("2 of 3 lives left" for assistive technology next to "Score n"). The question card is focusable and takes focus at the start and with each new card (never during feedback). A polite live region announces "Correct. Score 5.", "Wrong. 2 lives left.", "Too slow. 1 life left.", "Wrong. No lives left." and "Game over. Score 12." (plus " New best!") without question or answer text. When the third life goes the game is reported (AC 5.4.13) and the score stored as the best (only scores above 0), right away; after the feedback "Game over" (focused) shows the score, "{score} of {answered} right", "Best n" or the "New best!" badge, **Play again**, **Back to games** and my misses (AC 3.20.9). Best score is kept per scope under AC 3.20.8. (frontend tests: gameBest.test.ts, BucketDropPage)
- **AC 3.20.20** [Anyone] **Given** I play Match Race or Survival, **then** the best run for the scope becomes a ghost saved in `localStorage` key `quizymode.games.ghost.v1` (at most 20 entries, 300 marks per entry, oldest dropped; unreadable, invalid or unwritable storage never throws and shows no error, the ghost then lasts for the page's life). A Match Race ghost is the elapsed time (penalties included, the same clock as the header) at each matched pair, kept per scope and pair count, and replaced by a faster finish. A Survival ghost is the time since my first answer at each correct answer, kept per scope, and replaced by a run with more correct answers, or the same number reached faster; it is distinct from the best streak of AC 3.20.15. (frontend tests: ghostRun.test.ts)
- **AC 3.20.21** [Anyone] **Given** a ghost exists for the scope, **then** while I play a thin decorative track under the header shows my progress and a gray ghost marker (Match Race: pairs matched, scaled to the pair count; Survival: correct answers, scaled to the larger of the ghost's total and mine), with the readable text "Ghost: 4 of 8" (Match Race) or "Ghost: 4" (Survival) that is never announced as it changes; the marker steps on the run clock about every 250 ms (100 ms in Match Race) without easing. With no ghost nothing is shown. On the finish screen a line follows the best line: Match Race "1.4 s ahead of your ghost." / "2.1 s behind your ghost." / "Level with your ghost."; Survival, compared at the moment the third heart was lost, "3 answers ahead of your ghost at 0:42." / "1 answer behind your ghost at 0:42." / "Level with your ghost at 0:42."; with no ghost "First run: this is your ghost now." Every ghost line ends with a full stop and the same text ends the finish announcement. The Survival clock starts at the first answer, so a correct first answer is marked at 0:00 and the ghost counts it from the start. (frontend tests: ghostRun.test.ts, MatchRacePage, SurvivalPage)
- **AC 3.20.22** [Anyone] **Given** a game scope loads (#359), **then** Sort It builds its pool from the raw loaded items (not the AC 3.20.2 pool; the question is never shown, so it has no length rule and is not deduplicated). An item counts when its trimmed group label has 1 to 30 characters and its trimmed correct answer 1 to 25 characters. In order: repeated item ids are dropped (first wins); an answer (trimmed, whitespace-collapsed, case-insensitive) that appears in more than one group, or equals any group label, is dropped everywhere; a repeated answer inside one group is dropped (first wins); a group needs at least 4 items left. Groups are identified by the label trimmed, whitespace-collapsed and lowercased, shown with the first spelling. With fewer than 2 qualifying groups the scope has no sortable set. With more than 4 qualifying groups each run plays 4 of them, picked from the run id. (frontend tests: sortItPool.test.ts)
- **AC 3.20.23** [Anyone] **Given** I open Sort It at `/games/sort-it...`, **then** the heading "Sort It", the lives and "Score n" look and work like Bucket Drop (AC 3.20.16), and each falling card shows the correct answer text of an item while one bucket per group is shown, numbered 1 to N (2 to 4 buckets), in an order shuffled once per run. Buckets sit in a 2-column grid for 2 groups, 3 columns for 3 and a 2x2 grid below 640 px (one row from 640 px) for 4; long labels wrap and clamp to 3 lines. I pick a bucket by tapping it or pressing its number key (digits above the number of buckets are ignored; the same key guards as AC 3.20.17); there is no swipe. The hint is "Tap a group, or press 1, 2." (up to "1, 2, 3, 4."). (frontend tests: SortItPage)
- **AC 3.20.24** [Anyone] **Given** I play Sort It, **then** timing, feedback, lives, live-region announcements, finish card, best score (key `sort-it|{scope}`) and reporting follow AC 3.20.18, 3.20.19 and 5.4.13 with these differences: a correct pick turns the bucket green with "Correct"; a wrong pick or a landing shows "Group: {label}" on the card, the right bucket green and "Right answer" for assistive technology; announcements read "Wrong. The group is {label}. 2 lives left." and "Too slow. The group is {label}. 2 lives left."; each miss in the Misses list shows the answer text with its group label in bold. The run uses a deck of all items of the chosen groups. (frontend tests: sortItRun.test.ts, SortItPage, gameBest.test.ts)
- **AC 3.20.25** [Anyone] **Given** the scope has no sortable set, **then** `/games/sort-it...` shows "No sortable groups here", "Sort It needs 2 to 4 groups with at least 4 short answers each." and an **Open Quiz** link for the same scope; the hub shows no Sort It card. **Given** the scope has a sortable set, **then** the hub shows the Sort It card even when fewer than 3 items qualify for the other games (those cards are then hidden, and the whole-hub empty state of AC 3.20.3 shows only when neither applies). Sort It uses the same loaded roster; the server keeps items with a group label and a short answer even when the question is long (AC 3.20.26). (frontend tests: GamesHubPage, SortItPage)
- **AC 3.20.26** [Anyone] **Given** I call `GET /games/roster?scope=category[/l1[/l2]]&limit=n` (#381, anonymous), **then** it returns `{ items, scopes }` where `items` are the standard item shape (empty `collections`) and `scopes` has one `{ scope, available, returned, status }` per requested scope, in request order. `scope` is repeated 0 to 10 times (none means every category and reports one scope with an empty name); tokens are trimmed and repeated tokens (case-insensitive) count once. `limit` is 1 to 200 (default 200). **Given** more than 10 scopes, a limit outside 1 to 200, an empty segment, more than 3 segments, or a segment over 100 characters, **then** the response is 400. **Given** a scope's status, **then** it is `ok` when the scope contributed at least one item to the response, otherwise `empty`; `available` is its eligible-item count, which is 0 for an unknown category or an invalid navigation path (never an error), and can be above 0 for an `empty` scope whose items were all already taken by an earlier scope or the limit. **Then** eligibility is applied in SQL before sampling, so a scope with 3 eligible items among 1000 ineligible ones always returns them: the item is returned when (it has no question picture (AC 3.21.1, #407), its trimmed question has 1 to 80 and its trimmed correct answer 1 to 25 code points, and at least one distractor has 1 to 25 code points and differs from the answer ignoring case) or (its trimmed group label has 1 to 30 and its trimmed correct answer 1 to 25 code points). Trimming matches JavaScript `trim()`, including no-break and ideographic spaces. The picture exclusion applies to the question-game branch only; an item with a picture and a group label stays in the Sort It branch. This is a looser prefilter: the client still applies AC 3.20.2, AC 3.20.16 and AC 3.20.22. Keyword-filtered scopes do not use this endpoint and still sample before filtering. (backend tests: GameRosterPostgresTests, GameRosterHttpTests, GameRosterValidatorTests; frontend tests: games.test.ts, GamesHubPage)
- **AC 3.20.27** [Anyone] **Given** several scopes, **then** each scope's eligible items are shuffled and merged round-robin (one item per scope per turn), skipping items already taken by an earlier scope (overlapping scopes such as a category and one of its topics never duplicate an item), until `limit` items are chosen or every scope is exhausted; a small scope gives up its unused share to the others (scopes with 5 and 300 eligible items and limit 20 return 5 and 15). `available` is the scope's eligible count and `returned` the items it contributed. (backend tests: GameRosterPostgresTests)
- **AC 3.20.28** [Anyone] **Given** I call the roster endpoint, **then** visibility matches `GET /items`: guests and other users receive public items only, and a signed-in caller also receives their own private items; a guest never gets a 401/403. (backend tests: GameRosterPostgresTests, GameRosterHttpTests)
- **AC 3.20.29** [Anyone] **Given** I open the Games hub or an ordinary game URL, **then** an explicit collection/category/navigation/keyword scope wins, including when `all=1` is present. Without an explicit scope, `?all=1` deliberately plays all topics; otherwise every saved My topics path (up to 10, including refinements) is passed to `GET /games/roster` as a union. Account choices must finish loading before fetching; a failed account load offers Retry topics and never falls back to a global roster. With no choices, a prompt offers Choose topics and Play all topics, and no roster is fetched until a choice is made. Activity-derived defaults are deferred to #385.
- **AC 3.20.30** [Anyone] The Games hub, Match Race, Swipe Blitz, Survival, Bucket Drop and Sort It show a Game topics region with the active names, Change topics, Play My topics and All topics. Explicit links identify their own scope. The selected topic union and first successful roster are fixed for that page visit; editing topics never restarts the current run or swaps its questions. Opening Play My topics, All topics or another Games page starts a new visit using the appropriate current choices. Identity changes start a separate run and do not retain the previous identity's roster. The Review-based Boss Fight keeps its due-review scope.
- **AC 3.20.31** [Anyone] **Given** a roster scope reports `empty`, **then** its topic is named as unavailable. Playable selected scopes remain usable; when none yield a playable game the existing empty state remains with Change topics / Play My topics / All topics available. The page never substitutes unrelated topics automatically.
- **AC 3.20.32** [Anyone] Best-score and ghost identities for My topics use `topics:` followed by the sorted unique full topic tokens, so order does not change the record but different refinements or unions do. Existing explicit category, collection and keyword keys are unchanged, and explicit All topics reuses the existing global key.
- **AC 3.20.33** [Anyone] While the topic editor is open over a game, its controls keep focus even if the underlying game advances or finishes, and game answer hotkeys do not act on keys pressed inside the dialog. Saving edits applies to the next page visit; the current run continues without resetting.
- **AC 3.20.34** [Anyone] Games exclude items with a curated question picture (AC 3.20.2, 3.20.26): the client drops them from every question-game pool, `GET /games/roster` leaves them out of the question-game branch, and Sort It still counts such an item when it has a group label and a short answer. Each `GET /games/roster` scope result also carries `excludedPictures` (#403): the number of picture questions the caller can see in that scope (the same visibility and scope filter as the roster itself, so another user's private picture item is not counted) that no game can use; collection and keyword-filtered scopes count picture items in the loaded roster instead. **Given** a scope has no playable question at all and `excludedPictures` is above 0, **then** the empty state (a `status` region) reads "No games for this set" with "N picture questions here aren't playable as games. Practise them in Quiz." (singular: "1 picture question here isn't playable as a game. Practise it in Quiz.") and an **Open Quiz** link to the same scope's quiz (at least 44 px high, full width on phones). **Given** there are some questions but too few for games and `excludedPictures` is above 0, **then** the generic "Not enough short questions for games here" state stays and the picture line is added under it. **Given** games are available and `excludedPictures` is above 0, **then** the Games hub adds "N picture questions are left out of games." (backend test: GameRosterPostgresTests; frontend tests: gameRoster.test.ts, GamesEmptyState.test.tsx)

### AC 3.21 Question and explanation pictures, curated (#407, #403)

Curated pictures are bundled with the web app (`src/Quizymode.Web/src/assets/curated/**/*.webp`); an item references one by key (AC 2.1.19) as a question picture or an explanation picture. There is no upload on any screen and no external URL; uploaded pictures have their own review and access rules (AC 3.25, #400, dark) and their authoring screen is a follow-up (#402; see the known gaps in AC 3.21.1). Their backend foundation, #399 (private storage and picture normalization, API only, no item field and no UI), is AC 3.24 and AC 7.7. The first picture sets are the country flags (AC 3.22) and the explanation diagrams (AC 7.5.3).

- **AC 3.21.1** [Anyone] **Given** an item has a `questionImage`, **then** the picture is shown on the surfaces listed here, in a fixed 3:2 box centred at most 28rem wide and at most 30vh tall on phones (18rem from the `sm` breakpoint) with the picture fitted inside (`object-fit: contain`, never cropped) and a 1 px ring: Quiz and Practice, Test (also while a timer runs), Daily quiz, Review queue and Boss run questions (through the shared quiz renderer), Learn questions, Explore flashcards, the item detail page and the Edit item preview (AC 2.1.22). Thumbnails appear on the surfaces of AC 3.21.6. Uploaded `media:` references use the exact authorized item/collection blob resolver of AC 3.27.2; curated keys still resolve only from the build-time allowlist, and malformed/unbundled keys never become external URLs. Shared rendering, descriptions, JSON round-trip and moderation/lifecycle rules are specified in AC 3.27. Global search results currently omit pictures; this remains an explicit follow-up rather than an uploaded-delivery pass. (frontend tests: QuestionImage.test.tsx, curatedImages.test.ts, QuestionImagePlacements.test.tsx, ItemDetailPage.test.tsx, EditItemPage.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.21.2** [Anyone] **Given** I use an Explore flashcard with a picture, **then** the picture sits in one slot above both card faces, so it stays visible after flipping, no height jump happens on flip, and no image is inside a flip button (the button keeps its accessible name "Show answer and explanation"). In Quiz, Test, Learn and the detail page the picture is shown under the question text and pronunciation hint, before and after the answer is revealed. **Explanation pictures (#403)** use the same 3:2 box (the Enlarge button of AC 3.21.9 included) and are mounted only after the answer is revealed, directly below the explanation text and never before "Correct Answer": in a Quiz inside the answer panel (practice and Daily after an answer; the review queue and Boss runs too), on the flashcard back only while the card is flipped (the back face exists in the DOM before the flip, so the picture is conditionally rendered and unmounted again when flipping back, never just hidden), in Learn after the answer is submitted, in the Test review list and the Quiz mistake review as a thumbnail, on the item detail page under the explanation, as a thumbnail beside the explanation in item lists (only when the answer is shown) and on the review board, and in print (AC 1.14.4). A Test never mounts, preloads or announces an explanation picture before submit, and no explanation picture is ever preloaded. A failed explanation picture shows "Picture couldn't load" with Retry only, never blocks answering and has no Skip. (frontend test: QuestionImagePlacements.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.21.3** [Anyone, assistive technology] **Given** a picture is shown, **then** its `alt` is the item's alt text, which is never shown as a visible caption and never set as a `title`. Alt text describes the picture's design (colors, shapes, layout) and must not name the answer; it stays the same after the answer is revealed. Accepted limitation: a screen-reader user hears a description rather than seeing the flag, so the task differs from the visual one. (frontend test: QuestionImage.test.tsx; Python validator AC 5.2.23)
- **AC 3.21.4** [Anyone, 375x812 phone] **Given** a Quiz or Test question has a picture, **then** the picture box is about 218 px tall and all four answer options remain visible without scrolling. (manual check at 375x812, PR 2 evidence)
- **AC 3.21.5** [Anyone] **Given** a question picture is loading, **then** the box shows a pulsing skeleton, is `aria-busy="true"` and (large size) announces "Loading picture". **Given** the item has a question picture and it has not loaded (loading, failed, stuck or unknown key), **then** answering is off, computed by the page on every render (`item has questionImage and status is not loaded`, keyed by item id) so answers are never enabled for even one paint: the option buttons (native `disabled`, dimmed, `cursor-not-allowed`; group "Answer options" with `aria-disabled` and a hidden note "Answers are unavailable until the picture loads."), the "Type answer" checkbox and typed input with Check answer, and the flashcard grade buttons are disabled, and the answer handlers themselves ignore the answer, so pressing 1-4 or A-D (and Enter in typed answers) does nothing. Only answering is blocked: Next, Previous, the Test palette, Flag and Submit stay enabled, and thumbnails (small and extra-small) never block. **Given** it fails to load, **then** the box shows "Picture couldn't load" with **Retry** (min 44 px, `aria-label` "Retry loading picture"; it shows the loading state again, reloads the file, and moves focus to the picture box) and, where a question can be skipped (AC 3.21.10), **Skip question (not scored)**, plus "Answers are off until the picture shows. You can retry or skip this question." **Given** it is still loading after 10 seconds, **then** it shows "Picture is taking too long to load" with the same Retry and Skip (Retry restarts the 10 second timer; a late load re-enables answering and announces "Picture loaded. Answers are available."). **Given** the key is unknown, **then** the box shows "Picture isn't available" with Skip only (no Retry, no 10 second wait). Thumbnails show an icon only (no Retry) with an accessible name of the same message. (frontend tests: QuestionImage.test.tsx, QuestionImagePlacements.test.tsx, quizRunSkipped.test.ts)
- **AC 3.21.6** [Anyone] **Given** an item with a picture appears in the item list, a collection's item list, the admin review board or the `/items` page's item cards, **then** a small thumbnail (72x48 px, 96x64 px from `sm`, lazy loaded) shows before the question; in the quiz results review list and the Learn round recap a 48x32 px thumbnail shows before the question text (the recap shows one only for question pictures). In the admin daily-quiz page (AC 3.13.8) active rows show the small thumbnail; removed rows and question-quality table rows (AC 5.5.11) show a 48x32 px thumbnail, for the question picture only. **Given** the item has an explanation picture (#403), **then** the same small thumbnail (no Enlarge button) shows beside the explanation in the item list answer section (only when the answer is shown), the review board and the quiz review list. (frontend tests: QuestionImagePlacements.test.tsx, QuizCompletionView.test.tsx, AdminDailyQuizPage.test.tsx, QuestionQualityTab.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.21.7** [System] **Given** the web app is built, **then** every curated `.webp` is emitted as a separate content-hashed file `assets/m/<hash>.webp` and is never inlined as a `data:image/webp` URI in a JavaScript bundle; CI runs `npm run check:curated-images` after `npm run build`, which fails when a `data:image/webp` string is found in `dist/**/*.js`, when the number of distinct source `.webp` files differs from the number in `dist/assets/m/`, or when a `.webp` is emitted elsewhere. The deploy and rollback workflows upload `*.webp` in a separate step with `Content-Type: image/webp` and `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`, outside `--delete` (superseded hashed files stay in the bucket; accepted). Unit tests cannot prove the build output (Vitest does not run a production build), so build and deploy evidence (dist listing, `aws s3api head-object`, a CloudFront response) is collected in the PR that adds the first pictures. (script test: checkCuratedImages.test.ts)
- **AC 3.21.8** [Anyone] **Given** Learn saves a guest run with an item snapshot (AC 3.17.7), **then** the snapshot keeps the item's question and explanation picture references, and a restored run shows them. **Given** a stored snapshot has a malformed `questionImage`, **then** the run is treated as invalid and starts fresh; **given** only its `explanationImage` is malformed, **then** that picture is dropped and the run is kept (the question is still answerable). A stored run, quiz run or Daily record that predates skipping (no `skipped` field) restores with nothing skipped. (frontend tests: learnStorage.test.ts, quizRunSkipped.test.ts, learnRunSkipped.test.ts)
- **AC 3.21.9** [Anyone] **Given** a large picture of either slot is loaded, **then** an **Enlarge picture** button (`aria-label` "Enlarge picture", `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, 44x44 px, magnifier icon, bottom right of the picture box, hidden when printing) sits beside the picture (the image is never inside a button) and a mouse click on the picture opens the same view. The enlarged view is a modal `role="dialog"` named "Picture" with the picture (same bundled URL, same `alt`) fitted inside the viewport (`object-fit: contain`, no page horizontal scroll at 375 px or at 200% zoom), a visible caption with the alt text (the only place the alt text is shown) and a **Close** button that holds the initial focus. It is mounted only while open (no dialog exists in the page before Enlarge is used), traps Tab, locks page scroll while open, closes on Escape, Close or a backdrop click (a click on the panel does not close it) and returns focus to the Enlarge button. While it is open the single-key study shortcuts (1-4, A-D, arrows, F) do nothing; Space and Enter only activate the focused Close button. The dialog is rendered in a portal on `document.body` (the Test question-palette sheet and other modal dialogs share this), so it is positioned against the viewport even when the page is scrolled. (page-level test: QuizModePage.hotkeys.test.tsx) Thumbnails have no Enlarge button. (frontend tests: QuestionImage.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.21.10** [Anyone] **Given** a question picture has failed, is stuck or is unknown and I choose **Skip question (not scored)**, **then** the question is left out of the score and the run moves on: Practice records no answer and the item is listed as skipped (`QuizRunState.skipped`; the run is complete when every item is answered or skipped); a Test and the Daily quiz and review queue behave the same way (the Test records skips at submit, AC 3.18.8; a Test question's Skip simply goes to the next question); Learn records no attempt and no miss, drops the item from the later rounds and from completion (mastery counts only the other items); Explore flashcards move to the next card without a grade; the review queue posts nothing to the server for a skipped item. A skipped question is never answerable again in that run. The summary adds "N questions skipped because the picture was unavailable. Not scored." (singular "1 question ...") and when every question was skipped it reads "No questions were scored" with no percentage and no session is saved. A run with any skipped question shows the plain score with the skipped note: no tier line (such as "Perfect score"), no celebration. Review-queue Boss HP counts only the questions that were not skipped, so a run with skips can still be won. A stored practice or Learn run keeps its skipped ids (`skipped` is optional in stored runs; schema versions are unchanged); the Test palette marks them with a dashed amber button, a warning badge, the label ", not scored (picture unavailable)", the legend "Not scored (picture unavailable)" and "N not scored" in the counts line, and the Submit confirmation says "N questions with an unavailable picture will not be scored." The Test timer pauses for the active unresolved required picture (AC 3.27.3). (frontend tests: quizRunSkipped.test.ts, learnRunSkipped.test.ts, QuizCompletionView.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.21.11** [Anyone] **Given** I move through a Quiz, Test, Daily quiz or Learn run, **then** only the next question's picture is preloaded (a single `Image` per step); explanation pictures are never preloaded. The Read aloud buttons read the question and answer text only: pictures have no audio mode and no description is spoken. (frontend tests: QuestionImagePlacements.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.21.12** [Authenticated] JSON upload permits strict uploaded asset references with required alt text; the server verifies ownership/readiness for every row before side effects. The client names unsupported picture fields/row numbers, including curated references/URLs/bytes. Missing/null remains valid. Server errors preserve the pasted text and topic; imported pictures remain private pending human review (AC 3.27.4).

### AC 3.22 Country flags picture set (#407)

Category **geography** > **flags** > **countries** holds 195 flag questions, one per country, each with a curated picture (AC 3.21). The set is the 193 United Nations member states plus the two UN observer states (the Holy See and the State of Palestine). The 11 hand-written flag trivia items that were already in the set stay unchanged.

- **AC 3.22.1** [Anyone] **Given** I open a flag question, **then** it reads "Which country's flag is this?", the correct answer is the country, and there are three wrong answers that are other countries from the same world region (Africa, Asia, Europe, North America including Central America and the Caribbean, South America, or Oceania), with look-alike flags chosen first where they exist. **Given** the answer is revealed, **then** the explanation gives a short factual description of the flag and its source link opens the picture's Wikimedia Commons file page. The set exposes 195 distinct correct answers; none appears twice. (python tests: scripts/tests/test_flag_items.py)
- **AC 3.22.2** [Anyone] **Given** a country's answer is shown, **then** it is the United Nations short name (for example Türkiye, Czechia, Cabo Verde, Côte d'Ivoire, Timor-Leste, Republic of the Congo, Democratic Republic of the Congo), except where the formal UN name is awkward and a common English short form exists: Bolivia, Iran, Laos, Moldova, Russia, Syria, Tanzania, Venezuela, Vietnam, North Korea, South Korea, Vatican City (for the Holy See) and Palestine (for the State of Palestine). The rule is documented in `scripts/flag_country_data.py`. **Given** a flag question has alt text, **then** the alt text describes only colours, shapes and layout and never contains the country's name; the seed validator rejects a word that starts with the name (so "Omani" or "Cuban" fail) but not a name that is only the end of another word (for example "Oman" in "Roman").
- **AC 3.22.3** [Anyone] **Given** I open the Afghanistan flag question, **then** the picture is the black, red and green tricolour of the former Islamic Republic of Afghanistan and the explanation says that it is still used at the United Nations and that the Taliban flies a white flag bearing the Shahada inside the country. **Given** I open the Syria flag question, **then** the picture is the green, white and black flag with three red stars adopted in 2025 and the explanation says it replaces the earlier red, white and black flag with two green stars. **Given** any flag question, **then** its only keyword is its region (africa, asia, europe, north-america, south-america or oceania); there are no per-country keywords. (python tests: scripts/tests/test_flag_items.py)

### AC 3.23 Test-date plan (#364)

A visitor can set a test date for a collection and gets one small step on the Home **Today** lane each day. The server stores one row per (user, collection) holding only the test date; the step of the day is derived by the client from the date, the day the plan was created and the visitor's Learn and Quiz activity on that collection (AC 3.23.6). Guests keep plans in their browser (AC 3.23.8) and they move into the account at sign-in (AC 3.23.9).

- **AC 3.23.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `GET /study-plans`, **then** I get `{ plans }` for my own plans only, ordered by test date and then creation time. Each plan has `collectionId`, `available`, `collectionName`, `itemCount`, `testDate` (`yyyy-MM-dd`), `createdAt`, `firstLearnAt` and `lastActivityAt`. `firstLearnAt` is the earliest and `lastActivityAt` the latest completed run (Learn runs count for the first; Learn and Quiz runs, Test included, for the second) saved to my history with `SourceKind` collection and this collection, among runs completed on or after 14 days before the plan was created (imported guest runs count); either is `null` when there is none. Before listing, my plans whose test date is more than 2 days before today (UTC) are deleted. A plan whose collection no longer exists or is private and not mine is returned with `available: false`, `collectionName: null`, `itemCount: 0` and no activity, so a collection's name is never revealed after it stops being visible. Anonymous callers get 401.
- **AC 3.23.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `PUT /study-plans/{collectionId}` with `{ "testDate": "yyyy-MM-dd" }`, **then** in this order: anonymous callers get 401; a missing, malformed or out-of-window date (earlier than yesterday or later than 31 days after today, UTC) gets 400 with the stable title `StudyPlans.InvalidDate`; a collection that does not exist or that I cannot see (private and not mine) gets 404 with an empty body, byte-identical for both cases; otherwise the plan is created, or my existing plan has its date changed (its `createdAt` is kept and `updatedAt` advances) and I get 200 with the plan as in AC 3.23.1. Plans whose test date is more than 2 days past are pruned first, and only plans dated today or later count towards the limit of 5: creating a 6th upcoming plan gets 400 with title `StudyPlans.LimitReached` and the message "You can plan up to 5 tests at a time."; changing an existing plan is allowed at the limit. Repeating the same request is harmless, and a concurrent duplicate create is retried once as an update.
- **AC 3.23.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `DELETE /study-plans/{collectionId}`, **then** my plan for that collection is removed and I get 204 whether or not it existed. It never touches another user's plan, whatever the collection's visibility (a request for someone else's collection still gets 204 and changes nothing). Anonymous callers get 401. There is no admin endpoint and no cross-user view of plans.
- **AC 3.23.4** [Authenticated] **Given** plans are stored, **then** table `StudyPlans` has primary key (`UserId`, `CollectionId`), `TestDate` (a date without time zone), `CreatedAt`, `UpdatedAt` and an index on `CollectionId`; it has no foreign key, and `DELETE /collections/{id}` (AC 1.8.1) removes every user's plan for that collection in the same save. A plan is private to its user.
- **AC 3.23.5** [Anyone] **Given** I open a collection page that has at least one item, **then** the header actions show a **Plan for a test** button between **Share** and **Details** (guests too), which becomes **Test plan: Fri, Nov 6** (accessible name "Test plan: Friday, November 6. Edit") once I have a plan whose date has not passed. The button opens the **Plan for a test** dialog (`role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, labelled, focus trapped, Escape and backdrop close it, focus returns to the button). The dialog has a native date field limited to today through 30 days from today (local calendar days) prefilled with my plan's date, a "What your plan looks like" preview, **Remove plan** (only when a plan exists), **Cancel** and **Save plan**. The preview lists a **Today** row first, then at most one "Rest until {date}" line for the run of rest days that follows, then each of the last 7 days up to **Test day**, assuming each earlier day is done. Validation runs on change, and Save is disabled while the date is empty or invalid, with these messages: "Choose a test date.", "That date has already passed. Choose today or later.", "Choose a date within the next 30 days.", "Enter a valid date.". While a signed-in visitor's existing plan loads the dialog shows "Loading your plan…" with Save disabled. Errors keep the dialog open and the date: cap "You can plan up to 5 tests at a time. Remove a plan from another collection first." (guests are limited to 5 locally too), 404 "This collection is no longer available, so a plan can't be saved.", network "Your plan could not be saved. Check your connection and try again." and, for removal, "Your plan could not be removed. Try again." Guests see "Saved in this browser only. Sign in to keep your plan on all your devices." After a save or removal the dialog closes and a persistent polite status near the button says "Test plan saved for Friday, November 6." or "Test plan removed.".
- **AC 3.23.6** [Anyone] **Given** I have a plan, **then** today's step is derived by one rule, the same for signed-in visitors and guests. "Today" is the browser's local calendar day (not UTC like the daily quiz, AC 3.13.2), days left is the number of local calendar days from today to the test date (unaffected by daylight-saving changes), and plans with days left below zero are not shown. Only completed Learn, Quiz and Test runs started from that collection (source kind collection and its id) count as activity; Flashcards and Warm up do not. **Learned before today** means the plan's `firstLearnAt` falls on a local day before today; **done today** means `lastActivityAt` falls on today's local day. Guest activity comes from `quizymode.guest.v1` and is lost when guest history is cleared or ages out (50 runs, 90 days). Steps by days left:

  | Days left | Step |
  |---|---|
  | 0 | **Test day.** "Test day. Good luck!" with an optional **Warm up** link to Flashcards. Never shows a done state. |
  | 1 | **Timed practice test**, opening Test mode (`/quiz/collections/{id}/{slug}?mode=test`), plus a **Learn first** link when not learned before today. |
  | 2 or more, not learned before today | **Learn the basics**, opening Learn. |
  | 2 to 7, learned before today | **Practice quiz** (a normal Quiz of the full set; misses go to the Review queue for signed-in visitors per AC 3.14.1). |
  | 8 or more, learned before today | **Rest day**: "Next step: {weekday, month day}. Nothing to do today." (the day 7 days before the test) with a **Review anyway** link to the Quiz. A run on a rest day does not make it done. |

  The Learn, Practice quiz and Timed practice test steps show **Done for today ✓** once any Learn or Quiz run of that collection (a Test run included, whichever link was used) finished today, with "Tomorrow: {next step}." (Learn the basics, Practice quiz, Timed practice test, Test day or Rest day, assuming today's step is done) and a **Practice again** link. The countdown reads "Test today", "Test tomorrow", "Test in N days" (2 to 7) or "Test on Fri, Nov 6" (more than 7). The time estimate reads "About N min" (at least 1): Learn uses 30 seconds for each of the first 50 questions, Practice quiz and Timed practice test 20 seconds for each of the first 500. A collection with no questions shows "Nothing to study yet" and "This collection has no questions yet." with an **Open** link to the collection page. The Test mode link is not told apart from a Quiz in the saved history, so any Learn or Quiz run of the collection counts for the practice-test day (known limitation).
- **AC 3.23.7** [Anyone] **Given** I open `/` and auth state has settled, **then** the **Today** lane (AC 3.0.20) shows one card per upcoming plan above the Daily quiz card: at most 3, ordered by test date then creation time, after dropping plans whose test date has passed; further plans appear as "+2 more test plans" or "+1 more test plan" (plain text). Each card names its collection and the countdown ("{collection} · Test in 3 days"), the step title, the detail line and an action label; the card is one link to the step's page, and cards with a second link (Learn first, Warm up, Review anyway, Practice again) keep it as a separate link, never nested. Signed-in Practice quiz text says "About N min. Misses go to your Review queue."; for guests "About N min. Quiz yourself on the full set.". A plan whose collection is gone or no longer visible shows "Test plan", "This collection is no longer available." and a **Remove plan** button (accessible name "Remove test plan for this collection") that deletes it. Guests look each plan's collection up through `GET /collections/{id}`: only an HTTP 404 makes the plan unavailable, any other failure hides that plan. A failing `GET /study-plans` hides only the plan cards and nothing is shown while auth is loading.
- **AC 3.23.8** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not signed in, **when** I save a plan, **then** it is stored in `localStorage` key `quizymode.study-plans.v1` as `{ version: 1, plans: [{ collectionId, testDate, createdAt }] }` (at most 5 plans, one per collection, at most 4 KiB, past plans dropped on every write); reads tolerate missing, corrupt, wrong-version or oversized data by returning no plans, and write failures are reported as a save error. **Clear guest history** (AC 3.0.17) does not remove it. Signed-in mode never reads this key except for the sign-in claim.
- **AC 3.23.9** [Authenticated] **Given** I have browser-local plans and sign in, **then** once per page session the plans are claimed silently, in a chain independent of the topic-interest and guest-history claims: plans whose test date has passed are skipped, a collection that already has a plan in my account keeps the account's date (the account wins), the rest are saved with `PUT /study-plans/{collectionId}` in stored order, a plan the server rejects with 400 or 404 is dropped, and the first `StudyPlans.LimitReached` ends the pass. The local document is removed afterwards, except when the plan list could not be read or a save failed with a network error or 5xx, in which case it is kept for the next page session. After the plan claim and the guest-history import have both settled, the plans query refetches.

### AC 3.24 Private picture storage and bounded normalization, backend only (#399)

First slice of the uploaded-picture epic (#398): the API can accept a picture, normalize it and keep it in a private object store, but no screen uses it yet; items can reference an asset since #400 (AC 3.25), with no screen attaching pictures yet (#401 review queue, #402 authoring UI, #403 rendering, #404 AI triage, #405 quotas and cleanup). Production storage is live (`S3`, AC 7.7); with no storage provider configured every media call answers 503 (AC 3.24.8).

- **AC 3.24.1** [Authenticated] **Given** storage is configured, **when** I `POST /media/images` as `multipart/form-data` with exactly one file part named `file`, **then** the API answers `201` with `{ id, state: "Ready", width, height, bytes, format: "webp", sha256, processingVersion, createdAt }` describing the **normalized** picture and a `Location` of `/media/images/{id}/content`. The multipart body is read by hand (no `IFormFile` binding): the single `file` part is copied into memory and never to disk, stopping at the limit plus one byte. **Given** the file is over 2,097,152 bytes (`Media:MaxSourceBytes`), **then** the answer is `413` with ProblemDetails title `Media.TooLarge`, whether the size is known from `Content-Length`, counted while reading (including chunked bodies) or reported by Kestrel's own request limit (`2 MiB + 64 KiB`), and no row is created. **Given** the body is not `multipart/form-data`, has no `file` part or has more than one, **then** the answer is `400 Media.InvalidRequest`. The file name and declared content type are ignored, never stored and never logged; a JPEG uploaded as `x.png` is accepted. Anonymous callers get `401`. (backend tests: UploadImageHttpTests)
- **AC 3.24.2** [Authenticated] The format is decided from magic bytes only: JPEG (`FF D8 FF`), PNG (`89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 0A`) or WebP (`RIFF....WEBP`); anything else (GIF, SVG, PDF, HTML, empty) is `400 Media.UnsupportedFormat`. A file whose bytes do not decode as the format its magic bytes claim, or that is truncated or corrupt, is `400 Media.Unreadable`. Animated pictures are `400 Media.Animated`: a PNG with an `acTL` chunk before the first `IDAT` (APNG), a WebP with the VP8X animation flag or an `ANIM`/`ANMF` chunk, or any codec reporting more than one frame. (backend tests: ImageNormalizerTests)
- **AC 3.24.3** [Authenticated] The size limits are checked from the image header **before any pixel is decoded**: a side above 8,192 px (`Media:MaxSide`) or more than 16,000,000 pixels (`Media:MaxPixels`) is `400 Media.TooManyPixels`, so a tiny file that declares 100000x100000 pixels is refused without allocating its pixel buffer (a test asserts that no decode stage runs). (backend tests: ImageNormalizerTests)
- **AC 3.24.4** [Authenticated] The stored picture is a new WebP made from the decoded pixels, never a copy of the upload: EXIF orientation is applied; the picture is converted to sRGB and encoded without a colour profile; EXIF, GPS, XMP, ICC, text chunks and any bytes after the image are dropped (a polyglot file keeps nothing of its payload and its hash differs from the upload's); the longest side is at most 1,280 px (`Media:MaxOutputSide`) and a smaller picture is never enlarged; transparency is kept. A PNG is first encoded losslessly and kept if it is at most 51,200 bytes (`Media:LosslessKeepBytes`), which keeps text and line art crisp; otherwise (and for JPEG and WebP) lossy WebP is tried at quality 82, 72, 62 and 52, then again at a longest side of 1,024 and 800 px, and the first result of at most 102,400 bytes (`Media:MaxOutputBytes`) wins; if none fits the answer is `400 Media.CannotOptimize`. The output is verified before it is stored: a RIFF/WebP made only of `VP8`, `VP8L`, `VP8X` (alpha flag only) and `ALPH` chunks, with no ICCP, EXIF, XMP or animation. Its SHA-256 is recorded and `processingVersion` is 1. Measured with the test fixtures (a 960x640 flag, a 1280x800 chart and a 1280x720 diagram, all PNG): 42,342 B, 13,994 B and 11,398 B, each normalized in under 100 ms. The 20 to 50 KiB size target of the issue is not enforced: only the 100 KiB cap is, and whether text and line art stay legible is a human review of the rendered fixtures. (backend tests: ImageNormalizerTests)
- **AC 3.24.5** [System] The original upload is **never persisted**: it is validated and normalized in memory and discarded, so there is no quarantine prefix. Each upload creates a `MediaAssets` row (id, owner user id, state `Processing`, `Ready` or `Failed`, object key, SHA-256, width, height, bytes, format `webp`, source format `jpeg`/`png`/`webp`, source bytes, processing version, failure code, created, updated and a nullable deleted timestamp) with check constraints (valid state, `Ready` needs an object key, `Failed` needs a failure code, at most 102,400 bytes) and a unique object key. A refused upload (any 400 or 413) creates no row. Logs carry the asset id, byte sizes, result codes and elapsed time only. (backend tests: UploadImageHttpTests, MediaAssetPostgresTests)
- **AC 3.24.6** [Owner, Admin] `GET /media/images/{id}/content` (route `{id:guid}`) streams the normalized WebP through the API, because the bucket is private; the browser fetches it with the bearer token and shows it from a blob URL. **Given** I own the asset or am an admin and it is `Ready`, **then** the answer is `200` with `Content-Type: image/webp` (constant, never taken from storage), `Content-Length`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store, no-transform`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; sandbox` and `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-site`. Anonymous callers get `401`. **Given** the asset belongs to someone else (and I am not an admin), does not exist, is `Processing`, `Failed` or withdrawn (`DeletedAt` set), **then** the answer is the same empty `404`, indistinguishable by status, body or content type. There is no metadata endpoint (the upload response carries the metadata), no delete endpoint and no item field. (backend tests: UploadImageHttpTests)
- **AC 3.24.7** [System] Objects are immutable: the key is `normalized/v1/{assetId:N}/{sha256}.webp` and is validated against that exact pattern before any storage call (both stores; the file store also checks the resolved path stays inside its root). Writes are conditional (`PutObject` with `If-None-Match: *`): `412` means the key already holds an object and counts as success without overwriting; `409 ConditionalRequestConflict` or any other failure is a storage failure. Order: validate and normalize in memory, insert the row as `Processing`, write the object, then update to `Ready` only `WHERE State = 'Processing' AND DeletedAt IS NULL`; a completion that finds the asset withdrawn changes nothing and answers `409 Media.Withdrawn`. After the insert the write and both updates ignore request cancellation (the write has its own 30 s timeout), so a client that disconnects still leaves a `Ready` or `Failed` row. A storage failure marks the row `Failed` (`Media.StorageFailed`) and answers `503`. Reading a `Ready` asset whose object is missing or denied (`NoSuchKey`, `AccessDenied`) answers `404` and logs a warning with the asset id only; the store interface has no delete. (backend tests: UploadImageHttpTests, MediaStorageTests)
- **AC 3.24.8** [System] The provider is `Media:Storage:Provider` (`Media__Storage__Provider`): `None` (default), `S3` or `FileSystem`. With `None` uploads and downloads answer `503 Media.StorageUnavailable`, nothing is written and the app stays healthy. The setting fails closed: `S3` with no `Media:S3:BucketName`, or with the bucket `quizymode-web` (the website bucket that deploys sync with `--delete`, compared case-insensitively), is treated as `None` and logs an error; `FileSystem` outside the Development and Testing environments is treated as `None` and logs an error; an unknown provider is `None`. The S3 client is only created when `S3` is accepted. (backend tests: MediaStorageTests)
- **AC 3.24.9** [Authenticated, System] Normalization is bounded. Decoding, resizing and encoding run under one gate of `Media:MaxConcurrentDecodes` (1) slots held until the work really finishes (even if the client has gone); at most `Media:MaxWaiters` (4) further requests may queue and any more get an immediate `503 Media.Busy`, as does a request that waits longer than `Media:SemaphoreWaitTimeout` (10 s); `Retry-After: 5` is sent with `Media.Busy`. The work has a total budget of `Media:ProcessingBudget` (20 s) checked between decode, resize and every encode attempt; over budget is `503 Media.ProcessingTimeout`. JPEG is decoded at reduced scale toward 1,280 px. Uploads are rate limited to 20 per hour per user (`media-uploads`, `429` with `Retry-After`); the limiter is in memory per API instance and durable per-account quotas belong to #405. Every size, pixel, time and concurrency limit is an option, not a constant. (backend tests: ImageNormalizerTests, UploadImageHttpTests)
- **AC 3.24.10** [Owner] Deviations from the issue and epic text, accepted for this slice: (1) no quarantine versus normalized split, because the original is never stored (AC 3.24.5); (2) one API principal both writes and reads `normalized/*` instead of separate writer and reader roles (AC 7.7.3); (3) pictures are served by the API proxy with an IAM user's access keys instead of CloudFront with Origin Access Control and OIDC (AC 7.7); (4) processing is synchronous in the request with a time bound (AC 3.24.9), not a background worker; (5) the 20 to 50 KiB target is not enforced (AC 3.24.4); (6) item picture fields and approval state arrived with #400 (AC 3.25); reference-aware reconciliation/cleanup/deletion now ship dark with scoped launch gates (AC 3.27.7); (7) shipped as its own pull request before the rest of epic #398. Production verification (upload, read back, private bucket) was done on the live `S3` provider (#399 evidence).

### AC 3.25 Uploaded item pictures: review, access gate and duplicates, backend only (#400)

Second slice of the uploaded-picture epic (#398): an item can reference pictures uploaded through AC 3.24, a revision-bound review decides who may see them, and every read path hides unapproved ones. No screen attaches pictures yet, so the API routes are live once deployed but nothing in the app calls them (#401 review queue and policy text, #402 authoring UI, #403 rendering, #405 withdraw action, quotas, cleanup, media rate limit and CDN). Storage is live in production (AC 7.7), so assets can exist, but until the authoring UI (#402) ships only direct API callers can attach one. Text-only and curated items behave exactly as before.

- **AC 3.25.1** [System] The `AddItemImageReview` migration adds to `Items`: `QuestionImageAssetId` and `ExplanationImageAssetId` (`uuid`, null, foreign key to `MediaAssets` with `ON DELETE RESTRICT`, indexed), `QuestionImageAttribution` and `ExplanationImageAttribution` (`varchar(300)`, null), `ImageReviewState` (`varchar(20)`, null, indexed; `PendingReview`, `Approved`, `Rejected`, `Withdrawn`, and the reserved `Processing` and `Failed`, added by the `AddProcessingFailedImageReviewStates` migration; only `Approved` is ever eligible for other people) and `ImageRevision` (`int`, default 0, never reset). For an upload the existing key column holds `media:{assetId:N}` (so the games exclusion and `excludedPictures` keep treating the item as a picture item) and the alt column holds the alt text. Check constraints: `CK_Items_QuestionImage_Asset` and `CK_Items_ExplanationImage_Asset` (asset id set exactly when the key is `'media:' || replace(assetId::text, '-', '')`; a curated or absent key never starts with `media:`), `CK_Items_ImageReviewState` (valid value) and `CK_Items_ImageReviewState_Assets` (a state exists exactly when at least one asset id is set). New table `ItemImageReviews` (`Id`, `ItemId` with no foreign key so the trail survives item deletion, `Revision`, `ContentHash` `char(64)`, question and explanation asset id and SHA-256, `SubmittedBy`, `SubmittedAt`, `Decision` (`Approved`, `Rejected`, `Superseded`; null while undecided), `DecidedBy`, `DecidedAt`, `Reason` (500), `PolicyVersion` (`pictures-v1`)) with a unique index on (`ItemId`, `Revision`). One owner asset may be attached to several of the owner's items and to both slots. (backend tests: ItemImageReviewPostgresTests)
- **AC 3.25.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /items` or `PUT /items/{id}`, **then** `questionImage` and `explanationImage` accept `{ assetId, altText, attribution? }` (alt text 1 to 300 characters without line breaks or control characters; attribution at most 300). A key, a URL or any other member besides `assetId`, `altText`, `attribution`, `longDescription` and `remove` (even next to a valid `assetId`), a missing `assetId` or (on POST) `remove` is a 400 naming the field. An asset that is unknown, owned by someone else, not `Ready` or withdrawn is the same 400 `Item.ImageAssetInvalid`, so ids cannot be probed; the asset must belong to the item's creator, so an admin can keep, remove or re-describe a creator's picture but cannot attach their own upload. On PUT an omitted or `null` property keeps the slot, `{ assetId, altText, attribution? }` sets or replaces it (sending the current `assetId` re-describes it) and `{ "remove": true }` clears it (not combinable with `assetId`). An explanation picture may be used without explanation prose. A repo-managed item, or a slot that holds a curated picture, rejects picture input with 400 `Item.ImagesManagedBySeed`. The POST response and the PUT response include `questionImage`, `explanationImage` and `imageReview: { state, revision, reason? }` (`null` without an upload; `reason` is the rejection reason of the current revision, only while `Rejected`); `GET /items/{id}` and, since #401 (AC 3.26.5), `GET /items` return `imageReview` to the creator and admins only. (backend tests: ItemImageReviewHttpTests)
- **AC 3.25.3** [System] The state machine runs inside the save transaction of POST and PUT. `ContentHash` is the SHA-256 of a versioned (`v1`) canonical JSON of the saved question, correct answer, incorrect answers (in order), explanation, question speech and, per slot, the picture SHA-256, alt text and attribution; category, keywords, source and group label are excluded. A save with an uploaded slot and no state becomes `PendingReview` with the next revision and a new `ItemImageReviews` row (the previous undecided row becomes `Superseded`); a save that changes the hash does the same from any state, including `Approved`, `Rejected` (the owner's re-submission) and by an admin; a save that points a slot at a different asset id is always a new submission, even when the new asset has identical bytes and the hash is unchanged (the review row records asset ids, so approval must name the asset that is attached); a save that leaves the hash and both asset ids unchanged keeps the state, so saving a `Rejected` item unchanged leaves it `Rejected`; a save that removes the last uploaded slot clears the state and increments the revision. The revision is claimed first with one conditional `UPDATE ... WHERE ImageRevision = N` that writes revision, state and both slots together; another edit that got there first leaves zero rows and the save is refused with 409 `Item.ImageReviewStale` (a unique violation on (`ItemId`, `Revision`) maps to the same 409). A decision that commits first is not an error for the edit: the edit claims the next revision and re-submits, and a decision that arrives after the edit is refused (AC 3.25.4), so a decision can never apply to content the reviewer did not see. (backend tests: ItemImageReviewHttpTests, ItemImageReviewPostgresTests)
- **AC 3.25.4** [Admin] **Given** I call `PUT /admin/items/{id}/image-review` with `{ decision: "approve" | "reject", expectedRevision, reason? }` (`reason` required when rejecting, at most 500), **then** one conditional `UPDATE ... WHERE ImageRevision = expectedRevision AND ImageReviewState = 'PendingReview'` sets `Approved` or `Rejected` and the matching `ItemImageReviews` row gets `Decision`, `DecidedBy`, `DecidedAt`, `Reason` and `PolicyVersion`, in one transaction, and the answer is 200 `{ id, imageReview: { state, revision, submittedBy, submittedAt, decidedBy, decidedAt, reason } }`. An admin may decide their own submission; nothing is implicit and `decidedBy` equal to `submittedBy` is recorded and visible. Repeating the same decision on the same revision is a 200 with no new row; a different decision, an older revision or a state that is no longer pending is 409 `Item.ImageReviewStale` with `currentRevision` and `currentState`. Approval also requires every attached asset to be `Ready`, not withdrawn, and to carry the SHA-256 recorded at submission, else 409 `Item.ImageAssetUnavailable`. A missing item or an item without an uploaded picture is 404; invalid bodies are 400; anonymous is 401 and non-admin is 403. The decision never changes `IsPrivate`, `ReadyForReview` or collections. (backend tests: ItemImageReviewHttpTests, ItemImageReviewPostgresTests for concurrent decisions and edit-versus-decision in both orders)
- **AC 3.25.5** [Anyone] `GET /items/{id}/images/{question|explanation}` (anonymous allowed) streams the picture through the API with the headers of AC 3.24.6 (`Cache-Control: private, no-store, no-transform`). The creator and admins can read any state; everyone else only when they can see the item (public, Invariant I5) **and** its pictures are `Approved`. `GET /collections/{collectionId}/items/{itemId}/images/{slot}` (anonymous allowed) answers when the collection is public (a share link is a public collection) or mine, the item is in it, and the picture is `Approved` or I created the item. Both require the asset to be `Ready` and not withdrawn. Every refusal, and every curated, empty or unknown slot, is the same empty 404; storage being off is 503 only after the caller is authorized. `GET /media/images/{id}/content` is unchanged (owner or admin only). Anonymous media reads have no dedicated rate limit until #405. (backend tests: ItemImageReviewHttpTests with the authorization matrix: anonymous, owner, unrelated user, collection viewer by public collection and share link, admin, against text-only, curated, `PendingReview`, `Approved`, `Rejected` and `Withdrawn` items)
- **AC 3.25.6** [Admin] The publish guards return 409 `Item.ImageReviewRequired` only for a **private to public** transition (or a new public item) whose resulting pictures are not `Approved`, evaluated against the saved values: `PUT /admin/items/{id}/approval` (AC 2.8.6), `PUT /items/{id}/visibility` with `isPrivate: false` (AC 2.7.5), and admin `POST /items` or `PUT /items/{id}` with `isPrivate: false` (so a private `Approved` item edited and published in one request, which re-enters `PendingReview`, is refused). An admin edit of an already public item is allowed and re-enters `PendingReview`; the item stays public-flagged but is hidden by AC 3.25.7. The guards are workflow policy; the eligibility gate is the security boundary, and a race that leaves a public item with pending pictures (for example `ApproveItem` landing between a check and a save) still shows nothing to others (tested).
- **AC 3.25.7** [Anyone] Media eligibility is `ImageReviewState` null or `Approved`, applied by `ItemVisibility` and at every item read: the central visible-to, accessible-by-id (admins still read by id), addable-to-collection and interactable-by (comments and ratings) predicates; `GET /items` including random and `collectionId` (a collection the caller can open still hides unapproved items from non-creators); the item export; the games roster; the daily quiz (candidate ranking and served items); the review queue (list, summary and answer); global search items and its discoverable-collection item counts; the featured sets and featured collection counts; the taxonomy tree counts, the category counts (`GET /categories`, raw SQL), keyword lists and counts and tag keyword lists; taxonomy search counts and the topic-suggestion profile queries; and the item counts of a collection (`GET /collections/{id}`, my collections, discover, bookmarks, study plans) plus the discover item-match filter. The filter is applied before sampling, ranking, paging and counting. Admin reports (content report, question quality, daily-quiz admin, review board) stay unfiltered. The owner's own pending item still shows to the owner everywhere the owner already sees it.
- **AC 3.25.8** [Authenticated, Admin] JSON export/import now follows AC 3.27.4: safe owned asset references round-trip without approval, and foreign picture loss requires explicit acknowledgement plus an omission manifest. Seed ingestion still rejects uploaded references.
- **AC 3.25.9** [Authenticated] Add duplicate identity uses the uploaded picture's bytes (AC 2.1.21): the same question with the same uploaded bytes is `Item.Duplicate`, the same question with different bytes, a curated key or no picture is not. Duplicate identity is scoped to the caller's own items, so another user's private picture hash never reveals its existence: the response is the same as for a picture nobody else has. (backend test: Duplicate_AnotherUsersPrivateHash_DoesNotRevealExistence)
- **AC 3.25.10** [Owner] Remaining intentional constraints: asset processing is synchronous (Ready or never attached), so nothing sets the item-level `Processing` and `Failed` states yet; they exist, are never eligible for other people, and the owner sees them as text ("Pictures: Processing", "Pictures: Failed" with an Edit to resubmit link); the picture review trail has no foreign key to Items and survives deletion; non-owner writes against hidden unapproved content return404. Withdrawn now has an asset-wide producer/audit, authenticated and anonymous delivery is rate limited, author uploads and `media:` rendering work, and scoped cleanup/report/advisory are defined in AC 3.27. Third-party collections show approved pictures like item text; rejected/pending items remain visible to their owner. Broad production cleanup, provider privacy acceptance, representative live policy coverage, actual IAM/cache/alert provisioning and issue-attached production evidence remain external gates. The games roster, taxonomy, keywords, discover, bookmarks and study-plan count routes are each covered by an HTTP test showing an unapproved picture item is omitted for a non-owner and still counted for its creator. (backend tests: PictureEligibilityRoutesHttpTests, GameRosterHttpTests.UnapprovedUploadedPictureItems_AreOmittedAndNotCountedForANonOwner)
- **AC 3.25.11** [Authenticated] **Given** I add or edit an item, **then** the Question picture slot sits beside the question and the Explanation picture slot sits in More options (which opens by itself on Edit when an explanation picture exists; an explanation picture may stand with empty explanation text). An empty slot's picker is labelled "Add picture" and a filled slot's is labelled "Replace" (each with the slot name for screen readers); "Remove picture" asks for confirmation and takes effect on save. The alt-text field explains that I should describe the visible evidence, such as colors, pattern and labels, without giving the answer, and not name the country or the thing being identified. If I save a relevant edit (question, answers, explanation, rationale, speech or a picture) to an item that already has uploaded pictures, an in-page confirmation dialog (`role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, focus moved into it and trapped, Escape or Cancel keeps editing and returns focus to Save) explains that the item is unavailable to other people, including through existing shares, until the new pictures are approved; Save changes then sends the update with the stored picture revision. A failed save keeps my text and both pictures. Save and add another clears both picture slots; Save and study saves the pictures on the private draft and opens it. (frontend tests: ImageSlotEditor.test.tsx, EditItemPage.pictures.test.tsx, CreateItemPage.pictures.test.tsx)

- **AC 3.26.1** [Admin] **Given** I call `GET /admin/items/image-review`, **then** anonymous callers get 401 and non-admins 403. The response is `{ items, totalCount, page, pageSize, totalPages, oldestSubmittedAt }`; each row has `id`, `category`, `isPrivate`, `readyForReview`, `question`, `correctAnswer`, `incorrectAnswers`, `explanation`, `createdBy`, `questionImage` and `explanationImage` (key, alt text, attribution), `ageSeconds` and `imageReview { state, revision, submittedBy, submittedByDisplay, submittedAt, decidedBy, decidedAt, reason }`. `page` defaults to 1 and `pageSize` to 25 (clamped to 1 to 100). `submittedByDisplay` is the submitter's email, else name, from one batched user lookup (null when unknown; the UI then shows the id). (backend tests: ImageReviewQueueHttpTests)
- **AC 3.26.2** [Admin] **Given** I pass `state` (`PendingReview` default, `Approved`, `Rejected`, `Withdrawn`, case-insensitive), **then** the queue lists the items in that picture review state, oldest submission of the item's current revision first (then by id), for every state; an unknown or numeric `state` is 400. The queue ignores the item's own review flag, privacy and visibility: a private item, a public item and an item that is not ready for review are all listed. An item without its audit row is still listed, first. `oldestSubmittedAt` is the minimum submission time over the whole filtered set, not only the page. Asset-wide withdrawal and retention/reconciliation now create current-revision Withdrawn audit rows with actor/reason; no old approval is restored (AC 3.27.5-7). The dashboard counts `PendingReview` only. (backend tests: ImageReviewQueueHttpTests)
- **AC 3.26.3** [Admin] **Given** I open **Admin → Review Board**, **then** an accessible tab list offers **Items** and **Pictures (n)** (the count appears once loaded; arrow keys, Home and End move between tabs; `?tab=pictures` opens the second tab). The Pictures tab shows "{n} picture submission(s) waiting for review", "Oldest submitted {age} ago", and a link to the picture policy (version `pictures-v1`, opens in a new tab). The Pictures tab is a bulk review grid (AC 3.26.9): pending pictures, oldest first, five per row and 25 per page. Each tile shows one picture (the question picture, else the explanation picture; uploaded pictures are fetched with my bearer token through object URLs that are revoked when the tile goes away), its question, who submitted it and how long ago, and a checkbox. A picture opens an in-page zoom dialog (focus is trapped, Esc, the backdrop and the close button dismiss it, focus returns to the picture). **Select all on this page** and **Deselect all** change the ticked set; a live count shows how many are ticked. A reason field (trimmed, 1 to 500 characters, shown to the owner) is required before **Reject selected (n)** is enabled; **Approve selected (n)** needs no reason. Both actions are disabled while a request is saving, and the saving button reads "Approving..." or "Rejecting...". After a decision the ticked set clears, a polite status reports the counts (for example "Approved 2."), and any picture that could not be applied (changed or unavailable) stays in the queue with the note "they are still in the queue." An empty queue says "No pictures waiting for review"; a load error offers retry. (frontend tests: ImageReviewBulkQueue.test.tsx, ReviewBoardPage.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.26.4** [Admin] **Given** another admin or the owner already changed the item, **when** my decision gets 409 `Item.ImageReviewStale` or 404, **then** the card shows "This item changed — reload", disables its actions and the reason box, refreshes the queue, and offers Reload; repeating the same decision at the same revision is a 200 no-op (treated as success), and two admins with conflicting decisions on one revision get exactly one 200 and one 409. A 409 `Item.ImageAssetUnavailable` on approve shows "A picture is no longer available; it can't be approved." and leaves Reject enabled; other failures show "Could not save your decision. Try again." and keep the typed reason. A picture that fails to load shows a Retry and a "Picture not loaded; reload it before deciding." hint, and Approve stays disabled until it loads. (backend tests: ImageReviewQueueHttpTests; frontend tests: ImageReviewQueue.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.26.5** [Owner/Admin] **Given** I am the item's creator or an admin, **then** `GET /items`, `GET /items/{id}` and the `PUT /items/{id}` response include `imageReview { state, revision, reason? }`, with `reason` (the current revision's rejection reason) only while `Rejected`; everyone else, including anonymous callers, other users reading the item through a collection (`collectionId`), and the daily quiz and games roster, get `imageReview` `null` and never the reason. The reasons for a page are loaded in one batched query. The item lists (My items on the Categories page, collection and keyword lists) show "Pictures: Pending review", "Pictures: Approved", "Pictures: Rejected — {reason}" with an "Edit to resubmit" link to `/items/{id}/edit`, or "Pictures: Withdrawn"; nothing is shown without a review state. (backend tests: ItemImageReviewHttpTests, DailyQuizTests; frontend tests: ImageReviewStatus.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.26.6** [Admin] **Given** pictures await review, **then** the admin overview shows the pending count and the age of the oldest submission (AC 5.10.2, 5.10.3). No response-time promise is made to authors or shown in the app. (backend tests: GetAnalyticsOverviewTests; frontend tests: AdminOverviewPanel.test.tsx)
- **AC 3.26.7** [Owner] The picture policy lives in `docs/picture-policy.md` ("Version: pictures-v1", changed together with `ItemImageReviewRules.PolicyVersion`). It lists the permitted and refused pictures, the education exceptions, and the "Suspected illegal material" runbook (reject immediately, withdraw, never screenshot, download or log the picture, escalate to the owner with the uploader's (item creator's) account; the owner files the mandatory report under 18 U.S.C. 2258A and preserves the evidence; who decided, when and which policy version are recorded on the audit row). The audience is school-appropriate for ages 13+. The refuse list includes self-harm, drug and weapon instructions and profanity, and the policy names the resubmission and appeal path. The owner approved `pictures-v1` on 2026-11-06 (recorded in the policy file).
- **AC 3.26.8** [Owner] Deviations accepted for this slice: (1) the queue loads only the first page (25) and says so; deciding items loads the next ones; (2) the owner status line is rendered by the shared item list card, which serves the item lists on the Categories page, collections and keywords; `MyItemsActions.tsx` is unused and left alone; (3) `submittedBy` and `createdBy` are user ids, with a display name added only for the submitter; (4) the policy link opens GitHub, so it 404s until the policy file is merged to `main`; (5) an admin gets `imageReview` from `GET /items` only for items the list's read gate shows them (their own and approved ones); other users' pending or rejected items are reachable through the queue and `GET /items/{id}`; (6) the owner status line shows the full rejection reason, with "Edit to resubmit" on its own line.
- **AC 3.26.9** [Admin] **Given** I tick pictures on the Pictures tab and submit, **when** I call `POST /admin/items/image-review/bulk` with `{ decision, items: [{ id, expectedRevision }], reason? }` (1 to 25 unique items; a reject needs a reason, max 500), **then** each item is decided exactly as `PUT /admin/items/{id}/image-review` decides it (same revision check, same asset checks on approve, same no-op when repeated). The answer is 200 with `{ applied, failed, results: [{ id, applied, state, revision, errorCode }] }`, one outcome per item; one stale or unavailable item does not stop the others. Anonymous callers get 401 and non-admins 403. An invalid body answers 400 and changes nothing. The endpoint does not publish or approve any item (AC 3.25.6). (tests: ItemImageReviewHttpTests Bulk_*, ImageReviewBulkQueue.test.tsx)

### AC 3.28 Durable picture upload limits and kill switch (#405, partial; consolidated into MediaIngress/MediaAdmission)

- **AC 3.28.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I `POST /media/images`, **then** limits are counted from the database (so they hold across restarts and instances) by `MediaIngress` before the body is parsed and by `MediaAdmission` before decoding. A request is refused with `429` ProblemDetails when: I made `Media:MaxUploadsPerHour` (20) or `Media:MaxUploadsPerDay` (20) upload admissions in the rolling window, or my reserved transport bytes exceed `Media:MaxIngressBytesPerDay` (title `Media.UploadQuota`); `Media:MaxPendingReviewItems` (20) of my items are `PendingReview` (`Media.PendingReviewQuota`); or my retained normalized bytes of non-purged assets, plus the maximum output of in-flight uploads, would exceed `Media:MaxRetainedBytes` (20 MiB; `Media.StorageQuota`). Withdrawn assets keep counting until the cleanup worker purges them. No asset row is created for a refusal. (backend tests: MediaUploadLimitsTests, MediaIngressTests)
- **AC 3.28.2** [System] Global ceilings apply to everyone: `Media:MaxUploadsGlobalPerDay` (5000 admissions per rolling 24 hours) answers `429 Media.CapacityReached` with `Retry-After: 3600`; `Media:MaxGlobalRetainedBytes` (1 GiB, including reservations) answers `429 Media.GlobalStorageQuota`; `Media:MaxGlobalUploads` and `Media:MaxGlobalIngress` (8) bound concurrent work (`Media.GlobalBusy`). (backend tests: MediaUploadLimitsTests)
- **AC 3.28.3** [System] Quota races are closed: every count and the insert of the new admission row run in one transaction under the shared PostgreSQL advisory lock (`pg_advisory_xact_lock(398405)`), so concurrent requests never admit more than the limit. The former `MediaQuota` after-insert recount is retired. (backend test: Upload_ConcurrentRequests_NeverAdmitMoreThanTheDailyLimit)
- **AC 3.28.4** [Owner] Kill switch: `Media__UploadsEnabled=false` makes `POST /media/images` answer `503 Media.Disabled` before any read or write; reads of existing pictures are unaffected. The retired #405 option names `Media__MaxUploadsPerUserPerDay`, `MaxRetainedBytesPerUser`, `MaxPendingReviewsPerUser` and `MaxRetainedBytesGlobal` remain as deprecated aliases of `MaxUploadsPerDay`, `MaxRetainedBytes`, `MaxPendingReviewItems` and `MaxGlobalRetainedBytes`. An invalid value fails startup. The code never reads or logs picture content. **The release ships with uploads off: `Media__UploadsEnabled=false` must be set in production before the deploy and stays until the owner approves the policy and the launch checklist.** (backend tests: Upload_KillSwitchOff_Returns503Disabled, DeprecatedOptionNames_AreAliasesOfTheEnforcedOptions)
- **AC 3.28.5** [Owner] Still open under #405 (not in this slice): per-account admin overrides and the AI-call quota; cleanup and lifecycle jobs, orphan reconciliation and IAM delete/list permissions; report-image and admin withdrawal with delivery-cache bounds; metrics, alerts and logging audit; the remaining integrated tests; and the release checklist with production evidence.

### AC 3.27 Answer rationale per option (#367)

- **AC 3.27.1** [Owner/Admin] **Given** I create or edit an item (`POST /items`, `PUT /items/{id}`, or a bulk item), **then** I may send `incorrectAnswerRationale`, an object keyed by incorrect answer index (`"0"` to `"3"`), with a short "why this option is wrong" text per option (max 200 characters, trimmed). Blank entries and indexes outside the incorrect answers are dropped; a key beyond the answer count or a text over 200 characters is a 400 naming `IncorrectAnswerRationale`. Omitting it, or sending `null`, stores none (PUT replaces the whole map). The correct answer keeps `explanation`. The response and `GET /items`, `GET /items/{id}`, the admin review board and export return the map only when it has entries. (backend tests: IncorrectAnswerRationaleHelperTests)
- **AC 3.27.2** [Anyone] **Given** I answer a Quiz or Learn question wrongly and the answer is revealed, **then** the rationale for my chosen option (when it has one) is shown under that option. A correct answer or an option without a rationale shows nothing extra. (frontend tests: QuizRenderer, LearnQuestion, utils/incorrectAnswerRationale.test.ts)
- **AC 3.27.3** [Anyone] **Given** I open the Test "Review all" (or "Review mistakes") screen, **then** a wrong answer I gave shows its rationale under "Your answer", and each incorrect option that has a rationale is listed under the question as "Option: rationale", so every option is covered. (frontend tests: QuizCompletionView)
- **AC 3.27.4** [Admin/Owner] **Given** I open the item form, **then** each incorrect answer has an optional "Why this is wrong" field (max 200), kept with that answer when blank answers are removed; the Edit page loads the saved rationale.
- **AC 3.27.5** [Owner] **Given** a study guide section is generated (paste or upload), **then** the prompt asks for an optional `incorrectAnswerRationale` object keyed by incorrect answer text (max 200 characters each, based only on the section text). The parser keeps a rationale only when its key matches an incorrect answer (case-insensitive); unmatched keys and non-string values are ignored and the question is still kept. Rationales are stored per candidate (`IncorrectAnswerRationaleJson`, prompt version 2, so cached version-1 generations regenerate). Moderation checks the rationale text too. (backend tests: StudyGuideRationaleTests)
- **AC 3.27.6** [Owner] **Given** I accept study guide candidates, **then** each item carries the rationale of its incorrect answers by answer text; an incorrect answer edited so its text no longer matches the generated text loses its rationale. (backend tests: StudyGuideRationaleTests.MapRationale_*)
- **AC 3.27.7** [Owner] **Given** I paste or upload JSON items on `/items/upload-to-collection`, **then** an optional `incorrectAnswerRationale` object keyed by incorrect answer text (case-insensitive) is saved as the per-option rationale; unmatched keys are ignored. (frontend tests: utils/rationaleByAnswerText.test.ts)
- **AC 3.27.8** [Owner] Gaps accepted for this slice: (1) seed JSON files in the repo are unchanged; the seed format and seed sync accept an optional `incorrectAnswerRationale` (keyed by index), and it is applied as a changed field; (2) the review board list shows the rationale in the API response only, not in the admin UI; (3) existing items have no rationale until edited; (4) the term-pair import (`/create/import`) builds distractors from other terms and does not write rationales.

---

## 4. Users and auth

Authentication uses **email + password** with secure password hashing and short-lived access tokens, or **Continue with Google** (AUTH-003, #118) where that is enabled for the deployment (`VITE_GOOGLE_SIGNIN_ENABLED`; off unless the Cognito/Google setup in `docs/infra/COGNITO_SETUP.md` is complete).
