- **AC 5.11.1** **Given** I submit the feedback form, **then** the submission starts with status `New` and stores the current analytics session id (optional, at most 128 characters) so an admin can see the journey that led to the report.
- **AC 5.11.2** [Admin] **Given** I call `GET /admin/feedback`, **then** submissions are returned newest first, paged (`page`, `pageSize` 1-100), filterable by `type`, `status` (New, Reviewed, Archived), `from`/`to` UTC dates (inclusive), and `search` over details, page URL, and emails; the response includes the total and the count of `New` submissions. An invalid status or paging value returns **400**. `GET /admin/feedback/{id}` returns one submission (**404** when missing).
- **AC 5.11.3** [Admin] **Given** I call `PUT /admin/feedback/{id}/status` with `New`, `Reviewed`, or `Archived`, **then** the status persists; any other value returns **400** and an unknown id **404**.
- **AC 5.11.4** Every admin feedback endpoint returns **403** for non-admins (and **401** when unauthenticated). Contact emails (signed-in user email and the optional email typed into the form) are returned only by these endpoints.
- **AC 5.11.5** [Admin] **Given** I open `/admin/feedback`, **then** I see submissions with type, status, details, page URL, user and contact email, user agent, and time; I can filter by type, status (default New), date range, and text search; mark a submission New, Reviewed, or Archived; and show the session journey when a session id exists. The `/admin` dashboard shows the count of `New` submissions on the Feedback card. Loading, empty, and error states are shown.

### AC 5.12 Admin: AI providers (#202)

**API**

- **AC 5.12.1** [Admin] **Given** I am an admin, **when** I call `GET /admin/llm-providers`, **then** the API returns 200 with `vendors` (Gemini, Groq, Cerebras, OpenRouter, OpenAI, in that order, each with `vendor`, `keyConfigured`, `model`, and `coolingDownUntilUtc`: set while the vendor is skipped after an HTTP 429, AC 6.6.33, else null), `routes` (`free` and `premium`: ordered vendor names), `monthlySectionBudget` (config `StudyGuideGeneration:MonthlySectionBudget`, default 1000), and `monthlySectionsUsed` (section generations queued this UTC month, AC 6.6.10). On first read the settings are seeded with models Gemini `gemini-3.5-flash-lite`, Groq `openai/gpt-oss-120b`, Cerebras `gpt-oss-120b`, OpenRouter `google/gemma-4-31b-it:free`, OpenAI `gpt-5-nano` (stored as one vendor→model JSON map; a vendor missing from a saved map uses its default), free route Gemini → Groq → Cerebras → OpenRouter → OpenAI, and premium route OpenAI → Gemini → Cerebras → Groq → OpenRouter. DeepSeek was retired in #464: it is not a vendor, a saved route that still names it loads without it, and historical `LlmAttempts` rows with `vendor = DeepSeek` still appear in the attempts and usage reports.
- **AC 5.12.2** [Admin] **Given** I am an admin, **when** I call `PUT /admin/llm-providers` with `{ vendors: [{ vendor, model }], routes: { free, premium } }`, **then** the API validates it (400 with validation errors otherwise): each vendor is a known vendor (case-insensitive) listed at most once; each model is 1–100 characters matching `^[A-Za-z0-9._:\-/]+$`; each route lists 1–5 distinct known vendors (`DeepSeek` is rejected as unknown). Valid input updates the listed vendors' models and both routes and returns 200 with the same shape as GET; vendors not listed keep their model.
- **AC 5.12.3** [Admin] **Given** I am an admin, **when** I call `GET /admin/llm-providers/attempts?days=N` (`days` 1–90, default 7), **then** the API returns `{ attempts: [{ createdUtc, vendor, model, outcome, inputTokens, outputTokens, durationMs }] }`, newest first, at most 200 rows; `outcome` is `Success`, `ProviderError`, `Timeout`, `ContentFilter`, `LowQuoteYield`, `InvalidJson`, `RateLimited` (HTTP 429), or `AuthError` (the vendor rejected our key: HTTP 401; HTTP 403 whose body mentions an API key, authentication or credentials; or HTTP 400 with "API key" in the body; a 403 without such wording, for example a region or model-access block, is a `ProviderError`, and rows recorded before this rule may show `AuthError` for any 403). Each row also carries `httpStatus` (the vendor's HTTP status for every reply that was received, including 200 replies that turn out unusable; null for a timeout, a network failure, a missing key, or rows recorded before this field existed).
- **AC 5.12.4** [Authenticated (non-admin) / Anonymous] Every `/admin/llm-providers*` endpoint returns 403 for authenticated non-admins and 401 for anonymous callers.
- **AC 5.12.5** [System] Vendor API keys come only from configuration / environment (`Gemini:ApiKey` or `GEMINI_API_KEY`, `Groq:ApiKey` or `GROQ_API_KEY`, `Cerebras:ApiKey` or `CEREBRAS_API_KEY`, `OpenRouter:ApiKey` or `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`, `OpenAI:ApiKey` or `OPENAI_API_KEY`); they are never stored in the database, logged (the `Authorization` header is redacted from HTTP logs), or returned — the API only reports `keyConfigured`. The body of an HTTP 401 reply and of a 400 `AuthError` reply is never logged (only the vendor and HTTP status; some vendors, such as OpenAI, echo a masked key fragment there). An HTTP 403 reply (whether classified `AuthError` or `ProviderError`) and every other failed reply log a single-line excerpt of at most 300 characters. Key-shaped text is redacted from the whole body before the excerpt is cut, and replaced with `[redacted]`. The field or header name, its `:` or `=` separator and any surrounding quotes are kept. The following are redacted:
  - `sk-…` keys (including `sk-proj-…`) at the start of a word;
  - `sk-…` stuck to a preceding letter or digit, when at least 20 key characters follow and one of the first 48 is a digit;
  - `sk_live_…`, `sk_test_…`, `rk_live_…` and `rk_test_…` keys;
  - `AIza…`;
  - runs of `*` followed by 3–6 letters or digits;
  - Bearer tokens (subject to the token-shape rule below);
  - the value of an `Authorization`, `x-goog-api-key`, `x-api-key`, `api-key` / `api_key` / `apikey` / `api key` or `access_token` / `access-token` / `accessToken` header or bare `name: value` / `name=value` pair, with or without a `Bearer`, `Basic`, `Token`, `Digest` or `ApiKey` scheme;
  - the values of the `api-key`, `api_key`, `apikey`, `x-api-key`, `x-goog-api-key`, `key`, `token`, `access_token`, `authorization`, `secret` and `password` JSON fields, whether double-quoted (including escaped quotes inside the value), single-quoted or a number, and the same fields when the JSON is echoed inside an escaped string;
  - a field string value that the vendor cut off before its closing quote, up to the end of the body;
  - the values of the `key`, `api_key`, `apikey`, `token` and `access_token` URL query parameters (including HTML-encoded `&amp;key=`).
  - Token-shape rule: so that ordinary error text stays readable, a Bearer token, and a header or bare value that follows `:` without quotes, are redacted only when they look like a token: 16 or more characters, or a digit within the first 15 characters, or a symbol inside the value, or a lowercase letter followed by an uppercase letter. For example, "Bearer token expired" and "authorization: required for this endpoint" are logged unchanged. A value after `=`, or a quoted value (up to its closing quote, including spaces and escaped quotes), is always redacted.
  - Known limits (not redacted): JSON object or array values of secret fields, a short letters-only value after an unquoted `api_key:`, mixed quotes such as `{'api_key': "x"}`, bare `password=` / `secret:` / `token=`, and unknown schemes such as `Authorization: Key <token>`. Known over-redaction: prose such as "authorization: 3 attempts", "API key: 2 keys max", "Bearer 2fa required", "authorization: re-authenticate" (a symbol inside a word) or a 16+ letter word after `authorization:`; `disk_test_validation`-style text (`sk_test_` / `rk_live_` shapes with no word boundary); and an unterminated quote after a header name, which redacts to the end of the body.
- **AC 5.12.6** [Admin] **Given** I am an admin, **when** I call `GET /admin/llm-providers/usage?days=N` (`days` 1–90, default 30; outside that range or not an integer → 400, unlike the attempts endpoint which clamps), **then** the API returns 200 `{ days, sinceUtc, rows, topUsers }`. `rows` has one entry per (vendor, model) with at least one `LlmAttempts` row created since `sinceUtc`, ordered by vendor then model (ordinal, case-sensitive): `{ vendor, model, attempts, successes, inputTokens, outputTokens, avgDurationMs, estimatedCostUsd }`. `successes` counts `Success` outcomes; token sums include failed attempts (missing tokens count as 0) and are 64-bit; `avgDurationMs` is the rounded mean. `topUsers` lists up to 20 users, ordered by charged sections then total tokens (both descending), then user id ascending (ordinal, case-sensitive), from `StudyGuideQuotaCharges` created since `sinceUtc` (refunded charges no longer exist and do not count): `{ userId, chargedSections, inputTokens, outputTokens }`, user id only. `chargedSections` is the sum of charge costs; a user's tokens are the recorded tokens of the generations they were charged for, counted once per generation, from the kept attempt only (failed-vendor tokens appear only in `rows`; a generation not yet completed counts 0 tokens). Users who only attached to an existing generation for free are not listed. Attempts are kept for `AttemptRetentionDays` (default 90), which can shorten the effective window. [System] Totals are aggregated in the database: one grouped query over `LlmAttempts` returns one row per (vendor, model), and one grouped query over `StudyGuideQuotaCharges` (grouped per user and generation, left-joined to `SectionGenerations`, then grouped per user) returns one row per user. Individual attempt and charge rows are not loaded into the API. `avgDurationMs` is the summed duration divided by the attempt count, rounded midpoint-away-from-zero. Ordering, the top-20 cut and estimated cost are applied in the API after aggregation.
- **AC 5.12.7** [Admin] **Given** a model has prices configured as `LlmPricing:{model}:InputPerMTok` and `LlmPricing:{model}:OutputPerMTok` (USD per million tokens; put them in appsettings/JSON, since environment-variable keys cannot hold names with `-` and `.` comfortably), **then** `estimatedCostUsd` is `round(inputTokens / 1,000,000 × input price + outputTokens / 1,000,000 × output price, 4)` using decimal arithmetic, midpoints rounded away from zero. Both prices are required and must parse as non-negative invariant-culture decimals (only `.` as the decimal separator); otherwise `estimatedCostUsd` is null. A model name containing `:` can never be priced. No prices ship by default (`"LlmPricing": {}`). Free-tier vendors are always `0` without configuration (#464): every Groq and Cerebras model, and OpenRouter models ending in `:free`.

**UI**

- **AC 5.12.10** [Admin] **Given** I am on the Admin Dashboard, **when** I click **AI Providers**, **then** I am taken to `/admin/ai-providers`; non-admins are redirected home.
- **AC 5.12.11** [Admin] **Given** the AI providers page loads, **then** I see the monthly section budget ("X of Y sections generated this month", with a labeled progress bar) and a vendors table listing Gemini, Groq, Cerebras, OpenRouter, and OpenAI with a text badge **Key configured** / **No key** (keys are never shown), a "Cooling down until <local time>" note while a vendor's `coolingDownUntilUtc` is in the future (it disappears at expiry without reloading the tab), and a labeled **Model for <vendor>** input. While the response contains cooldown timestamps, the page refreshes settings every 15 seconds to pick up extensions or cleared cooldowns; background refreshes, including failures, preserve unsaved model and route edits. Each route editor allows up to five vendors.
- **AC 5.12.12** [Admin] **Given** the **Free route** and **Premium route** editors, **when** I reorder vendors, **then** each entry has labeled **Up** / **Down** / **Remove** buttons (keyboard-operable; Up is disabled on the first entry and Down on the last) and an **Add vendor to <route>** select for vendors not yet in the route; vendors without a key are marked "(no key, skipped)". When the saved route is non-empty and none of its vendors has a key, a `role="alert"` message under the route heading reads "No vendor in this route has a key; generation for free users is off." (or "premium users"); it follows the saved route only (unsaved edits do not toggle it) and updates after saving, and an empty route keeps the "No vendors in this route." line. **Save settings** validates with Zod (model 1–100 characters matching `^[A-Za-z0-9._:\-/]+$`, each route 1–5 distinct known vendors) and shows the first error (prefixed with the vendor for model errors); valid settings are sent with `PUT /admin/llm-providers` (models trimmed) and the page shows "AI provider settings saved."
- **AC 5.12.13** [Admin] **Given** the **Recent attempts** table, **when** I choose a window (last day, 7 days, 30 days), **then** it lists `GET /admin/llm-providers/attempts?days=N` rows newest first with time, vendor, model, outcome as text ("Auth error (check key)" for `AuthError`, "Rate limited (429)" for `RateLimited`; unknown values such as historical outcomes are shown as-is), an **HTTP** status column ("—" when none), input / output tokens, and duration.
- **AC 5.12.14** [Admin] **Given** the AI providers page, **then** below **Recent attempts** a **Usage** section (admin only) has a labeled **Usage window** select (Last day, Last 7 days, Last 30 days — the default, Last 90 days) and loads `GET /admin/llm-providers/usage?days=N` (AC 5.12.6) on every change without flashing the empty state. A "By vendor and model" table shows Vendor, Model, Attempts, Success rate (rounded %), Tokens in / out, Avg duration (ms), and Est. cost (`$x.xxxx`, or "n/a" with the tooltip "No price configured for this model"). A "Top users" table (note: "Top 20 users by charged sections in this window.") shows User ID, Charged sections, and Tokens in / out, or "No charged sections in this window." when empty. While loading it shows a spinner; on failure "Failed to load usage" with a retry button; with no rows, "No vendor calls in this window." replaces both tables. Tables scroll horizontally inside their container on narrow screens.
- **AC 5.12.15** [Admin] **Given** I open `/admin/ai-providers` at a 375px-wide viewport, **then** the page itself does not scroll sideways (`document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= 375`). The Vendors, Recent attempts and Usage tables scroll inside their own containers, the Free and Premium route editors stack in one column, and each route entry wraps its Up / Down / Remove buttons onto their own line instead of overflowing.


- **AC 5.12.16** [System] **Given** a study-guide generation tries a vendor, **then** exactly one `StudyGuideGeneration: {GenerationId} attempt with {Vendor} ({Model}) ended {Outcome}; HTTP {HttpStatus}, {DurationMs} ms, tokens in {InputTokens} out {OutputTokens}.` line is logged for that attempt, with the same values as its `LlmAttempts` row (AC 5.12.3); `none` and `n/a` replace missing values. The level is Information for `Success`, `LowQuoteYield` and `ContentFilter`, and Warning for `ProviderError`, `Timeout`, `InvalidJson` and `AuthError`. A failed vendor reply still logs its redacted excerpt on its own line (AC 5.12.5).
- **AC 5.12.17** [System] **Given** a generation's route has vendors without a configured key, **then** one Warning line per generation names the skipped vendors (`skipped vendor(s) without a key in the {Tier} route: {Vendors}`). It is logged even when another vendor in the route still serves the generation, so a missing key is not mistaken for a vendor outage. A completion line names the vendor and model that delivered the questions.
- **AC 5.12.18** [System] Production log levels live in the `Serilog` section of `appsettings.json` (`Serilog:MinimumLevel`), with a default of Information. These categories are Warning: `Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command`, `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Hosting.Diagnostics`, `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Cors`, `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc`, `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Routing`, `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Http.Result`, `Microsoft.AspNetCore.Connections` and `Microsoft.Extensions.Diagnostics.HealthChecks`. `Microsoft.AspNetCore.DataProtection` is Error. `Microsoft.AspNetCore` and the app's own categories stay at Information, so auth diagnostics remain. The user-upsert middleware never logs an email or a display name: its claims warning lists claim types only, and its upsert Debug line logs the subject only (#476, #490). The `Logging:LogLevel` section was removed because it had no effect (AC 5.12.20).
- **AC 5.12.19** [System] **Given** a request completes, **then** exactly one Information access line is written by `UseSerilogRequestLogging` with the method, path (no query string), status code, elapsed time and the request id from the logging scope. Status 400 to 499 logs at Warning. Status 500 and above, or an unhandled exception, logs at Error. `/health` and `/alive` are not logged. The query string is never logged (#490).
- **AC 5.12.20** [System] `UseSerilog` replaces the Microsoft logging pipeline. The `ILoggerFactory` the app resolves is Serilog's, so `Logging:LogLevel` has no effect, and the OpenTelemetry logging provider added by ServiceDefaults receives no application logs. Application logs reach Grafana Loki through the Loki sink. Restoring logs in the Aspire dashboard needs a Serilog OTLP sink, a separate change (#490). Regression test: `LoggingProviderTests`.
- **AC 5.12.21** [System] **Given** a study-guide generation skips an optional vendor because it has no key, **then** the `skipped vendor(s) without a key` line is Debug and does not appear in production logs. **Given** no vendor in the route has a key, **then** the route-level Warning is written and the generation fails (#490).

## 6. Study guides and import

### AC 6.1 to 6.4 Legacy study guide pipeline (retired in 3.101.0, #296)

**Retired in 3.101.0 (#296):** the legacy single-study-guide pipeline was removed together with its tables (`StudyGuides`, `StudyGuideImportSessions`, `StudyGuideChunks`, `StudyGuidePromptResults`, `StudyGuideDedupResults`, `StudyGuideUploads`), the `StudyGuideMaxBytes` per-user setting and the `/study-guide` and `/study-guide/import` pages. New study guides use the document flow (AC 6.6). The former ACs are retired:

- **AC 6.1.1-6.1.11** **Retired (#296):** `GET`/`PUT`/`DELETE /study-guides/current`, the per-user byte limit, the 14-day guide expiry and the Study Guide screen (6.1.6, 6.1.7).
- **AC 6.2.1-6.2.7** **Retired (#296):** `POST /study-guides/import/sessions`, `GET .../{id}`, `.../generate-chunks`, the owner check and the import wizard.
- **AC 6.3.1-6.3.6** **Retired (#296):** `.../chunks/{chunkIndex}/result`, `.../dedup-result` and the prompt copy/paste screens (including 6.3.1a).
- **AC 6.4.1-6.4.3** **Retired (#296):** `.../finalize` and the **Finalize import** step.
- **AC 6.4.4** [Anyone] **Given** I call any retired legacy endpoint (`GET`, `PUT` or `DELETE /study-guides/current`; `POST /study-guides/import/sessions`; `GET /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}`; `POST` to `.../{id}/generate-chunks`, `.../{id}/chunks/{chunkIndex}/result`, `.../{id}/dedup-result` or `.../{id}/finalize`), anonymous or signed in, **then** the API returns 404 Not Found; `GET /study-guides/documents` is unaffected. The OpenAPI document (`docs/openapi/quizymode-api.json`) has no `/study-guides/current` or `/study-guides/import` paths and no `StudyGuides` or `StudyGuideImport` tags. (backend tests: LegacyStudyGuideRoutesRemovedHttpTests)
