### AC 6.6 Study guide documents (v2, #203)

Upload a PDF, Word (`.docx`), PowerPoint (`.pptx`), or text / Markdown study guide (or paste text). The API extracts the text, splits it into sections, and on request generates up to 20 source-quoted multiple-choice questions per section through a multi-vendor LLM router; the owner reviews them and saves the chosen ones as private items in a link-shared collection. The original file is never stored — only its SHA-256, the SHA-256 of the normalized text, and the extracted section text, which is kept until 30 days after the last activity. Generations are a shared, system-owned cache keyed by (section text hash, mode `GuideOnly`, prompt version), so identical text is generated once for everyone. All endpoints require sign-in and are owner-only (404 for other users' documents); JSON is camelCase.

**API** (#198, #199, #202)

- **AC 6.6.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I am signed in, **when** I call `GET /study-guides/documents/config`, **then** the API returns `enabled` (true when at least one vendor in my tier's saved route, free or premium, has an API key configured, AC 5.12.1 and 5.12.5), `maxFileBytes` (20 MB), `maxPages` (300), `maxTextBytes` (1,048,576), `acceptedExtensions` (`.pdf`, `.docx`, `.pptx`, `.txt`, `.md`), `freeTierDelayMinutes` (10), `quickPathMaxSections` (3, the "Make a quiz" cap, AC 6.6.29), and my `quota` (`{ tier, dailyLimit, dailyUsed, monthlyLimit, monthlyUsed, remainingToday }`, AC 6.6.8). Limits come from the `StudyGuideDocuments` and `StudyGuideGeneration` configuration sections.
- **AC 6.6.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I am signed in, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/documents` as `multipart/form-data` with exactly one of `file` or `text` (at most `maxTextBytes`), plus `category`, `navigationKeyword1`, `navigationKeyword2` (a valid taxonomy path), optional `title` (≤ 200 characters; default = the file name without extension, else the first line of the text), and optional `keywords` (repeated and/or comma-separated; at most 10, letters/numbers/hyphens, ≤ 30 characters), **then** the API extracts and sections the text, stores the document and its sections, and returns 201 with the `Document` (sections with status `New`, `isDuplicate = false`, my quota). Neither or both of `file`/`text` → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.FileOrTextRequired`; field validation failures → 400; an invalid category or path → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.InvalidCategory` / `InvalidNavigationPath`; blocked language in the title, keywords, or extracted text → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.ProfanityBlocked` (nothing stored). Anonymous → 401. A body larger than 21 MB is rejected (413); more than 20 creates per user per hour → 429 "Too many study guide uploads".
- **AC 6.6.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I upload a file, **then** its type is detected from its bytes, not its name: `%PDF` → PDF; a zip containing `word/document.xml` → DOCX, or `ppt/presentation.xml` → PPTX; otherwise valid UTF-8 text without NUL bytes → text (`fileType` = `txt` for `.txt`, `.md`, and pasted text). Anything else → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.UnsupportedType`. Files over 20 MB or zips that would expand beyond 100 MB → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.FileTooLarge`; more than 300 pages/slides → 400 `TooManyPages`; extracted text over 1 MiB → 400 `TextTooLarge`; no text → 400 `Empty`; damaged or password-protected files → 400 `Unreadable`; extraction longer than 30 s → 400 `ExtractionTimeout`. Parsing is read-only and never runs macros.
- **AC 6.6.4** [Authenticated] **Given** a PDF where more than half of the pages have fewer than 40 non-whitespace characters, **then** the API returns 400 `StudyGuideDocument.Scanned` ("This looks like a scan; only PDFs with selectable text are supported for now."). For PDFs with at least 3 pages, lines that repeat (digits ignored) on at least 60% of pages are removed as headers/footers, and bare page-number lines ("12", "Page 3 of 10") are always removed.
- **AC 6.6.5** [System] Sections are at most 10,240 UTF-8 bytes, packed toward 8,192: PDFs pack whole pages and PPTX whole slides (an oversized page is split at paragraph, then line, then word boundaries), each page starting with a marker line `[p. N]` / `[slide N]`; DOCX splits at Heading 1–3 / Title paragraphs, then paragraphs; text splits at Markdown headings, then blank lines. Each section has `index`, `label` (`p. 3`, `pp. 12–14`, `Slide 4`, `Slides 4–6`, or the heading text ≤ 120 characters, else `Section N`), `pageStart` / `pageEnd` (null for DOCX/text), `sizeBytes`, and a SHA-256 of its normalized text (Unicode NFC, whitespace runs collapsed to one space, trimmed, lowercased).
- **AC 6.6.6** [Authenticated] **Given** I already have a document with the same normalized text, **when** I upload it again (any file type or pasted), **then** the API returns 200 with that document and `isDuplicate = true` (topic and title unchanged), resets its 30-day expiry, and restores section text that had expired. Duplicates are per user; another user gets their own document.
- **AC 6.6.7** [Authenticated] **Given** I own a document, **when** I call `GET /study-guides/documents/{id}`, **then** the API returns the `Document` (`id`, `title`, `fileType`, `pageCount`, topic, `keywords`, `collectionId`, `createdUtc`, `expiresUtc`, `isDuplicate = false`, `quota`, `sections`); each section has `preview` (first 240 characters without page markers, `""` once expired), `textExpired`, `status` from its attached generation (`New` none, `Queued` pending, `Processing`, `Generated`, `Failed` with a user-safe `error`), `notBeforeUtc` while queued, `cached` (Generated and I was not charged for it), `droppedCount`, `autoSave` (`null` for manual review; `Pending`, `Saved` or `Failed` for sections queued by "Make a quiz", AC 6.6.29), and — only when Generated — `candidates` (`id`, `index`, `question`, `correctAnswer`, `incorrectAnswers`, `explanation`, `sourceQuote`, `page`, `accepted`, `itemId`). `itemId` is the saved item's id and is present only when the candidate was accepted and that item still exists (a deleted item leaves `accepted = true` with no `itemId`). `GET /study-guides/documents` lists my documents newest first as `{ documents: [{ id, title, fileType, sectionCount, generatedSectionCount, acceptedCount, collectionId, createdUtc, lastActivityUtc, expiresUtc }] }`. Another user's or a missing document → 404.
- **AC 6.6.8** [Authenticated] **Given** my tier is Free (default) or Premium (admin setting `StudyGuidePremium = "true"`, AC 5.1.3b), **then** I may be charged at most 10 sections per UTC day and 60 per UTC calendar month (Premium: 50 / 1,000), unless `StudyGuideDailySections` / `StudyGuideMonthlySections` override the limits. Usage is the sum of my charges in the current UTC day / month; `remainingToday` = min(daily left, monthly left). Sections served from the shared cache or attached to a generation someone else already queued cost 0.
- **AC 6.6.9** [Authenticated] **Given** I own a document, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/generate` with `{ sectionIds }` (1–50 sections of this document; otherwise 400 / `StudyGuideDocument.InvalidSections`), **then** for each section: already attached to a queued, processing, or generated generation → no change, cost 0; a Completed generation exists for its text → attached, cost 0 (`cachedCount`); a Pending/Processing one exists → attached, cost 0, and if I am Premium the job becomes premium and due now; a Failed one exists → reset to Pending and charged 1; otherwise a new Pending generation is created and charged 1. New/reset jobs are due immediately for Premium and after `freeTierDelayMinutes` (10) for Free. The "Make a quiz" path (AC 6.6.29) skips the free-tier delay for the up to `QuickPathMaxSections` (3) sections it queues (`QuickPathFreeTierDelayMinutes`, default 0, and it also pulls a still-waiting free job it attaches to forward without changing its tier); the quota is unchanged. Re-submitting the same notes through Make a quiz pulls my own still-waiting free jobs for the target sections (at most the cap) forward and turns those sections into auto-save sections. A section left `Pending` after its shared generation failed may auto-save later when another user's retry of the same text completes. The API returns 202 `{ queuedCount, cachedCount, chargedSections, quota }`. All-or-nothing: if the charge would exceed my daily or monthly limit, it returns 429 `StudyGuideDocument.QuotaExceeded` with a message naming the limit and queues or charges nothing. A section whose text expired and that needs a charge → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.SectionExpired`. No vendor with a key in my tier's route (premium route for Premium users, free route otherwise) → 503 `StudyGuideDocument.Disabled`, checked before anything is charged, attached, or queued (so a cached-only request from such a user is also refused). Order of checks: validation 400 → document not found 404 → 503 Disabled → 400 InvalidSections / SectionExpired → 429 → 503 BudgetReached. Jobs queued before an admin route change that leave no keyed vendor still fail and are refunded (AC 6.6.13).
- **AC 6.6.10** [Authenticated] **Given** the global budget — section generations queued (created or retried) in the current UTC month across all users — plus this request's charged sections would exceed `StudyGuideGeneration:MonthlySectionBudget` (default 1,000), **when** I call generate, **then** the API returns 503 `StudyGuideDocument.BudgetReached` and charges nothing.
- **AC 6.6.11** [System] **Given** queued generations, **then** the in-API `StudyGuideGenerationWorker` polls every 15 s and claims up to 2 due jobs (Pending with `notBeforeUtc` passed, or Processing whose 5-minute lease expired), premium first, by atomically moving them to Processing with a new lease and incrementing `attempts` (PostgreSQL `UPDATE … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, so concurrent API instances never claim the same job). A job claimed more than 3 times is Failed and refunded. The queue lives in Postgres; nothing special happens on startup. The worker does nothing when no vendor key is configured. After every tick, even when claiming jobs failed, it runs the **auto-save sweep** (AC 6.6.29): for up to `AutoSaveBatchSize` (20) documents that have "Make a quiz" sections with `autoSave = Pending` whose generation is Completed, each document is saved in its own DI scope and database transaction, taking a per-document PostgreSQL advisory lock (`pg_try_advisory_xact_lock`); a document another instance or a manual accept holds is skipped and retried next tick. A save that throws or is rejected is retried on later ticks; after `AutoSaveMaxAttempts` (3) failures the section's `autoSave` becomes `Failed` (logged once) and the sweep ignores it, leaving the manual review flow (Save bar) available. A section whose generation Failed stays `Pending` (its charge was refunded), so a later retry from the picker that completes still auto-saves.
- **AC 6.6.12** [System] **Given** a claimed job, **then** the processor takes the route for the job's tier (AC 5.12.1), skips vendors without a key and vendors cooling down (AC 6.6.33), and calls each vendor in order with the admin-chosen model through one OpenAI-compatible client (`POST {base}/chat/completions`, Bearer key, `response_format: json_object`, temperature 0.2, `max_tokens` 8000 capped by the vendor's `{Vendor}:MaxTokens` (Groq defaults to 4000 to stay inside its 8K tokens/minute free limit); OpenAI reasoning models such as `gpt-5*` get `max_completion_tokens` and no temperature; `gpt-oss` models get `reasoning_effort: low`; OpenRouter calls send `HTTP-Referer: https://www.quizymode.com` and `X-Title: Quizymode`, plus `provider: { "data_collection": "deny" }` in the request body so routing excludes providers that collect prompt/completion data; other vendors do not receive this routing field). Default base URLs, each overridable with `{Vendor}:BaseUrl`: Gemini `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/openai`, Groq `https://api.groq.com/openai/v1`, Cerebras `https://api.cerebras.ai/v1`, OpenRouter `https://openrouter.ai/api/v1`, OpenAI `https://api.openai.com/v1`. Every vendor call is logged as an `LlmAttempts` row (vendor, model, outcome, tokens, duration, HTTP status). The HTTP status is recorded for every reply that was received (including 200 replies that are unusable) and left empty for a timeout, a network failure, or a missing key. A non-2xx reply is an `AuthError` for 401; for a 403 whose body (case-insensitive) contains "api key", "api_key", "apikey", "api-key", "authenticat" or "credential"; or for a 400 whose body mentions "API key". An HTTP 429 is `RateLimited` (AC 6.6.33). Any other reply, including a 403 without that wording (for example an unsupported region or model access) and a 404 for a model the vendor removed, is a `ProviderError`. Every failure outcome falls through to the next vendor.
- **AC 6.6.13** [System] **Given** a vendor reply, **then** prompt v2 asks for strict JSON `{"questions":[{question, correctAnswer, incorrectAnswers (1–4), explanation, sourceQuote, page}]}`, up to 20 questions only from the supplied section text, with `sourceQuote` copied verbatim (20–300 characters) and page markers in the text. Each question is kept only if it passes the item field rules, its `sourceQuote` — normalized (NFC, lowercase, curly quotes/apostrophes and dashes unified, line-break hyphenation and whitespace collapsed, leading/trailing ellipses and quotes stripped) — is at least 15 characters and occurs in the normalized section text without page markers, it has no blocked language, and it does not repeat another question; `page` comes from the marker before the quote (else the model's page if it is in the section). Dropped questions are counted in `droppedCount`. A provider error, auth error (rejected key), timeout, content-filter stop, invalid JSON, or low quote yield (nothing kept, or fewer than half of the returned questions kept) moves on to the next vendor. On success the job is Completed with its candidates, vendor, model, and tokens, and its stored source text is cleared. If every vendor fails but one returned at least one verified question, the best such result is kept; otherwise the job is Failed with a generic user-safe error and every charge for it is refunded (deleted).
- **AC 6.6.14** [Authenticated] **Given** generated candidates on my document, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/accept` with `{ candidates: [{ id, question?, correctAnswer?, incorrectAnswers?, explanation? }] }` (1–200; edits validated with item rules: question ≤ 1000, answers ≤ 500, 1–4 incorrect answers, explanation ≤ 3,500), **then** the API creates the selected (edited) questions as my **private** items (or public items when an admin sets `publish`, AC 6.6.27) through the standard bulk item-creation path (category, navigation path, and keywords from the document; `source` empty; `UploadId` = the document id), appends `\n\nFrom "<title>", <section label>` to each explanation, adds the new items to the document's collection, and returns 200 `{ collectionId, createdCount, duplicateCount, failedCount, itemIds }`. The first accept creates that collection — name = document title, description "Generated from study guide <title>", owner = me, `IsPublic = false` (owner only until I share it; #296); the request may carry an optional `collectionName` (trimmed; blank or omitted falls back to the document title; more than 200 characters is 400; text blocked by moderation is 400 `StudyGuideDocument.ProfanityBlocked` and nothing is saved) that names the collection only when it is created and is ignored once the document has a collection; later accepts reuse it, or create a new one if I deleted it. Edited candidates whose wrong answers repeat each other or equal the correct answer are rejected with 400 `StudyGuideDocument.InvalidEdit` and nothing is saved (#276). The accept is atomic: the items, the accepted-candidate rows, the collection links and `collectionId` commit in one database transaction (a failure part-way saves nothing), and on PostgreSQL it waits for the document's advisory lock so a manual Save that overlaps the auto-save sweep (AC 6.6.11) cannot create duplicate items or hit a unique-key error. The bulk item handler joins a caller's transaction when one is open and only opens its own when none is.
- **AC 6.6.15** [Authenticated] **Given** I accept candidates, **then** candidates already saved from this document are skipped and counted in `duplicateCount` (as are questions that duplicate my existing items or each other); a candidate that is not from a generated section of this document → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.InvalidCandidates` (nothing saved); blocked language in edits → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.ProfanityBlocked`; another user's document → 404.
- **AC 6.6.16** [System] Uploading a duplicate, generating, and accepting each set the document's `lastActivityUtc` to now and `expiresUtc` to 30 days later. The daily study guide cleanup (a background service that runs every 24 hours) deletes documents past `expiresUtc` that have no saved questions (their sections cascade) and, for documents with saved questions, keeps the document but clears its section text (`textExpired = true`); shared generations and candidates, accepted items, and collections are never deleted by expiry. `LlmAttempts` rows older than 90 days are trimmed by the same cleanup.
- **AC 6.6.17** [System] LLM vendor HTTP calls (the documents flow, the only LLM caller since #240) do not use the default HTTP resilience pipeline from service defaults (10 s attempt timeout, 30 s total, automatic retries). Each call is limited only by the configured vendor timeout (default 120 s), and failures are handled by the app's own vendor fallback, so a long completion is not cut off and a billable request is not silently retried (#206).

**UI** (#200)

- **AC 6.6.20** [Authenticated] **Given** I open **Turn notes into a quiz** (`/create/notes`, `h1` "Turn notes into a quiz"; the page title uses the same text), **when** `GET /study-guides/documents/config` returns `enabled = true`, **then** I see a three-step indicator (an ordered list "Add material", "Choose coverage", "Review"; the current step has `aria-current="step"`, here "Add material") above two keyboard-operable tabs (`role="tab"`, arrow keys switch): **Upload file** (drop zone plus a labeled file input whose `accept` list comes from `acceptedExtensions`, with the size/page limits shown and a note that scanned PDFs are not supported) and **Paste text** (textarea with a live UTF-8 byte counter against `maxTextBytes`); plus optional **Title**, the shared **Topic** group (AC 12.9: chips suggested from the title and pasted text, or, for a `.txt` or `.md` file, from the first 20,000 bytes read in the browser with `file.slice(0, 20000).text()`, or, for `.pdf`, `.docx` and `.pptx`, from the title and the file name, with the note "Topic suggestions use your title and file name for this file type."; the searchable picker; **Browse all topics**; AC 12.12), and optional comma-separated **Keywords** (**Title** and **Keywords** sit under a collapsed **More options** disclosure, `aria-expanded`, whose values survive collapsing), and a **My study guides** link. Above the buttons one line reads "A quick quiz covers up to N sections. You have M sections left today." (or "You've used all your sections for today. Try again tomorrow." when none are left). Before calling the API the form validates with Zod: a file is chosen (extension in the accepted list, not empty, at most `maxFileBytes`) or text is non-blank and within `maxTextBytes`; topic fields are present; title ≤ 200 characters; keywords well formed (≤ 10, letters/numbers/hyphens, ≤ 30 chars). A missing topic is caught first, before the Zod check, with "Choose a topic: pick a suggestion, search, or browse all topics." and focus on the search box (AC 12.14); the topic must be chosen before the upload because the document is created with it. An over-limit file or text disables both buttons. The primary button **Make a quiz** posts multipart `POST /study-guides/documents/quick` (AC 6.6.29) and routes by outcome: Ready replaces the page with Learn (`/learn/collections/{collectionId}/{slug}`), Generating opens the document page in quick mode, and NeedsChoice, OverAllowance and Unavailable open the document page with the server's one-line message above the section picker. The secondary button **Choose sections and review first** (the former **Upload study guide**) posts multipart `POST /study-guides/documents` (`file` or `text`, `title`, `category`, `navigationKeyword1`, `navigationKeyword2`, repeated `keywords`) and then navigates to `/study-guides/documents/{id}` as before, keeping the review-before-save flow; a note under the buttons says "Review first: you pick the sections and check every question before anything is saved." While either request runs both buttons are `aria-disabled` (a second click is ignored, one request is sent), the labels read "Making your quiz…" / "Uploading…", a live region says "Making your quiz. Reading your notes.", and after 3 seconds "Reading your notes. This can take a few seconds." appears; errors appear in a focused `role="alert"` and every typed value is kept. The intro no longer promises that every question is reviewed first (it now says Make a quiz saves them privately, ready to learn). The step indicator stays on this page; a duplicate (`isDuplicate = true`) shows "You already uploaded this study guide, so here is your existing copy." on the document page. API errors show the ProblemDetails detail (e.g. scanned PDF, unsupported type); 413 shows the file size limit; 429 a "wait a while" message; when config `enabled = false` or the API returns 503, the page shows "Feature unavailable" with links to the manual study guide flow and to My study guides.
- **AC 6.6.21** [Authenticated] **Given** I open `/study-guides/documents/{id}` for my document, **when** it loads, **then** the header shows the title, file type, page count, "N of M sections generated", the topic path, "Text kept until <date>", an **Open collection** link (`/explore/collections/{collectionId}`) once the document has a collection (with a primary **Learn** link to `/learn/collections/{collectionId}/{slug}` beside it once at least one question is saved), and the same three-step indicator with **Choose coverage** current while no section is generated and **Review** current once any section is generated; a missing or foreign document shows "could not be found" with a link back to My study guides.
- **AC 6.6.22** [Authenticated] **Given** the document page, **when** I use the **Choose sections** picker, **then** each section row has a labeled checkbox (the section label), a preview, the size, and a status chip with text — **New**, **Ready, free** (Generated and not charged to me), **Queued**, **Generating**, **Done**, **Failed** (with the error), or **Expired** (text no longer kept) — and only New/Failed sections whose text is kept can be selected. The initial selection is the first **New** sections in document order up to `quota.remainingToday` (max 50); generated and cached sections are never preselected and never count. A live counter shows "X of Y sections today" and "X of Y this month · <tier> plan" (used + selected), plus "N sections left today". **Generate N sections** is disabled when nothing is selected, the selection exceeds the sections left today (with an inline "more sections than you have left today" alert), or exceeds 50. Generating calls `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/generate` with the selected ids, clears the selection, refreshes the document, and shows how many sections were queued / ready for free / charged; 429 shows the API's quota message, 503 `BudgetReached` says generation is paused for the month, and other 503s say AI generation is unavailable.
- **AC 6.6.23** [Authenticated] **Given** any section is **Queued** or **Processing**, **when** the document page is open, **then** it polls `GET /study-guides/documents/{id}` every 3 seconds until none are; for a Free-tier user with queued sections whose `notBeforeUtc` is in the future it shows "Free plan: generation starts within about N minutes" (N rounded up from the latest `notBeforeUtc`), otherwise "Generating questions… This page updates automatically." A section also counts as active while it is Generated with `autoSave = Pending` (AC 6.6.29). Sections queued by "Make a quiz" replace that line with the progress panel of AC 6.6.31.
- **AC 6.6.24** [Authenticated] **Given** sections are **Generated**, **when** I review them, **then** questions are grouped by section label and numbered across the document; each shows the correct and wrong answers (with screen-reader labels), the explanation, and the source quote as `From your guide, p. 14: “…”` (falls back to the section label when the candidate has no page); sections with dropped questions show "N questions dropped: not found in your guide". Unaccepted questions have an **Include question N** checkbox (checked by default) and an **Edit** action that opens inline labeled fields (question, correct answer, 1–4 wrong answers with add/remove, explanation) validated with Zod using item limits (question ≤ 1000, answers ≤ 500, explanation ≤ 3500 characters) before the edit is kept; accepted questions show a **Saved** badge and no checkbox, plus an **Edit** link to `/items/{itemId}/edit` when `itemId` is present. A question left unsaved in a section whose `autoSave` is `Saved` (a duplicate of an existing item, or skipped as blocked or invalid) shows "Already in your items" and is never part of Save N selected. **Save N selected** posts `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/accept` with `{ candidates: [{ id }] }` for unedited and `{ id, question, correctAnswer, incorrectAnswers, explanation }` for edited questions (in batches of at most 200), then shows "Saved N questions to your collection" (with duplicate / failed counts when non-zero) and five actions: **Learn** (`/learn/collections/{collectionId}/{slug}`), **Start quiz** (`/quiz/collections/{collectionId}`), **Study flashcards** (`/explore/collections/{collectionId}`), **Add more** (scrolls to and focuses the section picker) and **Open collection** (`/collections/{collectionId}`). Until the document has a collection, the review area shows a labeled **Collection name** input (max 200 characters) defaulting to the document title, and the first save sends it as `collectionName`; once a collection exists the input is hidden and no name is sent. Edits whose wrong answers equal the correct answer or each other are rejected inline with "Wrong answers must be different from the correct answer." / "Wrong answers must be different from each other." (#276).
- **AC 6.6.25** [Authenticated] **Given** I open **My study guides** (`/study-guides/documents`, linked from Generate Quiz and the Create hub), **when** the list loads, **then** each document shows its title, "X of Y sections generated · Z questions saved", last activity and "Text kept until <date>", a **Collection** link when one exists, and a **Continue** button to its document page; with no documents I see an empty state linking to the upload page. Under the description, once both the list and `GET /study-guides/documents/config` have loaded, a polite live region (`aria-live="polite"`) shows two lines: "This month: X of Y sections used (free|premium plan)." (the monthly quota, AC 6.6.8; "This month: X sections used (… plan)." when the monthly limit is 0, "1 section used" for a single section) and "Across all your guides: N question(s) saved in M study guide(s)." (the sum of `acceptedCount` and the number of guides, current totals rather than monthly; correct singular forms; numbers use thousands separators). With no guides the zeros are shown above the empty state. If the config request fails the summary is omitted and the list still works; if the list fails only the existing error is shown. Tokens and cost are never shown.
- **AC 6.6.26** [Authenticated] The document pages are lazy-loaded routes behind sign-in (anonymous users are sent to sign in), the **Create** navigation item stays highlighted on `/study-guides/*`, and the page title fallback is "My Study Guides".
- **AC 6.6.27** [Authenticated, Admin] **Given** I call `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/accept` with the optional boolean `publish` (default `false`), **when** I am a non-admin and `publish` is `true`, **then** the API returns **403** before validating or writing anything (no items, collection, or accepted-candidate rows). **When** I am an admin and `publish` is `true`, **then** the created items are **public** (`IsPrivate = false`) and the `From "<title>", <section label>` suffix is **not** appended to their explanations, so a private file name or document title never becomes public; the document collection is still created with `IsPublic = false` and `IsDiscoverable = false` (owner only). Without `publish` (or `false`, for anyone) behavior is unchanged: private items with the suffix. Published items are not reviewed through the review board and get no cross-user duplicate check against the public library.
- **AC 6.6.28** [Authenticated, Admin] **Given** I review generated questions on the document page, **then** admins (and only admins) see a **Save as** control with "Private (only me)" (default) and "Publish to the library (public)" and helper text that the collection stays private; the choice is sent as `publish: true` on every accept batch (only when Publish is chosen) and the success message says "Published N questions". Non-admins do not see the control and never send `publish`.
- **AC 6.6.29** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /study-guides/documents/quick` (multipart, the same fields, validation, size limit and `study-guide-documents` rate-limit bucket as `POST /study-guides/documents`; anonymous → 401), **then** if no vendor in my tier's route has an API key the API returns 503 `StudyGuideDocument.Disabled` before creating anything; otherwise it creates the document exactly like create (201, or 200 with `document.isDuplicate = true`; extraction, topic, moderation and size errors keep their create status codes) and returns `{ document, outcome, message, queuedCount, cachedCount, chargedSections, collectionName }`. The **target sections** are the document's sections with `autoSave` not `Saved` and no accepted candidate (so an existing document is only filled in where nothing is saved yet). `outcome`: more target sections than `QuickPathMaxSections` (3, configurable) → `NeedsChoice` with nothing generated or charged ("This has N sections. A quick quiz covers up to 3, so pick the ones you want below."); otherwise the targets go through the standard generate path (AC 6.6.9, one source of truth for quota, cache, budget and the per-user lock) with `autoSave = Pending` set on every target (including cached and already-queued ones). Quota exceeded → `OverAllowance` (the 429 message, nothing charged), global budget, disabled or another failure → `Unavailable`, expired text → `NeedsChoice`, a concurrent change → one retry then `Unavailable`; all are 2xx because the document exists. When nothing is left to generate the auto-save runs inline: `Ready` only when at least one question was actually saved (and the response carries `document.collectionId` and `collectionName`), `NeedsChoice` with "We couldn't make any questions from these notes. Try a longer or more detailed section." when none were, and `Generating` when sections are still pending (an inline save failure degrades to `Generating`, never 500). A repeated request is idempotent (no second charge). Each outcome is logged once with its counts; no analytics event is added. A section the quick path queued is auto-saved whenever its generation completes, with no time window after the request: if its generation failed (refunded) and I retry it from the picker, it still auto-saves.
- **AC 6.6.30** [System] **Given** a section with `autoSave = Pending` whose generation is Completed, **when** the worker sweep (AC 6.6.11) or the quick endpoint runs, **then** all its candidates not yet saved are accepted as the document's owner with a non-admin context: items are always **private** (`IsPrivate = true`, `CreatedBy` = owner, `UploadId` = document id, never on the admin review board), added to the document's collection (created on first save: name = document title, `IsPublic = false`, `IsDiscoverable = false`, no empty collection when nothing was saved), and the section becomes `autoSave = Saved` in the same transaction. Blocked or invalid candidates are skipped, not failures (the public accept still refuses them). Re-running creates no extra items or accepted rows. Another user's cached generation for the same text only supplies candidates: the owner's items, collection and accepted rows are created for the quick user, and nothing is written to the other user's document. An admin using Make a quiz still gets private items; publishing stays an explicit review-flow action (AC 6.6.27).
- **AC 6.6.31** [Authenticated] **Given** I arrive at `/study-guides/documents/{id}` from Make a quiz (navigation state `quick`), **then** while any quick section is Queued, Processing or Generated with `autoSave = Pending` (and, for one render, while "Your quiz is ready. Opening Learn…" is announced before the page is replaced) the page shows a progress panel (`h2` "Making your quiz", focused on arrival) instead of the section picker, the step indicator, the free-tier wait line and the review/Save area: a `role="status"` line ("Your quiz is in line." while all are queued, "Making your quiz: X of N sections ready.", "Saving your questions…", or "Can't check progress right now. Trying again…" when a poll fails while keeping the last data; plus "This is taking longer than usual. Your questions will still save when they're ready." after 5 minutes), a progress bar, "Questions save automatically, so you can leave this page. Find them later in My study guides." and a list of the quick sections with "Waiting", "Making questions", "Saving", "Saved" or "Couldn't be made". When nothing is pending it acts once: at least one question saved and a collection → status "Your quiz is ready. Opening Learn…" and the page is replaced by Learn, with a notice when some sections failed (AC 3.17.1); all quick sections failed to generate → a focused `role="alert"` "We couldn't make questions from this guide. You weren't charged. Please try again in a bit." and the picker with the failed sections preselected; sections whose generation succeeded but whose automatic save failed three times (`autoSave = Failed`, charged) never use the "not charged" wording: the alert reads "We couldn't save your questions automatically. Review them below and press Save." and the review list with its Save bar is shown, and the Learn notice says "N section(s) couldn't be saved automatically. Open your study guide to save them." (the "wasn't counted against your limit" phrase is kept for sections whose generation failed); finished with no questions → the "couldn't make any questions" alert and the picker. The panel is derived from the fetched data, so reopening the page later shows progress or the result without redirecting; only the `quick` navigation state redirects. For `NeedsChoice`, `OverAllowance` and `Unavailable` (navigation state `quickMessage`) the page shows that one server line (`role="status"`) above the normal picker, with the first min(`quickPathMaxSections`, sections left today) never-processed sections preselected; the picker does not enforce the cap, only the quota as today. When the upload was a duplicate it also says "Using the topic from your earlier upload." After everything is saved, revisiting shows the heading "Your questions" with "Saved to your private collection. Use Edit to change one.", **Saved** badges and **Edit** links, no **Collection name** input and no Save bar; a section whose auto-save `Failed` keeps the normal review list and Save bar.
- **AC 6.6.32** [System] **Given** the free-provider rollout (#464), **then** the vendors are Gemini, Groq, Cerebras, OpenRouter, and OpenAI (DeepSeek is removed), and the migration `ReplaceLlmModelColumnsWithMap` replaces the per-vendor model columns with one `Models` JSON map: the OpenAI model is carried over; the Gemini model is carried over unless it is empty or a retired `gemini-2.*` model, which becomes `gemini-3.5-flash-lite`; Groq, Cerebras, and OpenRouter get their defaults (AC 5.12.1). A saved route equal to the old default (`Gemini,OpenAI,DeepSeek` free, `DeepSeek,OpenAI,Gemini` premium) becomes the new default; any other route only loses `DeepSeek` (and becomes the new default if nothing is left). With only one vendor's key configured (for example only `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`), a free-tier section is generated by that vendor and its `LlmAttempts` row records that vendor, the model, and tokens.
- **AC 6.6.33** [System] **Given** a vendor answers HTTP 429, **then** the attempt is recorded as `RateLimited` with HTTP 429, the same section falls through to the next vendor, and the vendor is put on an in-memory cooldown (per API instance, reset on restart): until the `Retry-After` header (seconds or date) or a Gemini `"retryDelay": "Ns"` in the body passes; else, when the body names a per-day limit ("per day", "PerDay", "daily", etc.), until the next midnight Pacific time; else 1 minute. Every cooldown is capped at 60 minutes. While a vendor is cooling down, later generations skip it with one Information line (`skipped {Vendor}: rate-limited, cooling down until …`) and a Warning line is logged when the cooldown starts; neither contains keys or the reply body. If every keyed vendor in the route is cooling down, the whole keyed route is tried anyway so a job is never failed by cooldowns alone.
- **AC 6.6.34** [Authenticated] **Given** I queue generation with `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/generate`, **when** I send `questionStyle`, **then** `Recall` (also the default when omitted) makes the recall-style questions of AC 6.6.9 and `ClinicalScenario` makes exam-style questions: each question is at most two sentences describing a client and asks a priority, first-action or red-flag question, with the same verbatim source quote, page and length limits. Any other value → 400 (validation). Each style has its own cached generation (`Mode` `GuideOnly` or `ClinicalScenario`), so the same section text can be generated once per style. A section already attached to a generation of the other style is relinked to the requested style: a cached result of the other style is not reused, so it is charged 1 like any new section. The Study guide page shows a **Question style** radio group above **Generate** (default Recall; the choice applies to that request only). The "Make a quiz" path (AC 6.6.29) always uses Recall. For `ClinicalScenario`, the worker keeps a question only when it is clinical-style by a deterministic check: at most two sentences, a client or patient cue (client, patient, resident, child, infant, adult, woman, man), and a priority cue (first, priority, initial, immediate, urgent, red flag). A recall-style question returned under the clinical request is dropped and counted in `DroppedCount`, and the completion log line names the `Mode`. (`StudyGuidePromptV2Tests`, `StudyGuideDocumentHttpTests`, `StudyGuideClinicalStyleTests`)
- **AC 6.6.35** [Authenticated] **Given** I own a study guide document, **when** I call `PUT /study-guides/documents/{id}/primary-source` with `{ "isPrimarySource": true|false }`, **then** the API stores the flag on the document and returns 200 `{ id, isPrimarySource }`; `GET /study-guides/documents/{id}` and each row of `GET /study-guides/documents` return `isPrimarySource` (default false). A missing `isPrimarySource` returns 400, anonymous callers 401, and a document I do not own 404 (nothing changes). The flag does not extend retention. On the document page I see a **Primary source** checkbox (with a one-line hint) that saves immediately and shows an alert if saving fails; in **My study guides** a primary source guide shows a "Primary source" badge. (#372; backend tests: StudyGuideDocumentHttpTests; frontend tests: StudyGuideDocumentPage.test.tsx, StudyGuideDocumentsPage.test.tsx, studyGuideDocuments.test.ts)
- **AC 6.6.36** [System] **Deferred (#372):** preferring the primary source's answer in conflict / near-duplicate flags is not implemented, because no conflict or near-duplicate flagging exists for study guide questions yet (only exact duplicate detection). It becomes in scope when the near-duplicate issue lands.

---

## AC 7. Developer tooling

### AC 7.1 Automated user guide screenshots

- **AC 7.1.0** [Dev tooling] User-guide screenshots are captured from either production (`https://www.quizymode.com`) or local (`http://localhost:7000`) using the existing Playwright screenshot projects for desktop and mobile. Production publishing does not commit generated screenshots back into the repo; GitHub Actions stores them in temporary build folders, assembles a static Pages site artifact, and deploys only the latest published guide.
- **AC 7.1.0a** [Dev tooling] The **Publish User Guide** workflow does not run after every Deploy. It runs on a daily schedule and on manual dispatch. A scheduled run first compares `main` with the commit of the last successful guide run; it captures and publishes only when guide-relevant files changed (web UI source under `src/Quizymode.Web/src` excluding `*.test.ts(x)`, `public/`, `index.html`, Vite config, `playwright/screenshots/`, `playwright/fixtures.ts`, `playwright.config.ts`, the guide site, guide generator, capture report and gate scripts, readiness script, or the workflow itself). Version-only `package.json`/`package-lock.json` bumps and backend-only changes skip the capture, and the published guide stays as it was. If there is no usable previous successful run, it captures. A manual run captures by default (`force` input, default `true`) and may pin `expected_build_version` (otherwise any live build); a scheduled run waits for the live build of the `main` commit it checked out (`<QuizymodeVersion>+sha.<short sha>`) and fails if prod does not reach it within 15 minutes, so the next scheduled run compares against the last commit actually captured. Dependency-only `package.json` bumps that change rendered UI need a manual run.
- **AC 7.1.1** [Dev tooling] Running `npx playwright test --project=screenshots` authenticates as the test user (credentials from `TEST_USER_EMAIL` / `TEST_USER_PASSWORD` env vars), visits each major signed-in route, and saves a full-page PNG screenshot to `docs/user-guide/screenshots/user/NN.{slug}.png` (`NN` is the step number from the guide's section order). Running `npx playwright test --project=screenshots-mobile` captures the same slug inventory to `docs/user-guide/screenshots/mobile/NN.{slug}.png` using a phone-sized browser profile. Each shot's outcome is written to `docs/user-guide/screenshots/failures.json` (AC 7.1.5). The target URL is controlled by `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL`, which defaults to the local dev server `http://localhost:7000`. Production is targeted only when `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` is set explicitly (the `generate-user-guide(s)-production` scripts and the Publish User Guide workflow set it to `https://www.quizymode.com`), so a bare `npx playwright test` never sends authenticated traffic to prod.
- **AC 7.1.2** [Dev tooling] Running `node scripts/generate-user-guide.js --guide desktop` rebuilds the desktop markdown guide; `--guide mobile` rebuilds the mobile markdown guide; `--all` rebuilds both. The guides do not need local PNGs: every step is listed and its image links to the published Pages copy (AC 7.1.8). Running `node scripts/build-user-guide-site.js` assembles one static user-guide site with `/assets/desktop/` and `/assets/mobile/` screenshots, a client-side Desktop or Mobile toggle, automatic initial mode selection from viewport width, and persisted user preference in `localStorage`.
- **AC 7.1.3** [Dev tooling] `playwright/.auth/` (session tokens) and `docs/user-guide/screenshots/` (generated screenshots) are excluded from source control via `.gitignore`. No screenshot PNG is tracked in git; readers see screenshots only through the GitHub Pages site. Earlier tracked PNGs were removed from the index with `git rm --cached`, and history was not rewritten.
- **AC 7.1.5** [Dev tooling] Each shot is attempted up to 3 times. After a failed attempt the runner waits 2 s, reloads the page, and tries again; after a second failure it waits 5 s, reloads, and makes a last attempt. No new attempt starts once a shot has used 90 s, and retries across the whole run stop after 5 minutes of total retry time. A page counts as loaded only when the URL is not `/login`, `#root` is visible, no "Something went wrong" error screen shows, no visible loading spinner remains (a visible `.animate-spin` element; other `role="status"` messages such as form or search feedback do not count), and the shot's expected elements appear. Each test has a 150 s limit. A shot that still fails is recorded in `failures.json` (per viewport: status, attempts, last error, path) and does not fail its test, so later shots still run.
- **AC 7.1.6** [Dev tooling] The Publish User Guide workflow does not deploy Pages when sign-in fails (no capture report is written), when a key page (`home`, `categories`, `collection-detail`, `mode-quiz`, `mode-flashcards`) was not captured in a viewport that ran, or when more than 10% of the recorded shots failed. In those cases the previous guide stays live. Otherwise it deploys and lists the failed shots in the job summary. The local `generate-user-guide(s)-{local,production}` wrappers still generate the guide, then list failed shots and exit non-zero, so failures are not silently ignored.
- **AC 7.1.7** [Dev tooling] A guide step whose screenshot failed in the current viewport shows a "Screenshot unavailable in this build" card instead of an image, and it does not fall back to the other viewport's image. The Desktop / Mobile switch (AC 7.1.2) works as before for steps that have a screenshot.
- **AC 7.1.8** [Dev tooling] The markdown guides (`docs/user-guide/user-guide.md` and `docs/user-guide/user-guide.mobile.md`) list every step and link each image to the published Pages copy, `https://yanis-kr.github.io/Quizymode/assets/<viewport>/NN.<slug>.png`, so they render on GitHub without local PNGs. Because the images come from the latest published guide, they can differ from this repo version.

### AC 7.2 Local E2E runner scripts

- **AC 7.2.1** [Dev tooling] `scripts/run-e2e-local-smoke.ps1` (Windows) and `scripts/run-e2e-local-smoke.sh` (Mac/Linux/WSL) start the local Aspire host and React dev server, wait for both to become healthy, run Playwright auth setup, then execute only tests tagged `@smoke` via `--project=smoke`.
- **AC 7.2.2** [Dev tooling] `scripts/run-e2e-local-full.ps1` and `scripts/run-e2e-local-full.sh` perform the same startup sequence and then execute the full E2E suite via `--project=e2e-full`.
- **AC 7.2.3** [Dev tooling] Both runner pairs share logic extracted into `scripts/_e2e-common.ps1` and `scripts/_e2e-common.sh` (prefixed `_` to signal they are not runnable directly). Shared functions cover: prerequisite checks, Aspire/React process management, health-poll loops, auth setup invocation, environment teardown (including on Ctrl+C), and a post-run summary that parses `playwright-report/results.json` and prints an AC-ID table.
- **AC 7.2.4** [Dev tooling] All four runner scripts read credentials exclusively from `TEST_USER_EMAIL` and `TEST_USER_PASSWORD` environment variables; no credentials are hardcoded. The `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` environment variable is set to `http://localhost:7000` before Playwright runs so the config targets the local dev stack instead of production.
- **AC 7.2.5** [Dev tooling] E2E test files live under `playwright/e2e/`. Tests tagged `@smoke` (substring in the test title) are included in the smoke project; all tests in that directory run under `e2e-full`.
- **AC 7.2.6** [Dev tooling] Each local runner script saves Playwright test artifacts (screenshots, traces) to a timestamped subfolder under `playwright/test-results/` named `smoke-YYYY-MM-DD_HH-mm-ss` or `full-YYYY-MM-DD_HH-mm-ss`. The folder path is printed at the end of the run. Subfolders older than 7 days are deleted automatically at the end of each run. The `smoke` and `e2e-full` Playwright projects are configured with `screenshot: "on"` so a screenshot is captured after every test.
- **AC 7.2.7** [Dev tooling] Local Playwright runs fail fast by default: failed tests are **not retried**, the per-test timeout is short (about 15 seconds), and default action / expectation waits are only a few seconds so broken selectors do not consume a minute per failure.

### AC 7.3 E2E test coverage baseline

- **AC 7.3.1** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/auth.spec.ts` covers the login flow (AC 4.3): login page renders the sign-in form; valid credentials redirect away from `/login`; invalid credentials show an error without navigating away. Tests run without pre-loaded auth state so they exercise the full sign-in path.
- **AC 7.3.2** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/categories.spec.ts` covers category browsing (AC 3.1, 3.4): `/categories` shows category cards; clicking a category with items navigates to the scoped URL; the category Sets view shows mode tabs (**List**, **Quiz**) and keyword buckets.
- **AC 7.3.3** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/items.spec.ts` covers item access (AC 2.3, 3.5): the **List** tab shows item links after switching modes; clicking an item opens the detail page with Question and Answer sections; the back control returns to the same list URL.
- **AC 7.3.4** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/collections.spec.ts` covers the collections page (AC 1.1, 1.3, 1.9): page loads with **Mine / Bookmarked / Discover** tabs; authenticated users see a create-collection action; switching to the Discover tab updates the URL query string to `?tab=discover`.
- **AC 7.3.5** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/quiz.spec.ts` covers quiz mode (AC 3.6): quiz loads with a question and answer options after navigating from the category Sets view; selecting an answer reveals the correct answer; the Next button advances to the next question; the score counter is visible throughout.

### AC 7.4 Autonomous development and release

- **AC 7.4.1** [Dev tooling] The scheduled PowerShell controller advances enabled Codex and Claude queues without a human PR-review or release-approval gate. A candidate becomes releasable only after exact-SHA testing, fresh independent agent review, published issue/PR evidence, and the configured required GitHub checks pass.
- **AC 7.4.2** [Dev tooling] Controller, provider, merge, deployment, rollback, and issue-finalization side effects use external durable checkpoints and resume without duplicating a PR merge or rollback dispatch. Pausing prevents new stages and releases while allowing an in-flight merge/deployment/rollback to reach a recorded outcome.
- **AC 7.4.3** [Dev tooling] A candidate containing a destructive migration cannot merge until automation creates a fresh production backup, restores it into an isolated local rehearsal database, and applies the candidate EF migrations successfully. Database rollback is not automatic; migrations must preserve compatibility with the previous application revision. A migration counts as destructive when its forward `Up()` body (or a raw SQL script as a whole) drops a table, column, schema, or database, truncates, or deletes without a `WHERE`, in SQL text or through EF Core's `DropTable`, `DropColumn`, or `DropSchema` builder calls; the `Down()` rollback body is ignored, so additive migrations are not flagged.
- **AC 7.4.4** [Dev tooling] If the merge-correlated deployment or configured production smoke check fails, automation dispatches an application rollback to the previous main SHA for both LightSail and S3/CloudFront, records the rollback outcome, keeps the issue open, and halts subsequent releases.

### AC 7.5 Curated image provenance and build (#407)

- **AC 7.5.1** [Dev tooling] Every curated flag picture has an entry in `data/curated-media/flags/manifest.json` recording the ISO code, country, region, Wikimedia Commons file title, file page URL, original file URL, source SHA-1, licence, author and attribution, output size and output SHA-256. Only public-domain and CC0 files are accepted without further conditions; CC BY and CC BY-SA are accepted only with the author recorded. A file with any other licence fails the build and must be replaced by a `flag-icons` (MIT) fallback that is recorded in the manifest. All 195 flags are public domain on Commons, so no fallback is used; the flag of Oman is the Commons file "Flag of Oman (WFB 2000).svg" (public domain, CIA World Factbook, 2:1 proportions, the pre-2004 ratio) because the file with the official 7:4 proportions (2004 decree) is under the Omani open government licence (OGL-om 1.0), which is not on the allowlist. Credits are listed in `docs/about.md`, shown on the About page.
- **AC 7.5.2** [Dev tooling] **Given** I run `npm run build:flag-images` (`scripts/curated-media/build_flag_images.mjs`, using the exact-pinned `sharp`), **then** the Commons SVGs are downloaded into a new temporary directory created for that run, its SHA-1 must match the Commons API value, SVGs containing scripts, entities or external references are rejected, and the image is rasterized to fit inside 640x640 as a WebP at quality 90 (lowered only if needed) to `src/Quizymode.Web/src/assets/curated/flags/<iso2>.webp`; every output must be at most 102400 bytes (total 1,342,826 bytes for 195 files, largest 41,494). **Given** I run `npm run check:flag-images`, **then** it verifies offline that the manifest and the files match (sizes, SHA-256, WebP format, 640 px limit, no unlisted files, licences on the allowlist); with `--rebuild` it also re-downloads every source and requires byte-for-byte identical output. Accepted limitation: replaced pictures leave older hashed files in S3.
- **AC 7.5.3** [Dev tooling] The curated explanation diagrams (#403) are original work released under CC0-1.0 (author "Quizymode"): the SVG sources are committed in `data/curated-media/diagrams/src/<name>.svg` (no `<text>`, labels are drawn as paths so the output does not depend on installed fonts; no scripts, entities or external references) and `data/curated-media/diagrams/manifest.json` records for each diagram its key (`curated:diagrams/<name>`), source file, licence `CC0-1.0`, author, title, alt text, source SHA-256 and the output file, size, SHA-256, width and WebP quality. **Given** I run `npm run build:diagram-images` (`scripts/curated-media/build_diagram_images.mjs`, exact-pinned `sharp`), **then** each SVG is rasterized to a 900 px wide WebP in `src/Quizymode.Web/src/assets/curated/diagrams/<name>.webp` of at most 50 KiB (quality 90, lowered only if needed) and the manifest hashes are rewritten. **Given** I run `npm run check:diagram-images` (offline, also a CI step next to `check:flag-images`), **then** it fails unless the manifest and the files match (licence, author, title and alt text present, source and output hashes, size, WebP format and width, no unlisted files in `src` or the assets folder) and every `curated:diagrams/` reference in the seed items names a manifest entry with the identical alt text; with `--rebuild` it also re-rasterizes the SVGs and requires byte-identical output. The shipped set is four diagrams attached as `explanationImage` to existing science geometry items whose explanation they illustrate: `pythagorean-theorem` (right triangle with legs a, b and hypotenuse c), `triangle-angle-sum` (the three angles of a triangle make a straight 180 degree angle), `circle-radius-diameter` (radius r, diameter d and circumference C) and `parallelogram-area` (base b and perpendicular height h). Syncing them to production needs the usual seed sync after deploy. (Node: `npm run check:diagram-images`; Python test: ShippedDiagramsTests in `scripts/tests/test_explanation_image.py`)

### AC 7.6 Frontend unit-test tooling

- **AC 7.6.1** [Dev tooling] The web app's unit tests run on Vitest 4 with `@vitest/coverage-v8` of the same major, sharing the app's single Vite install (Vite 8 since 3.113.1, AC 7.6.7; no nested Vite). `npm test` and `npm run test:coverage` keep the existing config (jsdom, `src/test/setup.ts`, 15 s test timeout, `coverage/coverage-summary.json` for the CI coverage summary). Coverage is not gated; Vitest 4's coverage-v8 remaps V8 data against the source AST, so reported branch and function percentages are lower than under Vitest 2 for the same tests (expected, not a regression). `vi.restoreAllMocks()` restores spies only; a test that needs `vi.fn()` call history reset between tests calls `vi.clearAllMocks()` (or `mockClear`). **Given** `npm audit` runs in `src/Quizymode.Web`, **then** it reports no critical or high advisories; remaining moderate advisories are limited to the ESLint toolchain's `minimatch` (no non-breaking fix upstream).
- **AC 7.6.2** [Dev tooling] Dependabot (`.github/dependabot.yml`) opens weekly update PRs for the web app, root tooling, NuGet and GitHub Actions; minor and patch updates are grouped per ecosystem. Dependabot PRs are not merged one by one: compatible updates are batched into one change set that bumps `QuizymodeVersion`, passes CI, and deploys once. Major updates are taken in their own PR (or deferred) after their release notes and test impact are checked.
- **AC 7.6.3** [Dev tooling] CI runs `npm run lint` in `src/Quizymode.Web` before the frontend tests, and a lint error fails the build. `eslint-plugin-react-hooks` stays pinned at 7.0.1 and `eslint-plugin-react-refresh` at 0.4.x (Dependabot ignores newer versions): 7.1 enables React Compiler rules that flag existing components, and that refactor is tracked in #425.
- **AC 7.6.4** [Dev tooling] Flaky-test fixes (#426): the study hotkey bindings (`useStudyHotkeys`, AC 3.16) are synced in a layout effect, so a key pressed right after a render, before passive effects run, uses that render's bindings (covered by a unit test that presses a key from a sibling layout effect). The Postgres pool-cap registration test uses a 30 s connect timeout and a 1 s cancellation token on the sixth open instead of a 2 s connect timeout that could also fail the first five opens. The anonymous collection-completion e2e test (AC 3.6.9 to 3.6.11) mocks its collection, rating, items and analytics API calls (fetch/XHR only, so the dev server's source modules still load) with `page.route`, so it no longer depends on seeded data. The expired Test-run restore test (AC 3.18) waits for the persisted run with `vi.waitFor`, which polls on real timers while the test fakes `Date` and intervals.
- **AC 7.6.5** [Dev tooling] Dependency batch 3.112.6: `marked` 18 (build-time About page rendering), `@hookform/resolvers` 6, YamlDotNet 18 (taxonomy parser), `xunit.runner.visualstudio` 4 (same 2,289 backend tests discovered), direct OpenTelemetry 1.20.1 references in the Aspire AppHost (overrides vulnerable transitive versions), and the `setup-dotnet` v6, `github-script` v9 and `deploy-pages` v5 actions. Dependabot updates `vitest` and `@vitest/*` together as one group, because the coverage provider must match the Vitest major, and ignores `@types/node` majors until the Node runtime (22 in CI and deploy) is upgraded. Vite 8 needs a build-config change (Rolldown no longer accepts the object form of `manualChunks`) and is taken separately.
- **AC 7.6.6** [Dev tooling] Dependency batch 3.112.7: Vitest 5 with `@vitest/coverage-v8` 5 (same major), `react-helmet-async` 3 (React 19 peer), `@types/node` 24.19, and the `setup-node` v7, `configure-aws-credentials` v6 and `configure-pages` v6 actions (node24 action runtime; deploy and rollback use the same credentials step). `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6 requires Vite 8 and is taken together with it.
- **AC 7.6.7** [Dev tooling] The web app builds with Vite 8 (Rolldown bundler) and `@vitejs/plugin-react` 6. Build options live under `build.rolldownOptions`; the shared `vendor` (React, React DOM, React Router, scheduler) and `query` (TanStack Query) chunks are kept with `output.codeSplitting.groups`, because Rolldown does not accept the object form of `manualChunks`. Curated pictures are still emitted as `assets/m/<hash>.webp` and `npm run check:curated-images` passes unchanged (AC 3.21.7); Vitest 5 runs on the same single Vite install. Lazy page chunks are unchanged (AC 8.2.1).
- **AC 7.6.8** [Dev tooling] A production web build (`vite build`, mode `production`) fails with "VITE_API_URL is not set for the production build" when `VITE_API_URL` is not loaded, instead of shipping a bundle whose API calls go to the `https://localhost:8082` fallback in `src/api/client.ts`. `.env.production` is saved as UTF-8 without a byte-order mark: Vite 8 reads a leading BOM as part of the first key, which hid `VITE_API_URL` from 3.113.1 until 3.114.1 so every API call from www.quizymode.com failed.

### AC 7.7 Media storage infrastructure (#399)

- **AC 7.7.1** [Owner] Uploaded pictures live in their own private S3 bucket `quizymode-media-prod` in `us-east-1`, never in `quizymode-web` (that bucket is synced with `--delete` on every deploy and CloudFront rewrites its 403 and 404 to `/index.html`). Bucket settings: Block Public Access all four settings on, Object Ownership `BucketOwnerEnforced` (no ACLs), default encryption SSE-S3 (AES256) with the bucket key enabled, versioning off, no CORS, no CloudFront distribution, a lifecycle rule aborting incomplete multipart uploads after 1 day, and a bucket policy that denies requests not using TLS and denies `s3:PutObject` when the `s3:if-none-match` request header is absent (so even the API's credentials cannot overwrite an object). The exact commands are in `docs/infra/S3_CLOUDFRONT.md`.
- **AC 7.7.2** [Owner] The API signs in as the IAM user `quizymode-api-media` with an inline policy allowing only `s3:PutObject` and `s3:GetObject` on `arn:aws:s3:::quizymode-media-prod/normalized/*` (no list, delete, ACL or other bucket actions).
- **AC 7.7.3** [Owner] Accepted deviation: that single principal both writes and reads, instead of separate writer and reader roles, and it uses long-lived access keys because the Lightsail container has no IAM role (`docs/infra/LIGHTSAIL.md`). The keys sit in the Lightsail deployment environment, which the deploy role can read and the deploy workflow copies to the runner's temporary `env.json`. Rotate them by creating a second access key, deploying, then deleting the first.
- **AC 7.7.4** [Owner] The Lightsail container environment variables are `Media__Storage__Provider=S3`, `Media__S3__BucketName=quizymode-media-prod`, `Media__S3__Region=us-east-1`, `Media__S3__AccessKeyId` and `Media__S3__SecretAccessKey` (namespaced on purpose, so the generic `AWS_*` variables and the default credential chain are untouched; if both keys are empty the SDK default chain is used). They are copied from the active deployment on every deploy, so they are set once on a new Lightsail deployment. The values are never committed or logged.
- **AC 7.7.5** [Dev] The default is `Media__Storage__Provider=None`, so a deploy without these variables serves 503 for media (AC 3.24.8). Local development uses `Media:Storage:Provider=FileSystem` from `appsettings.Development.json` (allowed in Development and Testing only): objects are written under `<content root>/.media/`, which is ignored by git and Docker (`**/.media`).
- **AC 7.7.6** [Dev] Image decoding uses SkiaSharp 3.119.4 (MIT) with `SkiaSharp.NativeAssets.Linux.NoDependencies` 3.119.4 for the Ubuntu 24.04 (Noble) based `aspnet:10.0` image, glibc (confirmed from `/etc/os-release` in the built image; no font or system library install), and storage uses `AWSSDK.S3` 4.0.12 (Apache-2.0), all pinned centrally in `Directory.Packages.props`; Dependabot watches them. Decode memory is bounded by the limits of AC 3.24.3 and AC 3.24.9 (at most 16 megapixels, one decode at a time per instance). Measured in the built image with `--memory 512m` (the Lightsail nano size): a 4000x4000 PNG (750,003 B) normalized to 16,186 B in 438 ms, a 4000x4000 JPEG (141,781 B) to 14,784 B in 274 ms and a 5000x3200 PNG to 10,274 B in 535 ms, with a process high-water of about 250 MB including the test harness (idle API 137 MiB). A PNG whose compressed text and colour-profile chunks (zTXt, iTXt, iCCP) before the image data total over 32 KiB, or that has more than 64 ancillary chunks before it, is `400 Media.Unreadable` before any codec is created, because libpng inflates those chunks while reading the header.

## AC 8. Performance characteristics

### AC 8.1 Database query behaviour

- **AC 8.1.1** `GET /collections` retrieves item counts for all user collections in a single batched `GROUP BY` query, not a correlated subquery per collection.
- **AC 8.1.2** `GET /ideas` and related idea-board endpoints aggregate rating counts and averages in SQL (a `GROUP BY` projection); rating rows are not loaded into application memory for aggregation.
- **AC 8.1.3** Multi-keyword item filtering (`?keywords=a,b`) uses a single correlated `COUNT` subquery with AND semantics, not one `EXISTS` subquery per keyword.
- **AC 8.1.4** Admin page-view analytics computes the authenticated/anonymous page-view split in a single `GROUP BY IsAuthenticated` query, not separate count queries. Session counts come from one additional query returning each session's first hit, which supplies the unique-session total and the auth split together.
- **AC 8.1.4a** Admin session analytics aggregates in SQL with two queries whose result size is bounded by **session** count, not hit count: one `GROUP BY SessionId` rollup and one boundary query returning each session's first and last hit. Summary, histogram, and landing-page figures are then derived in memory from those rows; per-hit rows are never loaded wholesale. Country and user-email lookups are performed only for the visible page of sessions.
- **AC 8.1.4b** Session analytics queries use provider-agnostic LINQ (`GroupBy`, `Count(Distinct)`, joins) rather than PostgreSQL-only constructs such as `DISTINCT ON`, `BOOL_OR`, or window functions, so they execute identically against the SQLite in-memory provider used by the test fixture.
- **AC 8.1.5** All read-only item, comment, and rating queries use `AsNoTracking()` so EF Core does not maintain change-tracking state for entities that are never modified.
- **AC 8.1.6** The database has indexes on `CollectionItems(CollectionId)`, `CollectionItems(ItemId)`, `LOWER(Keywords.Name)`, and `LOWER(Categories.Name)` to support efficient joins and case-insensitive name lookups.
- **AC 8.1.7** `POST /ideas` and `PUT /ideas/{id}` do not load all of the author's ideas into memory: the 24-hour create quota is a SQL `COUNT` over the author's ideas created in the window, and the exact-duplicate check (AC 4.9.4/4.9.5) first narrows in SQL to the author's ideas whose lower-cased title matches, then compares the normalized title, problem, and proposed change of only those candidates.
- **AC 8.1.8** `Items` and `Collections` have a stored, generated `SearchVector` (`tsvector`, English configuration) with a GIN index each (`IX_Items_SearchVector`, `IX_Collections_SearchVector`), and `Collections` has a trigram GIN index on `lower("Name")` (`IX_Collections_Name_Trgm`, extension `pg_trgm`). Items weigh Question (A), CorrectAnswer (B), Explanation (C); collections weigh Name (A), Description (B). The index is not partial, because the caller's own private items are searched too.
- **AC 8.1.9** The `SearchVector` columns are created with raw SQL in migration `AddGlobalSearchIndexes` and are deliberately **not** part of the EF model (so entity loads never select them and `EnsureCreated` still works on SQLite in tests); `dotnet ef migrations has-pending-model-changes` reports none. A future change to the type or length of `Question`, `CorrectAnswer`, `Explanation`, `Name` or `Description` must drop and recreate the generated column first (documented in `Migrations/README.md`). Keyword names are not full-text indexed: topic search runs in memory over the YAML taxonomy.

### AC 8.2 Route-level code splitting (PERF-001)

- **AC 8.2.1** [Anonymous] **Given** a visitor opens `/`, **then** the browser downloads only the eager bundle (home, categories, items list, explore/flashcards, quiz, daily quiz, collections, featured, item detail and comments, login and signup) plus shared vendor chunks; no admin (`/admin/*`), authoring (`/create`, `/create/question`, `/create/import`, `/admin/import`, `/items/:id/edit`), study guide (`/create/notes`, `/study-guides/documents`, `/study-guides/documents/:id`), review queue (`/review`), community (`/ideas`, `/roadmap`, `/feedback`) or legal/about (`/about`, `/privacy*`, `/terms*`) page code is fetched; `/search` is lazy as well, and so are the Games pages (`/games*`).
- **AC 8.2.2** [Any user] **Given** the user navigates to a lazy route, **then** the existing loading spinner renders inside the layout (header and navigation stay visible) until the page chunk arrives, then the page renders.
- **AC 8.2.3** [Any user] **Given** a lazy page chunk fails to load (for example a stale tab after a deploy), **then** the app reloads the page automatically at most once per tab session (guarded in `sessionStorage`); if loading fails again, or storage is unavailable, it shows "This page failed to load" with a **Retry** button that reloads the page only when clicked (browsers cache a failed module import for the life of the page), so it never loops. A successful lazy load clears the guard. Navigating to another route clears the failure view. Non-chunk render errors still reach the app-level error boundary and are reported per AC 5.6.
- **AC 8.2.4** [System] The deploy S3 sync keeps earlier hashed `*.js` and `*.css` files (they are excluded from `--delete`), so tabs running an older `index.html` can still open lazy routes after a deploy.

### AC 8.3 Topic suggestion data loading (#322)

- **AC 8.3.1** [System] **Given** the API starts, **then** topic suggestion runs no item query: its taxonomy part is built in memory from the YAML registry (no database access), and the item profiles are loaded lazily by the first **text** suggestion (AC 12.4). A request with empty text or fewer than 2 analysed terms (the fallback-only path, AC 12.5) never starts a load, so a client that only opens a form cannot cost a load.
- **AC 8.3.2** [System] **Given** the profiles load, **then** it is one narrow projection (category name, the two navigation keyword names, `Question`, `CorrectAnswer` and the first 500 characters of `Explanation`, cut in the database with `substring`) over eligible public items only (AC 12.7). It selects no `IncorrectAnswers`, no `ItemKeywords`, no author, and no private row. It runs on a background task in its own scope with the application-stopping token; requests never wait for it (until it finishes they are scored on taxonomy text only), and at most one load runs at a time. Each load logs once its row count, approximate bytes and duration, never any text.
- **AC 8.3.3** [System] **Given** the profiles are loaded, **then** a cheap fingerprint query (count and newest `COALESCE(UpdatedAt, CreatedAt)` of public items, one row) runs inline with a 3 second timeout at most once per hour. A changed fingerprint only marks the profiles stale; a stale model reloads only once `MinReloadInterval` (12 hours) has passed since the last load, so any number of data changes inside that interval cause no reload. A model older than 7 days reloads regardless. If the fingerprint query fails the previous model is kept.
- **AC 8.3.4** [System] **Given** a seed sync applies successfully, **then** the profiles are invalidated (`ITopicSuggestionInvalidator`): the next text suggestion reloads them without waiting for `MinReloadInterval`. No more than `MaxLoadsPerDay` (4) loads, failed ones included, start in any rolling 24 hours, so invalidations cannot exceed the cap. A failed load keeps the previous model and is retried no sooner than 5 minutes later.
- **AC 8.3.5** [System] **Given** normal operation, **then** a full load transfers roughly 3.5 MB (13.5k seed items; the evaluation report lists about 2.1 MB with no explanation and 3.5 MB with the 500 character cap), so the cap of 4 loads a day is at most about 14 MB a day and in practice 1-2 loads a day (about 100-200 MB a month, 2-4 % of the 5 GB egress quota). The in-memory model is a few MB (about 110k node-term postings). The settings live in the `TopicSuggestions` configuration section (`MinReloadInterval`, `MaxLoadsPerDay`, `FingerprintInterval`, `ExplanationCap`, `MaxModelAge`, `FailureRetry`). (backend tests: TopicSuggestionProfilesTests, SuggestTopicsHttpTests)

### AC 8.4 Database connection pool and startup migrations (#339)

- **AC 8.4.1** [System] **Given** the PostgreSQL connection string (`ConnectionStrings:quizymode` from Aspire, else `ConnectionStrings:PostgreSQL` / `APP_ConnectionStrings__PostgreSQL`) has no `Maximum Pool Size` (or `MaxPoolSize`), **then** the API caps its Npgsql pool at `Database:MaxPoolSize` (default 5, at least 1; raised to `Minimum Pool Size` if that is larger). An explicit value in the connection string is used as written. An invalid value stops startup with a message naming the key.
- **AC 8.4.2** [System] **Given** the connection string has no `Connection Idle Lifetime`, **then** idle pooled connections close after `Database:ConnectionIdleLifetimeSeconds` (default 60 s, at least the connection string's `Connection Pruning Interval`, 10 s unless set; the Npgsql default is 300 s). An explicit value is used as written (the option is then ignored). With pooling on, an effective idle lifetime below the pruning interval, or a pruning interval below 1 s, stops startup with a message naming the offending key and never the connection string (#350); Npgsql itself would only fail on first use, inside the startup migration.
- **AC 8.4.3** [System] All database access in the API process (requests, `UserUpsertMiddleware`, hosted services, workers, raw SQL) shares that one pool. With the default cap, one container holds at most 5 Supavisor session-mode slots (15 in total) and a Lightsail deploy overlap at most 10. When the pool is busy, callers wait up to the Npgsql connection timeout (15 s) instead of opening more connections; the post-seed startup burst (about 6 holders: the three cleanup services, topic suggestion profiles and the seeder handlers) briefly queues against the cap inside that timeout.
- **AC 8.4.4** [System] **Given** startup migrations fail with a Supavisor `EMAXCONNSESSION` error (SqlState `XX000`) or another transient PostgreSQL/Npgsql connection error, **then** the API logs a warning per failed attempt, naming the underlying PostgreSQL/Npgsql error type, SqlState and message even when EF Core wraps it (#350), and retries with a fresh scope, up to 6 attempts with waits of 2, 4, 8, 16 and 30 s. A non-transient error (for example a wrong password, `28P01`), cancellation, or a failure on the last attempt is rethrown and stops startup as before (the container restarts). EF Core `EnableRetryOnFailure` is not enabled because the API uses user-initiated transactions.
- **AC 8.4.5** [System] At startup the API logs `PostgreSQL pool: MaxPoolSize=..., ConnectionIdleLifetime=...s`; the connection string is never logged. (backend tests: PostgreSqlConnectionStringDefaultsTests, PostgreSqlRegistrationTests, StartupDatabaseRetryTests)
- **AC 8.4.6** [System] The OpenTelemetry metrics pipeline includes the `Npgsql` meter, so pool usage (busy and idle connections, pending requests) is exported with the other runtime metrics wherever an OTLP exporter is configured (#350).

## AC 9. Text-to-speech (browser TTS)

### AC 9.1 Quiz mode playback

- **AC 9.1.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] A speaker-icon button appears next to the "Question" heading in Quiz mode. Clicking it speaks the question text via the browser Web Speech API.
- **AC 9.1.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When the answer is revealed in Practice-mode Quiz (for Test see AC 9.1.5), a speaker-icon button appears next to the "Correct Answer" label. Clicking it speaks the answer text.
- **AC 9.1.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Starting a new TTS utterance cancels any utterance already in progress so speech does not overlap.
- **AC 9.1.4** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Clicking the same speaker button again while its text is still playing stops that utterance instead of restarting it.
- **AC 9.1.5** [Anyone] **Given** a started, unsubmitted Test, **then** **Read question aloud** stays available, but "Read answer aloud" and option pronunciation hints are not rendered. Speech in progress is cancelled when the Test is submitted or times out.

### AC 9.2 Explore (flashcard) mode playback

- **AC 9.2.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] A speaker-icon button appears in the question-face header of the flashcard in Explore mode. Clicking it speaks the question text without flipping the card.
- **AC 9.2.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] A speaker-icon button appears in the answer-face header of the flashcard in Explore mode. Clicking it speaks the answer text without flipping the card back.
- **AC 9.2.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Clicking the same speaker button again while its text is still playing stops that utterance instead of restarting it.

### AC 9.3 Lifecycle and fallback

- **AC 9.3.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When the user navigates away from a Quiz or Explore page, any in-progress speech is cancelled immediately.
- **AC 9.3.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] If `window.speechSynthesis` or `SpeechSynthesisUtterance` is unavailable, the speaker buttons are not rendered and the application does not throw.
- **AC 9.3.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] No backend calls, database fields, or cloud services are involved in TTS playback; all processing is client-side only.

### AC 9.4 List mode "Listen All"

- **AC 9.4.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] In list mode (category items view and collection detail view), a "Listen All" button appears in the control bar when `window.speechSynthesis` is available.
- **AC 9.4.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Pressing "Listen All" begins sequential TTS playback: speaks the question, pauses 2 seconds, then speaks the correct answer (regardless of the "Show/Hide answers" toggle), then moves to the next item, repeating until all items in the current list are read.
- **AC 9.4.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] While playback is active, the button label changes to "Pause". Pressing it cancels in-progress speech and pauses playback; the button changes to "Resume".
- **AC 9.4.4** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Pressing "Resume" restarts playback from the beginning of the item that was paused on.
- **AC 9.4.5** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When the last item's answer has been spoken, playback stops automatically and the button returns to "Listen All".
- **AC 9.4.6** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Navigating away from the list view cancels any in-progress "Listen All" playback.
