## AC 10. Featured page

A public page at `/featured` shows Admin-curated Sets and Collections with modification timestamps and sorting.

### AC 10.1 Data model

- **AC 10.1.1** [Admin] A `FeaturedItems` table stores entries of two types: `Set` (category slug + optional L1/L2 navigation keyword names) and `Collection` (reference to an existing Collection by ID). Each entry has a `DisplayName`, `SortOrder`, `CreatedBy`, and `CreatedAt`.
- **AC 10.1.2** [Admin] An `Item` record gains an `UpdatedAt` (`DateTime?`) column. It is set to `DateTime.UtcNow` whenever an item is modified via `UpdateItem`, `UpdateItemCategories`, `SetItemVisibility`, `ApproveItem`, or `RejectItem`.

### AC 10.2 API — Public

- **AC 10.2.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] `GET /featured` returns two arrays: `sets` and `collections`, each ordered by `SortOrder ASC, DisplayName ASC`.
- **AC 10.2.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Each Set entry includes: `id`, `displayName`, `categorySlug`, `navKeyword1`, `navKeyword2`, `itemCount` (count of public items matching the set's category/keyword path), `lastModifiedAt` (max of `COALESCE(UpdatedAt, CreatedAt)` across those items, or `null` if no items exist), `sortOrder`. The keyword name-to-ID lookup used to filter items **must only consider public (`IsPrivate = false`) keywords and must deduplicate by name** (the `Keywords` unique index is on `(Name, CreatedBy, IsPrivate)`, so the same name can appear multiple times across different creators; the lookup takes the first match per lowercase name to avoid a duplicate-key crash).
- **AC 10.2.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Each Collection entry includes: `id`, `collectionId`, `displayName`, `description`, `itemCount`, `lastModifiedAt` (max of `COALESCE(UpdatedAt, CreatedAt)` across items in the collection, or `null` if no items), `sortOrder`.

### AC 10.3 API — Admin

- **AC 10.3.1** [Admin] `GET /admin/featured` returns all featured items with full fields (type, display name, category/keyword path or collection reference, sort order, created at).
- **AC 10.3.2** [Admin] `POST /admin/featured` adds a new featured item. Requires `type` ("Set" or "Collection"), `displayName` (max 200 chars), and type-specific fields (`categorySlug` + `navKeyword1` for sets; `collectionId` for collections). `sortOrder` is optional; if omitted, the item is appended after the highest-ordered item of its type. Returns 201 with `{ id }`. Returns 409 Conflict if the identical set (`categorySlug` + `navKeyword1` + `navKeyword2`) or collection (`collectionId`) is already featured.
- **AC 10.3.3** [Admin] `PATCH /admin/featured/{id}` updates `displayName` and/or `sortOrder` on an existing item. Returns 204. Returns 404 if not found.
- **AC 10.3.4** [Admin] `DELETE /admin/featured/{id}` removes a featured item. Returns 204. Returns 404 if not found.
- **AC 10.3.5** [Anonymous, Authenticated (non-admin)] All `/admin/featured` endpoints return 403 Forbidden.
- **AC 10.3.6** [Admin] `POST /items/bulk` is an admin-only endpoint (AC 2.2.6.17a-c): admins succeed, authenticated non-admins get 403, anonymous callers get 401.

### AC 10.4 UI — Featured page

- **AC 10.4.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] A "Featured" link appears in the main nav between Categories and Collections (desktop and mobile).
- **AC 10.4.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] `/featured` renders a full-bleed dark page with a tab switcher (Sets | Collections) and sort controls (Last Modified | Name).
- **AC 10.4.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Each Set card shows: display name, category › L1 › L2 path as subtitle, item count, and last modified date. Clicking navigates to `/categories/:categorySlug/:kw1/:kw2?from=featured`.
- **AC 10.4.4** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Each Collection card shows: display name, description (if any), item count, and last modified date. Clicking navigates to the collection detail page with `?from=featured`.
- **AC 10.4.5** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Sorting by "Last Modified" orders cards newest-first. Sorting by "Name" orders alphabetically. Default is "Last Modified".
- **AC 10.4.6** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When navigated from Featured (i.e. `?from=featured` is present), both CategoriesPage and CollectionDetailPage display a "← Back to Featured" link at the top of the page content.
- **AC 10.4.7** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When a tab (Sets or Collections) has no items, the page shows an empty state: "No featured {tab} yet. Check back soon."

### AC 10.5 UI — Admin Featured page

- **AC 10.5.1** [Admin] `/admin/featured` is linked from the Admin Dashboard.
- **AC 10.5.2** [Admin] The page has a tab switcher (Sets | Collections). Each tab shows two panels: an "Add" panel on the left and the current ordered list on the right.
- **AC 10.5.3** [Admin] The Add panel has a free-text search box. For Sets, it filters all `cat › L1` and `cat › L1 › L2` taxonomy combinations client-side (loaded from `GET /taxonomy`). For Collections, it searches public collections via `GET /collections/discover`. Each result row has a checkbox. Already-featured items are greyed out and their checkboxes are disabled.
- **AC 10.5.4** [Admin] Checking one or more rows and clicking "Add N selected" bulk-adds them via parallel `POST /admin/featured` calls. Display names are auto-generated from the taxonomy path (e.g. `Cloud Computing › Aws › Saa C03`) or collection name. Items are appended at the bottom of the ordered list (sortOrder auto-assigned by backend).
- **AC 10.5.5** [Admin] The current list shows items ordered by `sortOrder`. Each row has ↑/↓ buttons to reorder: clicking swaps the item's position with its neighbor and updates `sortOrder` on all affected items via `PATCH`. The first item has no ↑ button; the last has no ↓ button.
- **AC 10.5.6** [Admin] Each row has a pencil icon for inline rename (display name only) and a trash icon to remove. Changes take effect immediately after save.

## AC 11. Global search (SEARCH-001..003, #324)

**Design:** One endpoint, `GET /search`, searches topics (the YAML taxonomy, in memory), discoverable public collections, public questions, and the signed-in caller's own content. Topics match on slugs and descriptions plus a hand-kept alias list (for example `ai` also reads as "artificial intelligence", so "artificial intelligence" finds `tech › ai-ml`), with prefix and one-typo tolerance. Collections use PostgreSQL full-text search plus trigram typo tolerance on the name; questions use full-text search only. Results come back grouped, each group shaped `{ results, hasMore }`. **The search text is never stored or logged**: it is not written to page views (AC 5.3.21), analytics events (AC 5.4.11), or request logs (AC 11.14). Topic counts are **public items only**, refreshed lazily at most every 10 minutes.

**Deviations from the issue text (also listed in the PR):** (1) there are no keyword full-text or trigram indexes, topic search is in memory; (2) the AI topic is `tech › ai-ml` (there is no `artificial-intelligence` node); (3) categories and topics are shown by slug with their YAML description, because the taxonomy has no separate display names; (4) groups are `{ results, hasMore }` objects, not bare arrays; (5) recent searches are not stored anywhere in v1, so the Privacy Policy is unchanged; (6) the legacy `StudyGuide` is not searched (it is being retired by #296); only study guide documents are; (7) the header shows the inline box at `xl` and above and the full-screen overlay below `xl` (there is no popover at 1024-1279 px), with no "recent searches" and no offline state; the 429 notice is static with a manual retry.

**API**

- **AC 11.1** [Anonymous] **Given** I call `GET /search?q=artificial intelligence`, **then** the API returns **200** and `groups.subcategories.results[0]` is `{ categorySlug: "tech", l1: "ai-ml", l2: null, ... }`. A typo such as `artifical` returns the same top result. Anyone may call the endpoint; no sign-in is needed.
- **AC 11.2** [Anonymous] **Given** `q`, **then** it is trimmed and must be 2-100 characters, otherwise **400**. `types` is a comma-separated list of `categories`, `subcategories`, `collections`, `items`, `mine` (case-insensitive; default: all); an unknown value returns **400**. `limit` is 1-20 (default 5) and `offset` is 0-200 (default 0), otherwise **400**. Each group returns at most `limit` results and `hasMore` is true only while `offset + limit` is at most 200 and more rows exist (so `hasMore` is false once the next page would start beyond the 200 cap).
- **AC 11.3** [Anonymous] **Given** a group was not requested, or is not permitted, **then** that group is `null` in the response. `mine` is always `null` for anonymous callers and for authenticated callers whose app user id is unresolved; requesting `types=mine` anonymously returns **200** with every group `null` (not an error).
- **AC 11.4** [Anonymous] **Given** `q` contains no searchable word (for example only punctuation) or only English stop words, **then** the API returns **200** with empty groups, and a query made only of excluded words (`-foo`) matches nothing. In topic (category and subcategory) search a negated word (a `-` at the start of a word) is skipped, so an exclusion never adds a topic match. Quotes (`"exact phrase"`), `or`, and `-exclude` are honoured. The last word, while it is still being typed (no trailing space, not inside quotes, not negated), also matches as a prefix. Queries are limited to 8 words of at most 40 characters each. Search syntax characters such as `! | ( : '` and non-ASCII text (for example `café`, `日本`, `½`, an emoji) never cause an error. If PostgreSQL still rejects a query the API returns **200** with empty groups and logs a warning that does not contain `q`; it never returns 500.
- **AC 11.5** [Anonymous] **Given** topic results, **then** `categories` and `subcategories` cover every taxonomy node (12 categories, L1 and L2 nodes) with `itemCount` equal to the number of **public** items under that node (private items are never counted, whoever is asking). Slugs repeat across categories (`general`), so a subcategory is identified by `categorySlug`, `l1` and `l2` together. Ranking: match quality (exact word, then prefix, then one typo; slug/alias matches above description matches), then category before L1 before L2, then nodes with items first, then more items, then slug.
- **AC 11.6** [Anonymous] **Given** collection results, **then** only collections that are public **and** discoverable are searched (a public collection that is not discoverable is not returned), ranked by relevance plus a small boost for item count. `itemCount` counts only non-private items. When I am signed in, my own collections are left out of this group because they appear in `mine`.
- **AC 11.7** [Anonymous] **Given** question results, **then** only items with `IsPrivate = false` are searched; being in a public collection does not make another user's private item discoverable, and admins get no extra visibility. Matching uses Question, CorrectAnswer and Explanation (weights A, B, C). Each result carries `snippet.text` (the Question only, plain text) and `snippet.highlights` (`{ start, length }` ranges). Offsets are UTF-16 code unit indices, the text never contains highlight markers, and CorrectAnswer and Explanation are never returned.
- **AC 11.8** [Anonymous] **Given** an item result, **then** `categorySlug`, `l1` and `l2` are returned only for a non-private category and non-private navigation keywords; otherwise those fields are `null`. No emails, `CreatedBy` values or other users' identifiers are returned.
- **AC 11.9** [Authenticated] **Given** I am signed in, **then** `mine` searches only my own: collections I created (any visibility), my unexpired study guide documents (`ExpiresUtc` in the future, title match), and my private items. Each result is `{ kind, id, title }` with `kind` one of `collection`, `studyGuideDocument`, `item`. I never see another user's private items, collections or study guide documents, and an expired document is not returned even if it is mine.
- **AC 11.10** [Anonymous] **Given** a caller makes more than 120 `GET /search` requests in one minute, **then** the API returns **429** with a `Retry-After` header and `detail` "Too many searches. Please wait a moment and try again." The window is a fixed one-minute window partitioned by user subject when authenticated, otherwise by the server-trusted client IP (`Connection.RemoteIpAddress`), exactly as in AC 5.6.4: a client-sent `X-Forwarded-For` value never selects the partition, so rotating it buys no budget. The production `ForwardedHeaders:KnownNetworks` value is verified before deploy.
- **AC 11.11** [System] **Given** the OpenAPI artifact is regenerated, **then** `GET /search` appears under the `Search` tag with 200, 400 and 429 responses.
- **AC 11.12** [System] **Given** the topic counts are older than 10 minutes, **when** the next search asks for topics, **then** the counts are reloaded (public items only); a failed reload keeps the previous counts and retries after a minute.
- **AC 11.13** [System] **Given** the page-view and analytics APIs, **then** the text a visitor searched for is never accepted or stored: `/search` page views lose their query string (AC 5.3.21) and the search analytics events have no field for it (AC 5.4.11).
- **AC 11.14** [System] **Given** ASP.NET Core logs a request for `/search` (for example "Request starting ... /search?q=..." or "Request finished"), **then** the logged `QueryString` is replaced with `?[redacted]`, so the search text does not reach the console or Loki sinks. The API itself never logs `q`.

**UI (header search and the `/search` page, #321)**

- **AC 11.15** [Anyone] **Given** the header, **then** exactly one search variant renders at a time (chosen with a media query, so ids never duplicate): at `xl` (1280 px) and up an inline input (224 px wide, `role="search"` named "Site search", with a visible shortcut hint while empty and unfocused ("⌘K" on Apple platforms, "Ctrl K" elsewhere)); below `xl` a magnifier icon button (`aria-label` "Search") that opens the full-screen overlay. No navigation link is hidden or wrapped at 1024 px or 1280 px. The dropdown sits above page content (the header has its own stacking level). There is no popover at 1024-1279 px.
- **AC 11.16** [Anyone] **Given** I open the overlay (icon or shortcut), **then** a modal dialog named "Search" fills the screen, the input is focused (text 16 px or larger, so iOS does not zoom), the page behind does not scroll, Tab cycles between the input and Cancel only, and the page has no horizontal scroll at 375 px. **When** I press Escape or Cancel, **then** it closes and focus returns to the icon. Choosing a result, or any route change, closes it without moving focus back; crossing into `xl` swaps to the inline input and keeps the text.
- **AC 11.17** [Anyone] **Given** I press Ctrl+K (or Cmd+K), **then** search opens or focuses with any text selected, even from inside another input, and the browser default is prevented, **unless** another dialog (`role="dialog"` or `aria-modal`) is mounted. `/` does the same but follows the AC 3.16.5 ignore rules (inputs, textareas, selects, contentEditable, textbox/combobox roles, IME composition, auto-repeat, already-handled keys, Ctrl/Alt/Meta held, a mounted dialog); the predicate is shared with the study hotkeys.
- **AC 11.18** [Anyone] **Given** I type 2 or more characters, **then** after a 200 ms pause one `GET /search` runs (earlier in-flight requests are cancelled, nothing is sent per keystroke, nothing is sent below 2 characters) with `limit=5`, and the dropdown lists groups Topics, Collections, Questions and (signed in) Mine, empty groups omitted, at most 5 rows each; Topics merges categories and subcategories (subcategories shown as `tech › ai-ml` with the slugs highlighted, description and public item count beside them). A group with more shows a "See all ..." row that opens `/search?q=...&tab=...`; a final "See all results for “...”" row opens `/search?q=...`. Question rows show the server snippet with highlights (rendered as text only, never as HTML) and `category › l1 › l2`. Titles are highlighted from the typed words on the client.
- **AC 11.19** [Anyone] **Given** the dropdown is open, **then** DOM focus stays in the input and the first row becomes active once results for the current text arrive; ArrowDown/ArrowUp move through rows (headings skipped) and wrap; Alt+ArrowDown or ArrowDown on a closed panel opens it; Enter opens the active row; Escape closes the panel and keeps the text, and a second Escape clears the input; Tab closes the panel and moves on. **When** results shown belong to earlier text (still debouncing or loading), **then** nothing is active and Enter searches the text as typed at `/search?q=...`; Enter with no active row does the same. Rows ignore mouse-down so the input keeps focus.
- **AC 11.20** [Anyone] **Given** the input, **then** it is a `role="combobox"` with `aria-autocomplete="list"`, `aria-keyshortcuts="Control+K Meta+K"` and a screen-reader label "Search Quizymode"; `aria-expanded` is true only while a listbox with at least one option is shown and `aria-controls` and `aria-activedescendant` are set only then; the listbox (`role="listbox"`, groups labelled by their headings, options with unique ids and `aria-selected`) is not rendered for hint, empty, loading or rate-limited states; a persistent polite live region announces "N results available. Use up and down arrow keys to navigate.", "No results for ..." and the error. axe reports no WCAG 2 A/AA/2.1 AA violations in the open dropdown.
- **AC 11.21** [Anyone] **Given** the dropdown, **then** with an empty input it shows "Search topics, collections and questions."; with one character "Keep typing. Search needs at least 2 characters." and sends nothing; while the first request is in flight three skeleton rows (panel `aria-busy`); while a newer request runs the previous rows stay at reduced opacity with a spinner in the input; with no results "No results for “...”", "Check the spelling or try fewer words." and a "Browse all categories" link (no listbox); on a non-429 failure "Could not load search results." plus a "Try again" option reachable with ArrowDown and Enter (never auto-activated, never retried automatically); on **429** a static amber notice "You are searching too fast. Try again in N seconds." (N from `Retry-After`, otherwise "Please wait a few seconds and try again.") with a manual "Try again" button; there is no automatic retry and no request is made until it is used.
- **AC 11.22** [Anyone] **Given** I am anonymous, **then** the UI never requests `mine`, there is no Mine group or tab, and `tab=mine` on `/search` is treated as All. **Given** I am signed in, **then** the dropdown and the All tab request `mine` and show my collections, study guide documents and private questions (labelled Collection, Study guide, Question), linking to `/collections/:id/:slug`, `/study-guides/documents/:id` and `/items/:id`. A null `mine` group counts as empty.
- **AC 11.23** [Anyone] **Given** I open `/search?q=&tab=all|topics|collections|questions|mine&page=`, **then** the URL is the only source of truth (an invalid tab becomes All; `page` is 1-11 and ignored on All; `q` is capped at 100 characters), the page is `noindex` with title "Search" (the text never appears in the title), the heading is `Results for “...”` (or "Search"), a search form (`role="search"`, "Search Quizymode") is prefilled and submitting keeps the tab and resets the page. A tab list (All, Topics, Collections, Questions, and Mine when signed in; no counts) uses `role="tablist"`/`tab`/`tabpanel`, a roving tabindex, ArrowLeft/ArrowRight (wrapping), Home and End; tab and page changes replace the history entry. With fewer than 2 characters it shows "Type at least 2 characters to search topics, collections and questions." and sends nothing. The page lazy-loads (AC 8.2). axe reports no violations on any tab and there is no horizontal scroll at 375 px.
- **AC 11.24** [Anyone] **Given** the All tab, **then** it requests `limit=5` and shows each non-empty group as a section with a heading and a "See all ..." button that switches tab. **Given** a single-type tab, **then** it requests `limit=20` and `offset=(page-1)*20`, rows are real links, Previous is disabled on page 1, Next is enabled only when the group reports `hasMore` (so it is disabled at the 220-result cap), the previous rows stay at reduced opacity while the next page loads, and after a page change focus moves to a hidden heading such as "Topics, page 2". Topics shows categories and subcategories together, so a Topics page can hold up to 40 rows. With no results: All shows a dashed card with "Browse categories" and "Browse collections" links; a single-type tab shows "No ... for “...”" with a "Search everything" button. Errors and 429 use the same copy and manual retry as AC 11.21.
- **AC 11.25** [Anyone] **Given** a result, **then** it links to: category `/categories/{slug}`; subcategory `/categories/{category}/{l1}` or `/categories/{category}/{l1}/{l2}`; collection `/collections/{id}/{name-slug}`; question `/items/{id}`; my study guide document `/study-guides/documents/{id}`.
- **AC 11.26** [System] **Given** analytics, **then** `search_performed` fires once per committed search with `total` (the number of results shown, zero allowed) and context type `search`: when a result is chosen from the dropdown, and when `/search` finishes loading results for a new text (not per keystroke, not on tab or page changes, and not again for the dropdown "See all"/Enter paths, which land on `/search`). `search_result_clicked` fires on choosing a result with its 1-based `position` and a context: `category` with the category slug, `collection` with the collection id, `item` with the item id, or `mine` with no id for the caller's own content. No payload, URL path or query string sent to the analytics APIs contains the search text (AC 5.3.21, 5.4.11).
- **AC 11.27** [Admin] **Given** I open Usage Analytics, **then** the new `search` feature area is labelled "Search" in the area tables.
- **AC 11.28** [Anyone] **Given** I use search in the web app, **then** cached search results are keyed per user (anonymous visitors share one key), so another account on the same tab never sees my `mine` titles, and all cached search results are purged when I sign out.

## AC 12. Topic suggestion and topic picker (#325)

**Design:** `POST /taxonomy/suggest` turns free text (a question and its answer, pasted notes, an import preview) into up to `limit` full `category > L1 > L2` topic paths, with no LLM. It is an in-memory BM25 model over three document levels (category, L1, L2): each document is the node's own slug and description (slug tokens count 3, description tokens 2) plus one count per eligible public item containing a term, so a large topic does not win by repetition. Text is tokenized, stop words and one-letter words are dropped, aliases are expanded (`AWS` also reads as "amazon web services"; the two-letter aliases `ai`, `ml`, `os`, `ci`, `cd`, `db`, `bi` expand only when typed in capitals), and words are lightly stemmed (plural, -ing, -ed, -ly). A path scores `0.5 * L2 + 0.35 * parent L1 + 0.15 * category` (each normalised to 0-1 per level), so evidence anywhere under an L1 lifts its best leaf; chips are the best L2 under each of the top distinct L1s. Text with fewer than 2 analysed terms, or whose best match covers less than 8 % of the evidence its terms could give, falls back to the caller's own recent paths, then popular public paths, then a static list, so the list is never empty. Accuracy was measured by 10-fold cross-validation over `data/seed-source` (13.5k items; every held-out item is absent from the model that scores it); the opt-in report is `QM_TOPIC_EVAL=1 dotnet test --filter TopicSuggestionEvalTests`. The text is read from the request body, never logged, stored or echoed, and no analytics event can carry it (AC 5.4.12). Loading and egress limits are in AC 8.3. The picker and chips UI is specified in the UI section of this chapter.

**Deviations from the issue text:** (1) paths use slugs because the taxonomy has no display names (`tech > ai-ml > deep-learning`), plus a `description`; (2) the AI example reads `tech > ai-ml` (there is no `artificial-intelligence` node); (3) `l2` is always present, because every create flow requires L2; (4) the response is `{ suggestions: [...] }`, not a bare array; (5) `reason` has a third value `yours`, so a caller's own paths are not labelled Popular; (6) the model is not rebuilt at startup or hourly: it loads lazily with the bounds in AC 8.3; (7) stemming runs in process (a light stemmer), not through a database call per request.

**API**

- **AC 12.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /taxonomy/suggest` with `{ "text": "Neural networks learn weights with backpropagation; deep learning models and machine learning training" }`, **then** the API returns **200** with `{ suggestions: [...] }` and one of the first 3 suggestions is `categorySlug: "tech"`, `l1: "ai-ml"` with `reason: "text"`; `{ "text": "EC2 instances, S3 buckets and VPC subnets" }` returns an `exams` path whose L1 starts with `aws` among the first 3. Each suggestion is `{ categorySlug, l1, l2, path, description, score, matchedTerms, reason }`: `l2` is always present and `(categorySlug, l1, l2)` always passes the taxonomy's navigation-path check; `path` is the three slugs joined with " › "; `description` is the L2 node's YAML description; `reason` is `text`, `yours` or `popular`. For `text`, `score` is the 0-1 path score rounded to 3 places and `matchedTerms` holds at most 5 of the words from my input (lowercased, first occurrence) whose stems appear in the suggested L2's profile; for the other reasons `score` is 0 and `matchedTerms` is `[]`. Suggestions never repeat an L1 for `text`. (backend tests: SuggestTopicsHttpTests, TopicSuggestionQualityTests)
- **AC 12.2** [Authenticated] **Given** the body, **then** `text` is optional (null means empty) and at most 5000 characters after trimming, otherwise **400**; `limit` is 1-10 (default 5), otherwise **400**; a malformed JSON body returns **400**; a body larger than 64 KB is rejected before it is read (**413** or **400**). Validation messages are fixed strings and the response never contains any part of the text. Anonymous callers get **401**.
- **AC 12.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I call it more than 60 times in a minute (a fixed window partitioned by my user subject), **then** the 61st call returns **429** with a `Retry-After` header and `detail` "Too many topic suggestions. Please wait a moment and try again."; other users are unaffected.
- **AC 12.4** [Authenticated] **Given** my text has at least 2 distinct analysed terms, **then** it is scored against the model. The first such call after a start asks for the profile load (AC 8.3); until it finishes the response is computed from taxonomy text only, whose weak evidence usually yields the fallback (the load takes a second or two). If the best match is too weak (confidence below 0.08) or nothing matches, the response is the fallback (AC 12.5). Text results are never padded with fallback entries.
- **AC 12.5** [Authenticated] **Given** my text is empty, shorter than 2 analysed terms, or matches nothing confidently (for example `""`, `"hi"`, `"asdf qwer"`), **then** the API returns **200** with up to `limit` entries and `score` 0: first my own most recent distinct valid paths (`reason: "yours"`, from my items, private ones included, newest first, cached per user for 5 minutes), then the most popular public paths (`reason: "popular"`, by public item count, from the same counts as AC 11.5), then, if the database has too few, the first leaf of each L1 in taxonomy order (`reason: "popular"`). The list is never empty and has no repeated path; every entry passes the navigation-path check, so legacy paths are never offered. This path never starts a profile load (AC 8.3.1).
- **AC 12.6** [Authenticated] **Given** another user's items, **then** their paths never appear as `yours` for me, and their private items never contribute to any score.
- **AC 12.7** [System] **Given** which items feed the profiles, **then** only items with `IsPrivate = false`, in a non-private category, with two non-private navigation keywords and a path that is valid in the YAML taxonomy; private items (mine too), private categories, private keywords and legacy paths contribute nothing. A word that appears only in private items (for example `zzqxv`) therefore never makes any topic match `text: "zzqxv zzqxv"`; that call returns the fallback. When an item's visibility changes or it is deleted, the change is reflected within `MinReloadInterval` (12 hours) at the latest. A category or navigation keyword turning private is not part of the reload fingerprint, so it is reflected at the next seed sync or within `MaxModelAge` (7 days). (backend tests: TopicSuggestionProfilesTests, SuggestTopicsHttpTests)
- **AC 12.8** [System] **Given** quality, **then** on 10-fold cross-validation over `data/seed-source` the model puts the true (category, L1) among the first 3 chips for at least 70 % of items (question + answer + explanation), and the true category first for at least 90 %; the evaluation report lists the numbers, per-category rows, and ablations (taxonomy-only, explanation cap, stemmer, path weights, threshold). A regression guard test fails if a held-out fold drops below 88 % for the first category or 85 % for the first three L1s. (backend tests: TopicSuggestionQualityTests; opt-in TopicSuggestionEvalTests)

**UI (#323)**

- **AC 12.9** [Authenticated] **Given** I use Create item (`/create/question`), Edit item, Generate Quiz from notes (`/create/notes`), Import (`/create/import`) or the legacy Study Guide import, **then** each shows the same **Topic** * group (one `fieldset` with the legend "Topic *"): suggested topic chips (Create item, notes and import only), the searchable picker (`role="combobox"`, labelled "Search topics"), and a **Browse all topics** disclosure holding the three cascading selects (**Category**, **Primary topic (rank 1)**, **Subtopic (rank 2)**, ids `{prefix}-category`, `-rank1`, `-rank2`). Nothing in the group is ever chosen for me: no chip is pre-selected or pre-highlighted, and no code path applies a suggestion without my click or Enter. (frontend tests: ItemTopicScopeFields.test.tsx)
- **AC 12.10** [Authenticated] **Given** a create form with chips, **then** one request with empty text is made on mount (its answer is cached for the session, so the common topics show before anything is typed), and after that a request is made only after a 600 ms pause in typing when the composed text, trimmed, has at least 20 characters (the text is capped at 5,000 characters, sent in the request body only, and never put in a URL, a query key or a log: the cache key holds a hash). With 1 to 19 characters no request is made and the chips already shown stay; unchanged text makes no request; clearing the text returns to the cached common topics. At most 5 chips show, headed "Suggested from your text" (reason `text`) or "Common topics" (reasons `yours`, `popular`, with the badges **Yours** and **Popular**; none for `text`). Each chip is a button of at least 44 px whose name is its path, description and badge; on a phone the chips stack full width, from 640 px they wrap. While the first answer is pending three skeleton bars show; while newer text is searched the old chips dim (and "Finding topics…" shows when there are none). (frontend tests: useTopicSuggestions.test.tsx, SuggestedTopicChips.test.tsx, topic-suggest.spec.ts)
- **AC 12.11** [Authenticated] **Given** Edit item, **then** the Topic group has the picker and Browse but no chips, and the page never calls `POST /taxonomy/suggest`. (The legacy Study Guide import page that shared this rule was removed in 3.101.0, #296.) (frontend tests: EditItemPage.test.tsx)
- **AC 12.12** [Authenticated] **Given** the text a flow suggests from, **then** Create item uses the question, correct answer and explanation; notes use the title plus the pasted text, or for `.txt` and `.md` files the title plus the first 20,000 bytes of the file read in the browser (`file.slice(0, 20_000).text()`), or for `.pdf`, `.docx` and `.pptx` the title plus the file name without its extension (with the note "Topic suggestions use your title and file name for this file type." under the chips), because the text of those files is only extracted on the server after the document is created; Import uses the question and correct answer of the first 20 rows of the pasted JSON, or of the Paste-text preview. Each is capped at 5,000 characters. (frontend tests: suggestionText.test.ts, StudyGuideUploadPage.test.tsx, UploadToCollectionPage.test.tsx)
- **AC 12.13** [Authenticated] **Given** the three states of the group, **then** (A) with nothing chosen it shows the chips, the picker and a collapsed **Browse all topics**, and suggestion requests are allowed; (P) with a partly set or legacy value (for example `?category=` alone, or a stored L1/L2 not in the taxonomy) it shows no chips and makes no request, shows the picker, opens **Browse all topics** and keeps the amber warnings ("Current value is not in the taxonomy list for this category. Choose a valid primary topic." and "Current value is not valid under this primary topic. Choose a valid subtopic."); (B) with a full valid path it shows only one removable breadcrumb pill (`tech › ai-ml › nlp`, with an "×" button named "Remove topic tech, ai-ml, nlp"), while the three selects stay mounted, hidden, so their ids and labels still resolve. A URL prefill counts as my choice (P or B), so chips never replace it. A chip, picker or Browse choice sets category, L1 and L2 in one change; typing afterwards changes no topic and makes no request; an answer that arrives after a choice is discarded. After a choice focus moves to the "×" button; the "×" clears all three values, focuses the search box and brings the cached chips back without a new request. A single polite live region announces "N topic suggestions available.", "Selected topic tech, ai-ml, nlp.", "Topic removed." and the picker's result counts. (frontend tests: ItemTopicScopeFields.test.tsx, CreateItemPage.topic.test.tsx)
- **AC 12.14** [Authenticated] **Given** a form whose topic is not a full category > L1 > L2 path, **when** I submit, **then** the page blocks the submit in JavaScript (the category select has no `required`, because a required control in a closed disclosure blocks the browser invisibly), shows "Choose a topic: pick a suggestion, search, or browse all topics." under the group (`aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby` on the group, `role="alert"`) and moves focus to the search box. This replaces the former messages "Select a category.", "Primary topic (rank 1) and subtopic (rank 2) are required." and the browser's native message on Create item, Edit item, notes, Import and the legacy Study Guide import; the error clears as soon as the path is complete. (frontend tests: CreateItemPage.topic.test.tsx, StudyGuideUploadPage.test.tsx, UploadToCollectionPage.test.tsx, EditItemPage.test.tsx)
- **AC 12.15** [Authenticated] **Given** the picker, **then** it filters the taxonomy's paths locally (no request): it lists nothing under 2 characters (the hint row says "Type 2 or more letters of a topic, such as aws or neural."), and from 2 characters every word must match a category, L1 or L2 slug word or the L2 description (exact, prefix, substring, or one typo for words of 4 or more characters, ranked in that order), so any L2 slug of 3 or more characters, optionally preceded by its L1 (`aws saa`), lists its path. Results are grouped by category with the matched words highlighted (rendered as text, never HTML), at most 50 options ("Showing the first 50. Keep typing to narrow it down." when more match); a miss says "No topics match “xyz”. Try a shorter word, or browse all topics." with a button that opens Browse. The input is `role="combobox"` with `aria-autocomplete="list"`; `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` and `aria-activedescendant` are set only while the list shows. Focus stays in the input; ArrowDown and ArrowUp move through the options (skipping the group headings, wrapping), no option is active until I move to one, Enter picks the active option (and never submits the form), Escape closes and a second Escape clears, Tab closes without picking, Alt+ArrowDown opens, and keys are ignored while composing text. On a phone the list is inline under the input (not an overlay) and the page does not scroll sideways; from 640 px it is anchored under the input. While the taxonomy loads the input is disabled with the placeholder "Loading topics…"; if it fails the input is disabled with "Couldn't load topics. Reload the page to try again." (frontend tests: topicPaths.test.ts over the committed 1,108-L2 fixture, TopicPathCombobox.test.tsx, topic-suggest.spec.ts with axe)
- **AC 12.16** [Authenticated] **Given** `POST /taxonomy/suggest` fails (a network error, 400, 429 or 5xx), **then** the chips quietly disappear (no banner, no retry button) and the picker and Browse keep working; no automatic retry is made, and after a 429 no further suggestion request is made for 60 seconds. (frontend tests: useTopicSuggestions.test.tsx, topic-suggest.spec.ts)
- **AC 12.17** [Authenticated] **Given** the caches, **then** `GET /taxonomy` is cached under `["taxonomy", userId or "anon"]` on every screen that reads it, suggestions under `["topic-suggest", userId or "anon", hash of the text]`, and signing out removes both (suggestions include my own recent paths). (frontend tests: AuthContext.test.tsx)
- **AC 12.18** [Authenticated] **Given** the analytics for chips (AC 5.4.12), **then** nothing is sent while I work or when I pick: on a successful save of Create item, notes or Import, if at least one chip was ever shown, the page sends `topic_suggestion_shown` (once per form session; `total` and the reason of the first chips shown) and exactly one of `topic_suggestion_accepted` (the saved path is a chip that was shown, picked by chip, picker or Browse; `position` is that chip's latest 1-based rank) or `topic_suggestion_overridden`; the path (`category/l1/l2`) is included only when the saved item is public and omitted for a private item (notes and imports always are). No event carries any text, and nothing is sent when no chip was ever shown. (frontend tests: topicSuggestionAnalytics.test.ts, CreateItemPage.topic.test.tsx, topic-suggest.spec.ts)

## AC 13. LLM model-list audit (#480)

**Design:** A daily background job (`LlmModelAuditService`) calls each configured vendor's model-list endpoint (`GET {BaseUrl}/models`; the key is sent in a header, `x-goog-api-key` for Gemini and `Authorization: Bearer` for the others, never in the URL; no generation, no token spend) and compares it with the model saved for that vendor in the admin AI-provider settings (AC 5.12). The first run happens 24 hours after the API starts, then every 24 hours. The job runs only when `Llm:ModelAudit:Enabled` is `true` (default `false`). Each call has a 15 s timeout, a 1 MB body cap and a 5000-model cap; keys, key-bearing URLs and response bodies are never logged. Nothing here ever changes a model: the admin changes it on the AI providers page.

- **AC 13.1** [System] **Given** a vendor with no API key (or no base URL), **then** it is skipped (logged as skipped) and produces no finding.
- **AC 13.2** [System] **Given** a vendor answers 401, 403, 5xx, times out, returns an oversized or unreadable body, **then** the check is logged as failed for that vendor and no finding is created or changed.
- **AC 13.3** [System] **Given** the configured model is in the vendor's list (Gemini ids are compared without the `models/` prefix), **then** no finding is created and no email is sent.
- **AC 13.4** [System] **Given** the configured model is missing from the list, **then** one open finding (`ModelMissing`, vendor, model, first and last seen) is stored in `LlmModelFindings`. A partial unique index allows only one open finding per vendor and model, so reruns and overlapping runs (timer and manual, or two instances) keep one row.
- **AC 13.5** [System] **Given** open findings that were not yet notified, **then** each run sends one digest email to `Llm:ModelAudit:AdminAddress` over SMTP (SES endpoint: `Llm:ModelAudit:Smtp:Host`, `Port`, `UserName`, `Password`, and `From`), and marks them notified only after a successful send. A finding is emailed once, not repeated daily. If the SMTP settings are missing or the send fails, a warning without any setting values is logged, the run does not fail, and the next run retries. No email is sent on a clean run. Secrets come only from environment variables or secret configuration, never from `appsettings*.json`.
- **AC 13.6** [System] **Given** an open finding, **when** the model is listed again, or an admin has switched that vendor to another model, **then** the next audit run marks the finding resolved. There is no manual dismiss.
- **AC 13.7** [Admin] **Given** `GET /admin/llm-model-findings`, **then** it returns **200** `{ findings: [{ id, vendor, model, kind, firstSeenUtc, lastSeenUtc }] }` with open findings only; anonymous callers get **401**, non-admins **403**.
- **AC 13.8** [Admin] **Given** `POST /admin/llm-model-audit/run`, **then** it runs the audit now and returns **200** `{ newFindings, resolved, emailSent }`; a second call within `Llm:ModelAudit:ManualRunCooldownMinutes` (default 10) returns **429** with `Retry-After`; anonymous callers get **401**, non-admins **403**. It works even when the daily job is disabled.
- **AC 13.9** [Admin] **Given** I am an admin and there are open findings, **then** every page shows a warning banner (`role="alert"`) listing each vendor and model with a link to `/admin/ai-providers`. It has no close button and goes away only once a later audit run resolves the findings. With zero findings nothing is shown. Non-admins and signed-out visitors never see it and the page makes no findings request for them. (backend tests: LlmModelAuditRunnerTests, HttpLlmModelCatalogClientTests, LlmModelAuditHttpTests, LlmModelAuditPostgresTests; frontend tests: LlmFindingsBanner.test.tsx)

## Unclarities / questions for product owner

- **Shareable link vs IsPublic**: Resolved — if a collection is **not** shared with others (IsPublic = false), only the owner can access it by ID or link. When **Shared with others** is on (IsPublic = true), anyone with the link (or who finds it by ID or via Discover) can view and quiz, and the collection appears in Discover. The UI uses the label **"Shared with others"** for this toggle. Users can search or enter a collection ID to open it (see AC 1.9.6).
- **Default collection**: Resolved — at signup the backend creates a personalized default collection when the user's display name is available: name = first 3 characters + `'s Collection` and description = `<display name> default collection` (for example `Abc's Collection` / `Abcdefgh default collection`). If a user has zero collections (e.g. legacy), the first `GET /collections` creates the same shape. If no display name is available, it falls back to "Default Collection".
- **Private keyword name collision**: When a public keyword and a private keyword share the same name/slug, navigation and item filtering resolve that name/slug to **one effective keyword** per scope: the **public** keyword is used when it exists; if no public keyword exists with that name/slug, the user's private keyword is used instead. This ensures a single, predictable path/keyword resolution when names collide.
- **Cloze sibling leak and grammar (#261)**: Cards created from one cloze line are saved as independent items with no link between them, so quiz and flashcard sessions can show a sibling's answer while another card from the same line is asked; the Paste text import (AC 2.6.11) warns about this in its preview (there is no per-card reject; remove the line instead). Distractors are written by the user, so in inflected languages they must already be in the grammatical form that fits the sentence; the app does not inflect them. With a multi-blank card (same cN used more than once) the correct answer is the answers joined with ", " (for example "H, O"), so typed-answer mode (#256) requires that joined text exactly.

## Updating this document

When you change application behavior (API contracts, authorization rules, or user-visible flows), update this document so it stays accurate.

- **API vs UI:** Keep **API** (HTTP, status codes, payloads, authorization) and **UI** (labels, screens, controls) in separate subsections under each feature so backend and frontend concerns stay separate.
- **Actors:** Use the standard actors: **Owner**, **Authenticated (non-owner)**, **Admin (non-owner)**, **Anonymous**. State the actor in each AC as `[Actor]`. For Admin (non-owner), use "Same as Authenticated (non-owner) (AC X.Y.Z)" when behavior is the same.
- **Format:** Use **AC section.subsection.id** with Given/When/Then; reference endpoints and status codes in API criteria, and copy/controls in UI criteria.
- **Atomicity:** Aim for **one AC = one verifiable behavior**. Avoid mixing concerns (e.g. do not combine route behavior, API response shape, UI labels, navigation, and error handling in a single AC); instead, split into separate ACs that each test one thing.
- See the Cursor rule "Update acceptance criteria when app behavior changes" for automation hints.

### 3.27 Complete uploaded-picture workflow (#398)

- **AC 3.27.1** [Author] Create/Edit share adjacent optional Question/Explanation picture controls. Choose JPEG/PNG/still WebP up to2MiB, acknowledge rights, supply descriptive alt<=300 and optional attribution<=300/longDescription<=2000. Only the server-normalized authenticated blob is previewed. Cancel/abort retains existing slot/text; failed selection requires Retry or Cancel before any Save. Save retries reuse upload IDs and durable create request identity; Save+another resets both slots/metadata and focuses Question. Curated slots remain read-only. Review state is distinct from privacy and library publication; relevant semantic/picture/description changes warn that existing shared/public eligibility is withdrawn pending review. Null long descriptions preserve existing review hash bytes.
- **AC 3.27.2** [Reader] Uploaded pictures resolve through exact item+slot or supplied collection+membership context; a denied collection route never falls back to direct item or asset preview. Auth identity, item/revision, slot and collection scope isolate/revoke object URLs. Question/Explanation use the shared responsive contain/zoom renderer; explanation and its descriptions never mount before reveal. Print fetches normalized pictures and blocks on unresolved required media. Question attribution is not an answer hint. Alt/long descriptions are escaped text; visual identification also offers not-scored Skip.
- **AC 3.27.3** [Learner] The active required question picture pauses the single persisted Test deadline from initial unresolved load, without waiting for the stuck timeout. Resume accumulates the exact pause once; refresh retains pause state. Initial/loading/unknown/failed unresolved required pictures at early/manual/timeout submit are excluded when unanswered. No scored questions means no percentage, completion/mastery/session/Daily callback. Retry/Skip recovery never scores a failed picture as wrong. Loaded question pictures also offer keyboard-accessible not-scored Skip. The explicit reason is persisted in practice/Learn/Test, including the last Test item; skipped items cannot later be answered. Loaded Skip grants no additional timer pause. Existing flashcard skip semantics remain.
- **AC 3.27.4** [Author] JSON export emits explicit safe owned uploaded asset references plus alt/attribution/descriptions, never storage keys/bytes/approval. Foreign picture loss is blocked until explicit acknowledgement, then listed in each row's `omittedPictures` manifest. The real file upload path preserves owned references; all media is validated before any Upload/item/collection side effect and uses the same serialized asset/readiness/review/duplicate rules as creation. Imported uploaded items are private PendingReview even for admins; exported itemId cannot overwrite anything. Existing per-row non-media bulk error/duplicate behavior is retained. Seed loading rejects uploaded/URL/base64/storage fields and sync refuses existing uploaded attachment state; IDs stay stable.
- **AC 3.27.5** [System] Before multipart buffering, separate durable ingress attempts reserve a whole bounded request envelope and owner daily bytes under the shared PostgreSQL lock, with global active-read leases, 20-second read timeout and whole-envelope bound. Malformed, duplicate-part, oversized and partial/aborted admitted requests spend transport allowance; completed replays still spend transport but no new decode allowance. Durable decode admission claims bound hourly/daily attempts, pending review, normalized retained storage and global active reservations before decode; the original never persists. Request keys are owner+digest bound and retries recover the same normalized asset. Expired lease recovery is bounded. Asset attachment, approval, import, cleanup and withdrawal serialize across replicas. Withdrawal atomically revokes the asset and advances EVERY referring item's eligibility revision; no approval/attachment race can restore it.
- **AC 3.27.6** [Reader/Admin] Visible item/collection/slot/current-revision reporting uses minimal enum metadata, anonymous share access, daily HMAC abuse keys, dedup and bounded allowance; reports do not automatically remove content. Admin reports/normalized previews/exact-revision withdrawal are available independently of upload/AI switches. Delivery revocation targets<=300s for previously successful URLs; already downloaded copies cannot be recalled.
- **AC 3.27.7** [Owner] Cleanup ships disabled; production execution requires explicitly test-owner scope or separate general-cleanup approval. Current PendingReview/Approved/other live references and applicable current undecided submissions protect bytes across owners. Deleted/historical audit rows do not retain objects; Withdrawn item references are inactive. Only due current rejected submission decisions determine rejected retention; withdrawing one slot never restarts its sibling clock. Cleanup applies due current-rejection proof and initial withdrawal deadlines before its bounded candidate batch, so protected older rows cannot starve later eligible assets. Reference-aware expiry commits durable tombstones before idempotent deletion, retains retry/backoff and persisted prefix-list cursors, and never revives keys on restore. Normalized objects only; raw-source cleanup is N/A. Defaults/retention/restore/IAM/cache verification and actual-route caveats are in [MEDIA_OPERATIONS.md](infra/MEDIA_OPERATIONS.md). Configuration/code is not evidence of deployed permissions, cleanup, caches or alerts.
- **AC 3.27.8** [Admin] Optional Gemini vision triage has a real bounded normalized-WebP adapter, exact semantic context, strict result schema and durable revision/content+asset/model/policy/provider jobs/budgets. It is off by default and requires explicit processing/privacy acceptance plus an independent switch; refresh never calls provider. Timeout/unknown/malformed/stale/budget/outage leaves manual review pending; no path autoapproves. The lawful synthetic live evaluator reports class counts/disagreements/coverage gaps, never calls mocked accuracy real, and cannot establish visual harmful sensitivity from text proxies. Missing lawful positive fixtures or actual successful outputs remain NOT RUN and prevent claiming #404 complete.

- **AC 3.27.9** [Reader/Admin] Both item and collection picture-content GET routes accept optional positive `expectedRevision`: current equality and current authorization/state/slot are required; stale revision is404 and invalid/empty/zero is400. Omitted query preserves legacy current-slot GET without snapshot guarantees. Every first-party uploaded study, ordinary/admin/report preview, recap and print request requires the positive DTO revision and fails closed without it; denial never retries an unbound route. Relevant incorrect-answer rationales participate in the deterministic semantic review hash/context and edit warning; null/empty rationale preserves historical hashes.
